  // The bottom contextual toolbar should always reveal its first tools when a character
  // animation row/clip is selected, rather than inheriting a stale right-end scroll offset.
  const previousSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof previousSelect==='function'&&!previousSelect.__evv206ToolbarStart){
    const wrappedSelect=function(which){
      const result=previousSelect.apply(this,arguments);
      const key=String(which==null?'':which);
      if(key==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(key)||/^char\d+$/.test(key)){
        const reset=()=>{
          ['bottomToolsClip','bottomToolsDefault','bottomToolsKeyframe'].forEach(id=>{const row=$(id);if(row)row.scrollLeft=0;});
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(reset));
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSelect.__evv206ToolbarStart=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrappedSelect;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v216-text-style-and-speech-shape-engine">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const PATHS={
    oval:'M50 3 C76 3 97 23 97 50 C97 77 76 97 50 97 C24 97 3 77 3 50 C3 23 24 3 50 3 Z',
    pill:'M22 5 H78 C91 5 98 22 98 50 C98 78 91 95 78 95 H22 C9 95 2 78 2 50 C2 22 9 5 22 5 Z',
    'speech-bottom':'M17 5 Q7 5 7 16 V69 Q7 80 18 80 H38 L29 96 Q28 99 32 97 L56 80 H82 Q93 80 93 69 V16 Q93 5 82 5 Z',
    'speech-bottom-left':'M18 5 Q7 5 7 16 V69 Q7 80 18 80 H25 L12 97 Q10 100 15 98 L43 80 H82 Q93 80 93 69 V16 Q93 5 82 5 Z',
    'speech-bottom-right':'M18 5 Q7 5 7 16 V69 Q7 80 18 80 H57 L85 98 Q90 100 87 95 L75 80 H82 Q93 80 93 69 V16 Q93 5 82 5 Z',
    'speech-top':'M17 20 Q7 20 7 31 V83 Q7 94 18 94 H82 Q93 94 93 83 V31 Q93 20 82 20 H56 L32 3 Q28 1 29 6 L38 20 Z',
    'speech-left':'M20 7 H83 Q94 7 94 18 V82 Q94 93 83 93 H20 Q9 93 9 82 V62 L2 39 Q1 35 6 37 L20 43 Z',
    'speech-right':'M17 7 H80 Q91 7 91 18 V43 L98 63 Q99 67 94 65 L80 59 V82 Q80 93 69 93 H17 Q6 93 6 82 V18 Q6 7 17 7 Z',
    cloud:'M24 77 C12 77 5 68 5 57 C5 47 12 40 22 38 C20 22 33 10 48 12 C57 1 78 8 80 24 C94 24 99 39 92 50 C101 65 88 80 74 78 C62 91 44 89 37 80 Z',
    'cloud-callout':'M24 70 C10 70 5 60 8 49 C2 38 11 27 23 27 C25 11 43 7 53 18 C65 5 85 13 83 29 C99 32 100 52 88 60 C88 72 75 79 62 74 L43 74 L32 96 Q29 100 28 95 L30 74 Z',
    thought:'M20 70 C7 66 5 51 15 43 C12 29 25 21 36 25 C43 10 63 11 69 26 C83 18 96 31 90 44 C102 56 90 72 77 70 C64 85 45 82 39 73 Z M20 83 C16 83 13 86 13 90 C13 94 16 97 20 97 C24 97 27 94 27 90 C27 86 24 83 20 83 Z M35 91 C32 91 30 94 30 97 C30 99 32 100 35 100 C38 100 40 98 40 95 C40 93 38 91 35 91 Z',
    burst:'M50 2 L58 16 L72 7 L72 23 L92 20 L82 39 L99 50 L82 61 L92 80 L72 77 L72 94 L58 84 L50 99 L42 84 L28 94 L28 77 L8 80 L18 61 L1 50 L18 39 L8 20 L28 23 L28 7 L42 16 Z',
    tag:'M4 50 L27 7 Q29 4 34 4 H84 Q96 4 96 16 V84 Q96 96 84 96 H34 Q29 96 27 92 Z'
  };
  const SHAPES=[
    ['rounded','Rounded'],['square','Square'],['pill','Capsule'],['oval','Oval'],
    ['speech-bottom','Speech ↓'],['speech-bottom-left','Speech ↙'],['speech-bottom-right','Speech ↘'],['speech-top','Speech ↑'],
    ['speech-left','Speech ←'],['speech-right','Speech →'],['cloud','Cloud'],['cloud-callout','Cloud tail'],
    ['thought','Thought'],['burst','Comic burst'],['tag','Point label']
  ];
  const escapeAttr=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/</g,'&lt;');
  function pathFor(shape){return PATHS[shape]||'';}
  // Largest rectangle (fractions of the 100x100 shape box, 2.5-unit safety margin) that lies fully
  // inside each shape. The padded text box is fitted into it, so the shape always wraps all text.
  const SAFE={
    oval:[0.18,0.185,0.82,0.815],pill:[0.13,0.115,0.87,0.885],
    'speech-bottom':[0.125,0.085,0.875,0.765],'speech-bottom-left':[0.125,0.085,0.875,0.765],'speech-bottom-right':[0.125,0.085,0.875,0.765],
    'speech-top':[0.125,0.235,0.875,0.905],'speech-left':[0.225,0.105,0.885,0.895],'speech-right':[0.095,0.125,0.765,0.875],
    cloud:[0.255,0.27,0.85,0.735],'cloud-callout':[0.28,0.2,0.79,0.7],thought:[0.215,0.29,0.85,0.665],
    burst:[0.205,0.255,0.795,0.745],tag:[0.29,0.09,0.915,0.91]
  };
  function fitRect(key,x,y,w,h){
    const S=SAFE[key];if(!S)return {x,y,w,h};
    const W=w/(S[2]-S[0]),H=h/(S[3]-S[1]);
    return {x:x-S[0]*W,y:y-S[1]*H,w:W,h:H};
  }
  function svgMarkup(shape){
    const d=pathFor(shape);
    if(shape==='rounded')return '<rect x="4" y="4" width="92" height="92" rx="17"/>';
    if(shape==='square')return '<rect x="5" y="5" width="90" height="90" rx="3"/>';
    if(!d)return '<rect x="4" y="4" width="92" height="92" rx="12"/>';
    return '<path d="'+d+'"/>';
  }
  function svgDoc(shape){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none"><g fill="#fff">'+svgMarkup(shape)+'</g></svg>';}
  window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss=function(shape){
    const key=String(shape||'rounded');
    if(key==='rounded'||key==='square'||!PATHS[key])return 'none';
    const uri='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svgDoc(key));
    return 'url("'+uri+'")';
  };
  window.ethvelleTextBackgroundShapePicker=function(model){
    const t=model||{};const chosen=String(t.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
    return '<div class="evpa-shape-group-label" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:750;color:#DCE3F2;margin:6px 0 2px">Background shape</div><div class="evpa-bg-shapes" role="group" aria-label="Text background shapes">'+SHAPES.map(([key,label])=>'<button type="button" class="evpa-bg-shape-btn '+(chosen===key?'active':'')+'" data-bg-shape="'+key+'" title="'+escapeAttr(label)+'" aria-pressed="'+(chosen===key?'true':'false')+'" onclick="ethvellePicsartSetValue(\'textBackgroundShape\',\''+key+'\')"><svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true"><g fill="currentColor">'+svgMarkup(key)+'</g></svg><span>'+label+'</span></button>').join('')+'</div>';
  };
  window.ethvelleDrawTextBackplate=function(ctx,x,y,w,h,radius,shape){
    if(!ctx||!(w>0)||!(h>0))return false;
    const key=String(shape||'rounded');
    if(key==='square'){ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);return true;}
    if(SAFE[key]){const q=fitRect(key,x,y,w,h);x=q.x;y=q.y;w=q.w;h=q.h;}
    if(key==='rounded'){
      const r=Math.max(0,Math.min(Number(radius)||8,w/2,h/2));
      if(typeof ctx.roundRect==='function'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);ctx.fill();}
      else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.lineTo(x+w-r,y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+r);ctx.lineTo(x+w,y+h-r);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y+h,x+w-r,y+h);ctx.lineTo(x+r,y+h);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x,y+h,x,y+h-r);ctx.lineTo(x,y+r);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x,y,x+r,y);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
      return true;
    }
    const d=pathFor(key);if(!d)return false;
    try{
      if(typeof Path2D==='undefined')return false;
      const path=new Path2D(d);ctx.save();try{ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.scale(w/100,h/100);ctx.fill(path);}finally{ctx.restore();}return true;
    }catch(err){return false;}
  };
  // Public synchronizer used by the live text renderer and older saved text nodes.
  window.ethvelleSyncTextBackplateShape=function(node,t){
    if(!node||!t)return;
    const key=SHAPES.some(x=>x[0]===String(t.textBackgroundShape))?String(t.textBackgroundShape):'rounded';
    node.dataset.evTextBackgroundShape=key;
    node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-mask',window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss(key));
    const S=SAFE[key];
    if(S){
      const pad=Math.max(0,Number(t.textBackgroundPadding==null?(t.textHighlightPadding==null?8:t.textHighlightPadding):t.textBackgroundPadding))||0;
      const kx=1/(S[2]-S[0]),ky=1/(S[3]-S[1]),f=v=>(+v).toFixed(4);
      const edge=(frac,k)=>'calc(-'+pad+'px - '+f(frac*k)+' * (100% + '+(2*pad)+'px))';
      node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-inset',edge(S[1],ky)+' '+edge(1-S[2],kx)+' '+edge(1-S[3],ky)+' '+edge(S[0],kx));
    }
  };
  window.ethvelleSyncComposerBackplate=function(model){
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText'),plate=document.getElementById('evtcStageBackplate'),t=model||{};
    if(!input||!plate)return;
    if(!t.textBackgroundEnabled){plate.style.display='none';return;}
    const r0=input.getBoundingClientRect(),shp0=String(t.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
    const r=SAFE[shp0]?(q=>({left:q.x,top:q.y,width:q.w,height:q.h}))(fitRect(shp0,r0.left,r0.top,r0.width,r0.height)):r0;
    plate.style.display='block';plate.style.left=r.left+'px';plate.style.top=r.top+'px';plate.style.width=r.width+'px';plate.style.height=r.height+'px';
    const h=String(t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66').replace('#','');
    const rgb=/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h)?[0,2,4].map(i=>parseInt(h.slice(i,i+2),16)):[24,42,102];
    plate.style.backgroundColor='rgba('+rgb.join(',')+',1)';plate.style.borderRadius=(Number(t.textBackgroundRadius)||8)+'px';
    const shape=String(t.textBackgroundShape||'rounded'),mask=window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss(shape);
    plate.style.webkitMaskImage=mask;plate.style.maskImage=mask;plate.style.webkitMaskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.maskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.webkitMaskRepeat='no-repeat';plate.style.maskRepeat='no-repeat';
    plate.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:t.textBackgroundOpacity)))/100);
  };
  // Keep the selection visuals in sync after each shape tap.
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const btn=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.evpa-bg-shape-btn'):null;if(!btn)return;
    const container=btn.parentElement;if(container)container.querySelectorAll('.evpa-bg-shape-btn').forEach(b=>{const on=b===btn;b.classList.toggle('active',on);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');});
  },true);
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v219-color-row-order-css">
  /* Tool buttons always lead every colour row in this fixed order: eyedropper, custom wheel, transparent. */
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .ev-eyedrop-text{order:-3!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-color-wheel{order:-2!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-transparent{order:-1!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v219-color-row-order-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Several older layers rebuild the colour row (open, tab change, Transparent tap) and each rebuild
     dropped the eyedropper button and re-created it a frame later, so the row visibly jumped
     (eyedropper / wheel / transparent changed places). This guard runs as a MutationObserver
     microtask, i.e. BEFORE the browser paints, and puts the eyedropper straight back in front of
     the wheel, with transparent right after it. It is idempotent, so it never loops. */
  let proto=null, observed=null, observer=null, busy=false;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  function inComposer(d){return d.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')||!!($('ethvelleTextComposer')&&$('ethvelleTextComposer').classList.contains('active'));}
  function fix(){
    if(busy)return;
    const d=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock'),pal=$('evpaPalette');
    if(!d||!pal||inComposer(d))return;
    const live=pal.querySelector('.ev-eyedrop-text');
    if(live){proto=live.cloneNode(true);proto.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');}
    busy=true;
    try{
      pal.querySelectorAll('.evpa-color-wheel').forEach(wheel=>{
        const parent=wheel.parentElement;if(!parent)return;
        let eye=parent.querySelector(':scope > .ev-eyedrop-text');
        if(!eye&&proto)eye=proto.cloneNode(true);
        if(eye&&eye.nextElementSibling!==wheel)parent.insertBefore(eye,wheel);
        const clear=parent.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-transparent');
        if(clear&&wheel.nextElementSibling!==clear)wheel.after(clear);
      });
    }catch(_e){}
    busy=false;
  }
  function attach(){
    const d=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!d||typeof MutationObserver!=='function')return false;
    if(observed===d)return true;
    if(observer)observer.disconnect();
    observer=new MutationObserver(fix);
    observer.observe(d,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    observed=d;fix();return true;
  }
  function boot(){
    if(attach())return;
    const wait=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(attach())wait.disconnect();});
    wait.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  ['openEthvelleTextStylePanel','openEthvelleTextComposer'].forEach(name=>{
    const old=window[name];
    if(typeof old!=='function'||old.__ev219Order)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const r=old.apply(this,arguments);attach();return r;};
    wrapped.__ev219Order=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeMediaAccessTool,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #evcaFrameCaptureTool-keyframe,

  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDeleteTool{
    flex:0 0 auto!important;flex-basis:auto!important;width:auto!important;min-width:68px!important;max-width:none!important;
    display:flex!important;flex-direction:column!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:flex-start!important;
    gap:5px!important;padding:10px 7px 8px!important;margin:0!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;text-align:center!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeMediaAccessTool svg,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #evcaFrameCaptureTool-keyframe svg,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDoneTool svg,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDeleteTool svg{width:21px!important;height:21px!important;flex:0 0 21px!important;margin:0!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeMediaAccessTool span,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #evcaFrameCaptureTool-keyframe span,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDoneTool span,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDeleteTool span{font-size:10px!important;font-weight:600!important;line-height:1.15!important;white-space:nowrap!important;margin:0!important;}
</style>


<script id="ethvelle-v222-audio-speed-tool">
/* v222: Speed button on the bottom toolbar for a selected audio clip.
   The Speed panel, timeline length, live preview (playbackRate) and export already support audio clips through
   speedTargetClip(); only the audio-exclusive toolbar was hiding the button. Runs last so earlier audio-toolbar
   patches (v66/v67/v68) cannot hide it again. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  function selAudio(){try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='audio')||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function apply(){
    const tool=document.getElementById('clipSpeedTool'),row=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');
    if(!tool||!row)return;
    const clip=selAudio();
    if(!clip)return;
    tool.classList.add('evca-audio-only-tool');
    if(tool.style.display!=='flex')tool.style.display='flex';
    tool.dataset.evcaV66Hidden&&delete tool.dataset.evcaV66Hidden;
    const sp=Number(clip.speed)||1,lab=document.getElementById('clipSpeedLabel');
    if(lab){const t=Math.abs(sp-1)<0.01?'Speed':(sp.toFixed(2).replace(/0+$/,'').replace(/\.$/,'')+'x');if(lab.textContent!==t)lab.textContent=t;}
    /* keep it right after Split so it sits with the other audio tools */
    const split=document.getElementById('clipSplitTool');
    if(split&&split.nextElementSibling!==tool&&split.parentNode===tool.parentNode)split.parentNode.insertBefore(tool,split.nextElementSibling);
  }
  function install(){
    const prev=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV222AudioSpeed)return;
    const w=function(){const r=prev.apply(this,arguments);apply();return r;};
    w.__evV222AudioSpeed=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=w;
    try{syncBottomToolsMode=w;}catch(_e){}
    const tool=document.getElementById('clipSpeedTool');
    if(tool&&!tool.__evV222Obs){tool.__evV222Obs=true;new MutationObserver(()=>{if(selAudio()&&tool.style.display!=='flex')apply();}).observe(tool,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style']});}
  }
  install();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',install,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{install();apply();});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v223-outer-stroke">
/* v227: text stroke is drawn as its OWN layer behind the text, so it only shows OUTSIDE the letters.
   v227 fixes: (1) the stroke layer is now updated IN PLACE instead of being deleted and re-created, and the
   change check no longer uses glyph.style.cssText (other scripts re-write the stroke every frame, which reorders
   cssText, so the layer was rebuilt ~100x per second while playing = the blinking), (2) the layer takes its width and
   height from the exact fractional layout size, so it can no longer wrap onto different lines than the text. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const NEUTRAL='0.001px transparent';
  const CLS='evpa-stroke-clone';
  const S=(el,k,v)=>{if(el.style.getPropertyValue(k)!==String(v)||el.style.getPropertyPriority(k)!=='important')el.style.setProperty(k,v,'important');};
  function parseStroke(v){
    v=String(v||'').trim();if(!v)return null;
    const m=v.match(/^(-?[\d.]+)(?:px)?\s*(.*)$/);if(!m)return null;
    const w=parseFloat(m[1])||0;if(Math.abs(w-0.001)<1e-6)return 'mine';
    let col=m[2]||'transparent',op=1;
    const r=col.match(/^rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*(?:,\s*([\d.]+)\s*)?\)$/i);
    if(r){op=r[4]==null?1:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,parseFloat(r[4])));col='rgb('+r[1]+','+r[2]+','+r[3]+')';}
    else if(/^transparent$/i.test(col)){op=0;}
    return {w:w,color:col,op:op};
  }
  /* v233: 3D stroke. The outline gets a solid extrusion (depth) toward the bottom-right, built from a few hard
     drop-shadow steps that get darker the farther they go, so the letters look like thick 3D cut-outs instead of a flat
     2D outline. The very same step geometry/colours are used by the canvas export (ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth) so the exported
     video matches the preview. */
  function rgbOf(col){
    col=String(col||'').trim();
    let m=col.match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*(?:,\s*([\d.]+)\s*)?\)/i);
    if(m)return {r:+m[1],g:+m[2],b:+m[3],a:m[4]==null?1:parseFloat(m[4])};
    m=col.match(/^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i);if(m){const h=m[1];return {r:parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16),g:parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16),b:parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16),a:1};}
    m=col.match(/^#([0-9a-f]{3})$/i);if(m){const h=m[1];return {r:parseInt(h[0]+h[0],16),g:parseInt(h[1]+h[1],16),b:parseInt(h[2]+h[2],16),a:1};}
    return null;
  }
  window.ethvelleStrokeExtrude=function(w,unit,col){
    unit=Number(unit)||1;w=Math.max(0,Number(w)||0);
    const depth=Math.max(2*unit,Math.min(14*unit,w*0.7+1.5*unit));
    const n=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,Math.ceil(depth/(1.5*unit))));
    const c=rgbOf(col)||{r:0,g:0,b:0,a:1};
    const steps=[];
    for(let k=1;k<=n;k++){
      const f=0.38+0.34*(k/n);
      steps.push({k:k,x:depth*0.32*k/n,y:depth*0.9*k/n,r:Math.round(c.r*(1-f)),g:Math.round(c.g*(1-f)),b:Math.round(c.b*(1-f)),a:c.a});
    }
    return {depth:depth,n:n,steps:steps};
  };
  window.ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth=function(ctx,line,x,y,maxWidth,w,unit){
    try{
      if(!ctx||!(w>0))return;
      const ex=window.ethvelleStrokeExtrude(w,unit,ctx.strokeStyle);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor='transparent';ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.shadowOffsetX=0;ctx.shadowOffsetY=0;
      ctx.lineWidth=w*2;ctx.lineJoin='round';
      for(let i=ex.steps.length-1;i>=0;i--){
        const st=ex.steps[i];
        ctx.strokeStyle='rgba('+st.r+','+st.g+','+st.b+','+st.a+')';
        ctx.strokeText(line,x+st.x,y+st.y,maxWidth);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }catch(_e){try{ctx.restore();}catch(__){}}
  };
  function depthFilter(st){
    const ex=window.ethvelleStrokeExtrude(st.w,1,st.color);
    const sx=ex.steps.length?ex.steps[0].x:0,sy=ex.steps.length?ex.steps[0].y:0;
    return ex.steps.map(p=>'drop-shadow('+sx.toFixed(3)+'px '+sy.toFixed(3)+'px 0 rgb('+p.r+','+p.g+','+p.b+'))').join(' ');
  }
  function applyStrokeProps(c,st){
    S(c,'-webkit-text-stroke',(st.w*2)+'px '+st.color);
    /* v306: bent text is one span per letter. A span with its own '0 transparent' stroke hides the outline, so every
       bent letter of the stroke copy carries the same outline (this is why the stroke vanished when Bend was used). */
    if(c.firstElementChild){
      c.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(function(ch){S(ch,'-webkit-text-stroke',(st.w*2)+'px '+st.color);S(ch,'paint-order','normal');});
    }
    S(c,'opacity',String(st.op));
    S(c,'filter',depthFilter(st));
  }
  /* v233: the stroke layer mirrors the glyph's layout props IN PLACE (no clone rebuild) and in the same task as the write
     (microtask), so dragging Letter/Line spacing moves the stroke and the letters together instead of vibrating. */
  const LAYOUT=['fontFamily','fontSize','fontWeight','fontStyle','letterSpacing','lineHeight','textAlign','whiteSpace','textTransform'];
  const kebab=n=>n.replace(/[A-Z]/g,m=>'-'+m.toLowerCase());
  function layout(c,glyph,cs){
    LAYOUT.forEach(n=>S(c,kebab(n),cs[n]));
    /* v294: copy the SPECIFIED line-height (e.g. '1.26'), not the computed px string - a rounded px value could land on a different pixel row than the glyph. */
    if(glyph.style.lineHeight)S(c,'line-height',glyph.style.lineHeight);
    if(glyph.style.letterSpacing)S(c,'letter-spacing',glyph.style.letterSpacing);
    S(c,'text-decoration',glyph.style.textDecoration||'none');
    S(c,'transform',glyph.style.transform||'none');
    S(c,'transform-origin',glyph.style.transformOrigin||'center center');
    /* v296: the stroke copy is an absolutely-positioned paint twin. Its measured layout-box
       origin/size and text metrics match the real glyph, but it is removed from line layout entirely:
       it can never narrow the shrink-to-fit text container, create a second wrap, or move the fill.
       offsetLeft/offsetTop are layout coordinates (not transformed screen coordinates), so the copied
       transform/transform-origin is then applied exactly once to both twins. */
    let left=0,top=0;
    try{
      if(glyph.offsetParent===glyph.parentElement){left=Number(glyph.offsetLeft)||0;top=Number(glyph.offsetTop)||0;}
      else if(glyph.offsetParent){
        const gr=glyph.getBoundingClientRect(),pr=glyph.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
        const ox=(parseFloat(cs.transformOrigin)||0),oy=(String(cs.transformOrigin||'').split(/\s+/)[1]&&parseFloat(String(cs.transformOrigin).split(/\s+/)[1]))||0;
        const tr=cs.transform&&cs.transform!=='none'?new DOMMatrixReadOnly(cs.transform):null;
        if(tr){
          const q=tr.transformPoint(new DOMPoint(-ox,-oy,0,1)),w=q.w||1;
          left=(gr.left-pr.left)-ox-q.x/w;top=(gr.top-pr.top)-oy-q.y/w;
        }else{left=gr.left-pr.left;top=gr.top-pr.top;}
      }else{left=Number(glyph.offsetLeft)||0;top=Number(glyph.offsetTop)||0;}
    }catch(_e){left=Number(glyph.offsetLeft)||0;top=Number(glyph.offsetTop)||0;}
    S(c,'position','absolute');S(c,'display','inline-block');S(c,'float','none');S(c,'vertical-align',cs.verticalAlign);
    S(c,'left',left+'px');S(c,'top',top+'px');S(c,'right','auto');S(c,'bottom','auto');
    S(c,'margin','0');S(c,'box-sizing',cs.boxSizing);S(c,'padding',cs.padding);S(c,'border',cs.border);
    S(c,'width',cs.width);S(c,'height',cs.height);S(c,'max-width','none');
    S(c,'white-space',cs.whiteSpace);S(c,'overflow-wrap',cs.overflowWrap);S(c,'word-break',cs.wordBreak);
    S(c,'text-indent',cs.textIndent);S(c,'direction',cs.direction);
  }
  /* v232: stop the stroke "blink" while the Amount slider is dragged. Other scripts write the REAL stroke onto the glyph
     on every slider tick; this layer then had to swap it for NEUTRAL from a MutationObserver, and the observer wrapper
     (v189) delivers late when the frame budget is used up, so one frame was painted with the real stroke on top of the
     fill = flash. Now the write itself is intercepted on the glyph's style object: the real value is only remembered
     (node.__evStroke) and NEUTRAL is what ever reaches the glyph, so the real stroke can never be painted there. */
  const WATCH_KEBAB=new Set(['letter-spacing','line-height','transform','transform-origin','font-size','font-family','font-weight','font-style','text-align','white-space','text-decoration','width','height']);
  const WATCH_CAMEL=['letterSpacing','lineHeight','transform','transformOrigin','fontSize','fontFamily','fontWeight','fontStyle','textAlign','whiteSpace','textDecoration','width','height'];
  function guard(node,glyph){
    if(glyph.__evStrokeGuard)return;
    glyph.__evStrokeGuard=true;
    const st=glyph.style,origSet=st.setProperty.bind(st);
    const owner=()=>{const p=glyph.parentNode;return (p&&p.classList&&p.classList.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content'))?p:node;};
    function take(v){
      const str=String(v==null?'':v).trim();if(!str)return false;
      const cur=parseStroke(str);if(!cur)return false;
      const n=cur!=='mine'?owner():null;
      if(n)n.__evStroke=cur;
      origSet('-webkit-text-stroke',NEUTRAL,'important');
      if(n)schedule(n);                 /* same-task sync after the real glyph is neutral */
      return true;
    }
    st.setProperty=function(k,v,p){
      const key=String(k).toLowerCase();
      if(key==='-webkit-text-stroke'&&take(v))return;
      const r=origSet(k,v,p);
      if(WATCH_KEBAB.has(key))schedule(owner());
      return r;
    };
    const origGet=st.getPropertyValue.bind(st);
    /* Chromium exposes CSSStyleDeclaration's named CSS properties as dynamic properties, not
       prototype accessors. Defining an own accessor is therefore required to catch assignments
       such as glyph.style.letterSpacing = ... in the same task. */
    ['webkitTextStroke','WebkitTextStroke'].forEach(function(name){
      try{Object.defineProperty(st,name,{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get:function(){return origGet('-webkit-text-stroke');},set:function(v){if(!take(v))origSet('-webkit-text-stroke',v);}});}catch(_e){}
    });
    WATCH_CAMEL.forEach(function(name){
      const cssName=name.replace(/[A-Z]/g,function(ch){return '-'+ch.toLowerCase();});
      try{Object.defineProperty(st,name,{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get:function(){return origGet(cssName);},set:function(v){origSet(cssName,v);schedule(owner());}});}catch(_e){}
    });
  }
  function sync(node){
    if(!node||!node.querySelector)return;
    const glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
    if(!glyph)return;
    guard(node,glyph);watchSize(node,glyph);
    const raw=glyph.style.getPropertyValue('-webkit-text-stroke');
    const cur=parseStroke(raw);
    if(cur&&cur!=='mine')node.__evStroke=cur;           /* others just (re)wrote a stroke: take it as the truth */
    else if(!cur&&!node.__evStroke)node.__evStroke={w:0,color:'transparent',op:0};
    if(raw!==NEUTRAL||glyph.style.getPropertyPriority('-webkit-text-stroke')!=='important'){
      glyph.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-stroke',NEUTRAL,'important');
    }
    const st=node.__evStroke||{w:0,color:'transparent',op:0};
    let old=node.querySelector(':scope > .'+CLS);
    if(!(st.w>0)||st.op<=0){if(old)old.remove();node.__evSig='';return;}
    /* Force the glyph's new shrink-to-fit width/line layout to resolve before the absolute
       stroke twin copies it. Without this read, getComputedStyle can still report the previous
       parent-constrained width during the same task as a spacing/font write. */
    glyph.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cs=getComputedStyle(glyph);
    const sig=glyph.innerHTML;
    if(!old||node.__evSig!==sig){                         /* only the text/bend content needs a rebuild */
      node.__evSig=sig;
      const c=glyph.cloneNode(true);
      c.className=CLS;c.removeAttribute('id');c.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      c.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(e=>e.removeAttribute('id'));
      [c].concat([...c.querySelectorAll('*')]).forEach(e=>{
        S(e,'color','transparent');S(e,'-webkit-text-fill-color','transparent');S(e,'background','none');S(e,'background-image','none');
        S(e,'text-shadow','none');S(e,'box-shadow','none');S(e,'-webkit-text-stroke','0 transparent');S(e,'animation','none');S(e,'transition','none');
        if(e!==c){S(e,'-webkit-background-clip','border-box');S(e,'background-clip','border-box');}
      });
      S(c,'max-width','none');S(c,'margin','0');
      S(c,'pointer-events','none');S(c,'user-select','none');S(c,'mix-blend-mode','normal');
      S(c,'paint-order','normal');S(c,'stroke-linejoin','round');
      layout(c,glyph,cs);applyStrokeProps(c,st);
      if(old)old.replaceWith(c);else node.insertBefore(c,glyph);
      return;
    }
    layout(old,glyph,cs);applyStrokeProps(old,st);        /* same text: just follow size/spacing/colour/opacity */
  }
  /* v294: layout changes that do not touch any style inside the text (the overlay box being resized to fit the new spacing,
     stage zoom, font load) used to reach the stroke only from the 600ms timer, so the stroke lagged behind the letters = shaking.
     ResizeObserver callbacks run after layout and BEFORE paint, so the stroke now follows in the very same frame. */
  let sro=null;
  function watchSize(node,glyph){
    if(typeof ResizeObserver!=='function')return;
    if(!sro)sro=new ResizeObserver(es=>{es.forEach(e=>{const n=nodeOf(e.target)||(e.target.classList&&e.target.classList.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')?e.target:null);if(n)schedule(n);});});
    if(!node.__evRO){node.__evRO=true;sro.observe(node);}
    if(!glyph.__evRO){glyph.__evRO=true;sro.observe(glyph);}
  }
  function syncAll(root){(root||document).querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content').forEach(sync);}
  let queued=false;
  const pending=new Set();
  const syncing=new WeakSet();
  function flush(){queued=false;const list=[...pending];pending.clear();list.forEach(sync);}
  function schedule(n){
    if(!n)return;
    try{followKick();}catch(_e){}
    /* The setter that changed spacing/line-height/transform/font has already written its value.
       Sync the clone before returning from that setter, so Chromium cannot paint a stale frame. */
    if(!syncing.has(n)){
      syncing.add(n);
      try{sync(n);}catch(_e){}
      finally{syncing.delete(n);}
    }
    pending.add(n);
    if(!queued){queued=true;(window.queueMicrotask||Promise.resolve().then.bind(Promise.resolve()))(flush);}
  }
  function nodeOf(el){return el&&el.closest?el.closest('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content'):null;}
  /* v294 FRAME FOLLOWER. The editor's MutationObserver wrapper (v189) only spends ~6ms per frame on observers, so while a slider is being
     dragged the stroke layer was often synced a frame or more LATE, and the old stroke was painted next to the new letters = shaking.
     While the user is interacting (and for a moment after) this loop checks, once per frame and BEFORE paint, whether any text layer's
     glyph style changed (spacing, line height, angle/perspective, size...) and re-syncs the stroke in that very frame. It reads inline
     style strings only (no layout), so it is cheap, and it stops by itself ~0.8s after the last interaction. */
  const FOLLOW=['letter-spacing','line-height','transform','transform-origin','font-size','font-family','font-weight','font-style','text-align','white-space','text-decoration','width','height','padding','margin','opacity'];
  let followRaf=0,followUntil=0;
  function followTick(){
    followRaf=0;
    const root=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer')||document;
    root.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content').forEach(node=>{
      const glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');if(!glyph)return;
      const stl=glyph.style;let sig='';
      for(let i=0;i<FOLLOW.length;i++)sig+=stl.getPropertyValue(FOLLOW[i])+'|';
      sig+=glyph.textContent.length+'|'+(node.__evStroke?node.__evStroke.w+','+node.__evStroke.color+','+node.__evStroke.op:'');
      if(node.__evFollowSig!==sig){node.__evFollowSig=sig;try{sync(node);}catch(_e){}}
    });
    if(performance.now()<followUntil)followRaf=requestAnimationFrame(followTick);
  }
  function followKick(){followUntil=performance.now()+800;if(!followRaf)followRaf=requestAnimationFrame(followTick);}
  ['input','change','pointerdown','pointermove','pointerup','touchmove','keydown'].forEach(ev=>document.addEventListener(ev,followKick,{capture:true,passive:true}));
  function boot(){
    const host=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer')||document.body;
    new MutationObserver(ms=>{
      ms.forEach(m=>{
        const t=m.target;if(t&&t.nodeType===1&&t.closest&&t.closest('.'+CLS))return;
        if(m.type==='attributes'||m.type==='characterData'){const n=nodeOf(t.nodeType===1?t:t.parentElement);if(n)schedule(n);}
        else{
          const n=nodeOf(t.nodeType===1?t:t.parentElement);if(n)schedule(n);
          m.addedNodes.forEach(a=>{if(a.nodeType!==1)return;if(a.classList&&a.classList.contains(CLS))return;
            if(a.matches&&a.matches('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content'))schedule(a);
            else if(a.querySelectorAll)a.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content').forEach(schedule);});
        }
      });
    }).observe(host,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style','class'],characterData:true});
    syncAll();
    setInterval(()=>syncAll(),600);   /* font loading / resize change the glyph box without any DOM mutation */
    window.addEventListener('resize',()=>syncAll(),{passive:true});
    window.ethvelleSyncStrokeLayers=()=>syncAll();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v226-chroma-row-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* v226: the Key color row used to be built as [green, white, black ...] and re-arranged one frame later by the
     eyedropper patch ([eyedropper, wheel, green, white, black ...]), so every swatch jumped sideways (the black block
     "blinked"). Arrange it in the same task that builds it, before the browser paints. */
  function arrange(){try{if(typeof window.__ev226EnsureChromaPalette==='function')window.__ev226EnsureChromaPalette();}catch(_e){}}
  ['renderChromaKeyPanel','resetChromaSettings','applyEthvelleChromaCustomColor','saveEthvelleChromaCustomColor'].forEach(function(name){
    var orig=window[name];
    if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__ev226)return;
    var w=function(){var r=orig.apply(this,arguments);arrange();return r;};
    w.__ev226=true;window[name]=w;
  });
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v225-swatch-pick-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* v225: remember which colour (or Transparent) the user explicitly tapped for each effect of each text layer,
     recorded BEFORE the stacked colour patches run so the single marker owner (syncMarkers) agrees with them. */
  function note(key,val){try{const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;if(!t)return;(window.__ev224Picked=window.__ev224Picked||{})[String(t.id||'')+'|'+key]=val;}catch(_e){}}
  const oc=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof oc==='function'&&!oc.__ev225){const w=function(key,color){note(key,String(color||'').trim().toUpperCase());return oc.apply(this,arguments);};w.__ev225=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=w;}
  const on=window.ethvellePicsartSetNone;
  if(typeof on==='function'&&!on.__ev225){const w=function(key){note(key,'__none__');return on.apply(this,arguments);};w.__ev225=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetNone=w;}
})();
</script>
<style id="ethvelle-v227-text-quality-css">
/* v227 quality: do NOT pin text to a fixed-resolution GPU layer. will-change:transform / preserve-3d / hidden backface
   made the browser rasterize the text once at its first size and then stretch that bitmap when the text is enlarged
   (blurry text). Without them the text is redrawn sharp at its real on-screen size. */
#overlayMediaLayer .overlay-text-render,
#overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content,
#overlayMediaLayer .evpa-text-effect-layer,
#overlayMediaLayer .evpa-stroke-clone{
  will-change:auto!important;
  transform-style:flat!important;
  -webkit-backface-visibility:visible!important;backface-visibility:visible!important;
  text-rendering:geometricPrecision;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
}
/* v227 italic / tilt corner cut: gradient & texture text is painted with background-clip:text, which only paints
   INSIDE the text box. Italic and tilted letters overhang that box (top-right of f, arm of T ...) and were cut off.
   Extra padding with the same negative margin makes the box bigger without moving the text or changing its layout. */
#overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content > .evpa-text-effect-layer,
#overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content > .evpa-stroke-clone{
  padding:.16em .34em!important;
  margin:-.16em -.34em!important;
  box-sizing:content-box;
  background-origin:content-box;
}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v228-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* v228 fix 1  -  Transparent colour lock.
     "Transparent" sets the layer's opacity (textOpacity / strokeOpacity / shadowOpacity / textBackgroundOpacity) to 0 and
     leaves it there, so picking any other colour afterwards changed only the (invisible) colour. Picking a colour,
     using the colour wheel or the eyedropper now brings the opacity back (to the value it had before Transparent,
     or the normal default) so the chosen colour is visible straight away. Nothing else about the editors changes. */
  var PAIRS={textColor:'textOpacity',strokeColor:'strokeOpacity',shadowColor:'shadowOpacity',textBackgroundColor:'textBackgroundOpacity'};
  var ALPHA_KEYS={textOpacity:1,strokeOpacity:1,shadowOpacity:1,textBackgroundOpacity:1};
  var DEFAULTS={textOpacity:100,strokeOpacity:100,shadowOpacity:100,textBackgroundOpacity:78};
  var last={};
  function target(){try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function slot(t,a){return String(t&&t.id!=null?t.id:'')+'|'+a;}
  function remember(t,a){try{if(t&&Number(t[a])>0)last[slot(t,a)]=Number(t[a]);}catch(_e){}}
  function isZero(t,a){return !!t&&t[a]!=null&&Number(t[a])===0;}
  function restoreValue(t,a){var v=last[slot(t,a)];return v>0?v:DEFAULTS[a];}
  function syncUi(a,val){
    try{
      var dk=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
      if(dk){dk.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(function(r){var oi=String(r.getAttribute('oninput')||'');if(oi.indexOf("'"+a+"'")>-1){r.value=val;r.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',val+'%');}});}
      var o=document.getElementById('evpa-v-'+a);if(o)o.textContent=val+'%';
      var ps=document.getElementById('evts-'+a);if(ps){ps.value=val;var po=document.getElementById('evts-'+a+'-value');if(po)po.textContent=String(val);}
    }catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();}catch(_e){}
  }
  function revive(colorKey){
    var a=PAIRS[colorKey];if(!a)return;
    var t=target();if(!t||!isZero(t,a))return;
    var v=restoreValue(t,a);
    window.__evKeepDock=true;
    try{
      if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue(a,v);
      else{t[a]=v;if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v228 transparent restore:',err);}
    finally{window.__evKeepDock=false;}
    syncUi(a,v);
  }

  function wrapOnce(name,make){
    var fn=window[name];
    if(typeof fn!=='function'||fn.__ev228)return;
    var w=make(fn);w.__ev228=true;window[name]=w;
  }
  function installColorFix(){
    // Remember the visible opacity right before it becomes Transparent.
    wrapOnce('ethvellePicsartSetNone',function(orig){return function(key){try{var a=PAIRS[key];if(a)remember(target(),a);}catch(_e){}return orig.apply(this,arguments);};});
    // Swatch tap / eyedropper / saved colour: restore opacity after the colour is applied.
    wrapOnce('ethvellePicsartSetColor',function(orig){return function(key){var r=orig.apply(this,arguments);try{revive(key);}catch(_e){}return r;};});
    // Older text-style panel path (opened from the composer).
    wrapOnce('ethvelleTextStyleChanged',function(orig){return function(key,value){
      try{if(ALPHA_KEYS[key]&&Number(value)===0){var t0=target();if(t0)remember(t0,key);}}catch(_e){}
      var r=orig.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(PAIRS[key]&&typeof value==='string'&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(value))revive(key);}catch(_e){}
      return r;};});
    // Custom colour wheel: when it opens on a Transparent layer, restore opacity the moment a colour is actually chosen.
    wrapOnce('openEthvelleColorPicker',function(orig){return function(key){
      var out=orig.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        var a=PAIRS[key]||'textOpacity',t=target(),host=document.getElementById('ethvelleColorPicker');
        if(host&&t&&isZero(t,a)&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetPickerOpacity==='function'){
          if(host.__ev228Arm){host.removeEventListener('pointerdown',host.__ev228Arm,true);host.removeEventListener('input',host.__ev228Arm,true);}
          var arm=function(e){
            var el=e.target;if(!el||!el.closest)return;
            if(!el.closest('#evpaHueRing,#evpaSatSquare,#evpaPickHex'))return;
            host.removeEventListener('pointerdown',arm,true);host.removeEventListener('input',arm,true);host.__ev228Arm=null;
            var cur=target();if(!cur||!isZero(cur,a))return;
            try{window.ethvellePicsartSetPickerOpacity(restoreValue(cur,a));}catch(_e){}
          };
          host.__ev228Arm=arm;host.addEventListener('pointerdown',arm,true);host.addEventListener('input',arm,true);
        }
      }catch(_e){}
      return out;};});
  }

  function installAll(){try{installColorFix();}catch(e){console.warn(e);}}
  installAll();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',installAll,{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',installAll,{once:true});
  setTimeout(installAll,1500);setTimeout(installAll,5000);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v234-scrubber-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ev234Pill) return; window.__ev234Pill = true;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = '.ev-clip-dur-pill{position:absolute;top:2px;left:6px;z-index:6;pointer-events:none;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:4px;background:rgba(20,20,22,.72);color:#fff;font:600 9px/12px system-ui,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.2px;white-space:nowrap;will-change:left}'
    + 'body.ev-pill-hide .ev-clip-dur-pill{display:none}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  function fmt(sec){
    sec = Math.max(0, Number(sec)||0);
    if(sec < 60) return sec.toFixed(1)+'s';
    var m = Math.floor(sec/60), s = sec - m*60;
    return m+':'+(s<10?'0':'')+s.toFixed(1);
  }
  var raf = 0;
  function sync(){
    raf = 0;
    var scroller = document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
    if(!scroller || typeof overlays === 'undefined' || !Array.isArray(overlays)) return;
    var sRect = scroller.getBoundingClientRect();
    document.querySelectorAll('.ev-clip-dur-pill').forEach(function(p){
      var host = p.parentElement;
      if(!host || !host.classList.contains('selected')) p.remove();
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected[data-overlay-id]').forEach(function(clip){
      var id = clip.getAttribute('data-overlay-id');
      var ov = overlays.find(function(o){ return o && String(o.id) === id; });
      var pill = clip.querySelector(':scope > .ev-clip-dur-pill');
      if(!ov || (ov.type !== 'video' && ov.type !== 'image')){ if(pill) pill.remove(); return; }
      var len = Number(ov.trimEnd) - Number(ov.trimStart);
      if(!(len > 0)) return;
      if(!pill){ pill = document.createElement('div'); pill.className = 'ev-clip-dur-pill'; clip.appendChild(pill); }
      var txt = fmt(len);
      if(pill.textContent !== txt) pill.textContent = txt;
      var cRect = clip.getBoundingClientRect();
      var want = (sRect.left + 6) - cRect.left;              // keep pill at the visible left edge
      var max = Math.max(6, cRect.width - pill.offsetWidth - 6);
      var left = Math.max(6, Math.min(max, want));
      if(pill.style.left !== left+'px') pill.style.left = left+'px';
    });
  }
  function queue(){ if(!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(sync); }
  function boot(){
    var scroller = document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
    var rows = document.getElementById('overlayRows');
    if(!scroller || !rows){ setTimeout(boot, 400); return; }
    scroller.addEventListener('scroll', queue, {passive:true});
    new MutationObserver(queue).observe(rows, {childList:true, subtree:true, attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class','style','data-overlay-id']});
    // The CapCut recording hides the pill while an edge handle is being dragged.
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
      if(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.anim-clip-handle, [data-ov-handle]')) document.body.classList.add('ev-pill-hide');
    }, true);
    var show = function(){ document.body.classList.remove('ev-pill-hide'); queue(); };
    document.addEventListener('pointerup', show, true);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel', show, true);
    queue();
  }
  boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v239-canonical-keyframe-contact-sync">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV239KeyframeSyncInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV239KeyframeSyncInstalled=true;
  let raf=0;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  function currentTime(){
    try{
      const t=typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec);
      return Number.isFinite(t)?t:0;
    }catch(_e){return Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}
  }
  function nearest(keys,property,t,tolerance){
    let best=-1,distance=Infinity;
    for(let i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
      const key=keys[i];if(!key||(property&&key.property!==property))continue;
      const d=Math.abs(Number(key.t)-t);
      if(Number.isFinite(d)&&d<=tolerance&&d<distance){best=i;distance=d;}
    }
    return best;
  }
  function paint(track,attribute,index){
    if(!track)return;
    track.querySelectorAll('.kf-marker['+attribute+']').forEach(marker=>{
      const active=Number(marker.getAttribute(attribute))===index&&index>=0;
      marker.classList.toggle('active',active);
      marker.classList.toggle('under-playhead',active);
    });
  }
  function sync(){
    raf=0;
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
      if(!id)return;
      const t=currentTime();
      const objectClip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
      if(objectClip){
        const prop=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(objectClip):(objectClip.type==='audio'?'volume':'transform');
        const keys=Array.isArray(objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
        const tol=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/(typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?pxPerSecond():100));
        const idx=nearest(keys,prop,t,Math.max(.001,tol));
        const prior=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
        if(idx>=0)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id,index:idx,source:'playhead'};
        else if(prior&&String(prior.id)===id)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
        const track=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-object-kf-track]')).find(node=>String(node.dataset.objectKfTrack||'')===id)||null;
        paint(track,'data-object-kf-idx',idx);
        if(prior&&String(prior.id)===id&&Number(prior.index)!==idx&&typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
        return;
      }
      let clip=null,track=null;
      if(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id)){
        clip=typeof window.getClipByRef==='function'?window.getClipByRef(id):null;
        track=$(id==='main'?'kfTrack':'kfTrack-'+id);
      }else if(/^char\d+$/.test(id)){
        clip=typeof activeClip!=='undefined'?activeClip:null;
        track=$('kfTrack');
      }
      if(!track)return;
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)?clip.keyframes:[];
      const tol=typeof window.keyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.keyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/(typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?pxPerSecond():100));
      const idx=nearest(keys,null,t,Math.max(.001,tol));
      const previous=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null?Number(selectedKfIdx):-1;
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined')selectedKfIdx=idx>=0?idx:null;
      paint(track,'data-kf-idx',idx);
      if(previous!==idx&&typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle canonical keyframe sync:',err);}
  }
  function schedule(){if(raf)return;raf=requestAnimationFrame(sync);}
  // The clock setter is called on every accepted scrub frame, after moved clips' keyframe
  // timestamps have already been committed. This schedules one time-based selection pass.
  ['setCurrentAbsoluteTime','seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds','renderTimelineScrubPreview','movePlayhead'].forEach(name=>{
    const prior=window[name];
    if(typeof prior!=='function'||prior.__evv239KeyframeSync)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);schedule();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv239KeyframeSync=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',schedule,{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',schedule,{capture:true,passive:true});
  const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(scroller)scroller.addEventListener('scroll',schedule,{passive:true});
  schedule();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v240-selection-keyframe-final-guard">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV240FinalGuard)return;
  window.__ethvelleV240FinalGuard=true;
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function'&&!priorSelect.__evv240KeepTapSelection){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const result=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);
      // The v76 playhead auto-select helper may run on pointer-up even for a simple tap.
      // Keep a tap's explicitly selected clip stable; real timeline scrubbing is exempt.
      if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
        const selectedValue=which==null?(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:null):which;
        window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId=selectedValue==null?'':String(selectedValue);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv240KeepTapSelection=true;
    window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  // When the playhead moves during an actual scrub, v76 remains free to update the selection.
  // A non-scrub programmatic render/scroll should not erase the last explicitly selected clip.
  const timeline=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
  if(timeline&&!timeline.dataset.evv240TapGuard){
    timeline.dataset.evv240TapGuard='1';
    let pointer=null;
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      const clip=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id],#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id],#timelineInner .anim-clip'):null;
      pointer=clip?{id:clip.dataset.overlayId||clip.dataset.bgId||(clip.id==='animClipEl'?'main':clip.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+clip.dataset.segIdx:''),x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,moved:false,pointerId:e.pointerId}:null;
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!pointer||pointer.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
      if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-pointer.x,e.clientY-pointer.y)>=7)pointer.moved=true;
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    const clear=e=>{if(pointer&&pointer.pointerId===e.pointerId)pointer=null;};
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',clear,{capture:false,passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointercancel',clear,{capture:false,passive:true});
  }
})();

<!-- Ethvelle v242: restore timeline watermarks, remove only clip-length timer chips, apply FX per layer, and add shape clips. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v242-clip-duration-only-css">

<script id="ethvelle-v242-media-shapes-and-clip-duration-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safeText=(s)=>String(s||'').trim().replace(/[\u200e\u200f]/g,'');
  const looksLikeDuration=(value)=>{
    const v=safeText(value).replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g,'');
    return /^(?:\d{1,2}:)?\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?(?:\s*s)?$|^\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*(?:s|sec|secs|seconds)$/i.test(v);
  };
  function hideClipDurationBadges(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip,#timelineInner .anim-clip,#timelineInner .track-clip').forEach(clip=>{
      ['::before','::after'].forEach(pseudo=>{
        try{
          const value=getComputedStyle(clip,pseudo).content;
          const attrs=String(value||'').match(/attr\((data-[a-z0-9_-]+)\)/ig)||[];
          const attrValue=attrs.map(a=>{const m=a.match(/attr\((data-[a-z0-9_-]+)\)/i);return m?clip.getAttribute(m[1]):'';}).join(' ');
          if((value&&value!=='none'&&value!=='normal'&&looksLikeDuration(value))||looksLikeDuration(attrValue))
            clip.classList.add(pseudo==='::before'?'evv-hide-clip-duration-before':'evv-hide-clip-duration-after');
        }catch(_e){}
      });
      const cr=clip.getBoundingClientRect();
      clip.querySelectorAll(':scope > *, :scope > * *').forEach(node=>{
        if(!node||node.nodeType!==1)return;
        if(node.matches('.evlt-quality-row-badge,.clip-media-label,.clip-media-label-audio,.overlay-clip-label,[data-ev-quality-badge]'))return;
        if(node.matches('.anim-clip-handle,.sel-edge-top,.sel-edge-bottom,.sel-edge-left,.sel-edge-right,.keyframe-track,.kf-marker'))return;
        const classes=String(node.className&&node.className.baseVal!==undefined?node.className.baseVal:node.className||'');
        const named=/\b(timer|duration|timecode|clip-time|cliptime|clip-timer)\b/i.test(classes);
        const attrText=[node.getAttribute('data-duration'),node.getAttribute('data-time'),node.getAttribute('data-clip-time'),node.getAttribute('aria-label'),node.getAttribute('title')].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
        const txt=safeText((node.textContent||'')+' '+attrText);
        const r=node.getBoundingClientRect();
        const topRight=r.width>0&&r.height>0&&r.width<=120&&r.height<=32&&r.right>=cr.right-Math.max(28,Math.min(48,cr.width*.12))&&r.top<=cr.top+Math.max(22,cr.height*.25);
        if(topRight&&(named||looksLikeDuration(txt)))node.classList.add(looksLikeDuration(txt)?'evv-hide-clip-duration-badge':'evv-hide-clip-duration');
      });
    });
  }
  let durationRaf=0;
  function scheduleDurationSweep(){if(durationRaf)return;durationRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{durationRaf=0;hideClipDurationBadges();});}
  const timeline=$('timelineInner');
  if(timeline&&typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(scheduleDurationSweep).observe(timeline,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','data-duration','data-time','aria-label','title']});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scheduleDurationSweep,{once:true});else scheduleDurationSweep();

  // Applying a scene effect to a selected visual clip creates a normal five-second
  // timeline effect layer. It overlaps (uses a free sub-lane) and tracks the selected
  // media clip, so it never washes the entire canvas or pushes the source clip away.
  const sceneEffectTypes=[]; /* v435: inbuilt scene effects removed */
  const priorSetVisualEffect=window.setEthvelleVisualEffect;
  function clonePlain(value){try{return value==null?value:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));}catch(_e){return value;}}
  function transformAt(clip,time){
    let tr=null;
    try{const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',time):null;tr=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,time,keyed):null;tr=tr||keyed||clip.transform||{};}catch(_e){tr=clip.transform||{};}
    let motion={};try{motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?(window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,time)||{}):{};}catch(_e){}
    return {scale:(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1),offsetX:(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0),offsetY:(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0),rotation:(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0)};
  }
  function effectThumb(type){
    const glyph={rain:'≋',storm:'ϟ',clouds:'☁',snow:'✳',lightning:'ϟ',flash:'✦',fog:'〰',sunrise:'◉',blur:'◌',...(window.__evSceneFxPack?window.__evSceneFxPack.glyphs:{})}[type]||'FX';
    const svg='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="96" height="48" viewBox="0 0 96 48"><defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#243B80"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#503B8D"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="96" height="48" rx="7" fill="url(#g)"/><text x="48" y="32" text-anchor="middle" font-size="25" fill="#FFFFFF" font-family="Arial">'+glyph+'</text></svg>';
    return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
  }
  function addTrackedEffectClip(source,type){
    const startNow=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const start=startNow>=Number(source.trimStart)&&startNow<Number(source.trimEnd)?startNow:Number(source.trimStart)||0;
    const base=transformAt(source,start);
    const id='ovfx'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6);
    const transparent='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><rect width="512" height="512" fill="none"/></svg>';
    const src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(transparent);
    const thumb=effectThumb(type);
    const typeLabels={rain:'Rain',storm:'Storm',clouds:'Clouds',snow:'Snow',lightning:'Lightning',flash:'Flash Lighting',fog:'Fog',sunrise:'Cloud Sunrise',blur:'Blur',...(window.__evSceneFxPack?window.__evSceneFxPack.labels:{})};
    const clip={
      id,name:(typeLabels[type]||'Video')+' Effect',color:'#5578F5',type:'image',src,
      sourceDuration:5,sourceOffset:0,sourceWidth:Number(source.sourceWidth)||512,sourceHeight:Number(source.sourceHeight)||512,
      trimStart:start,trimEnd:start+5,thumbs:[thumb],peaks:[],speed:1,
      transform:{...base},mirrorX:!!source.mirrorX,mirrorY:!!source.mirrorY,
      crop:clonePlain(source.crop),visualMask:null, // v391: an effect starts unmasked; a mask applied to this effect later remains independent.
      visualEffects:{type,intensity:135,density:145,speed:115,scale:100,x:0,y:0},
      ethvelleEffectClip:true,ethvelleEffectType:type,ethvelleEffectLabel:typeLabels[type]||'Effect',ethvelleEffectTargetId:String(source.id),
      clipTracking:{enabled:false,targetId:'',targetBaseTransform:null,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,followScale:true,followRotation:true} /* v416: an effect is an independent clip; tracking is opt-in from the Tracking panel */
    };
    overlays.push(clip);
    timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,clip.trimEnd);
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayListPanel==='function')renderOverlayListPanel();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(id);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast((typeLabels[type]||'Effect')+' effect added · 5 seconds',{duration:1500});}catch(_e){}
    return clip;
  }
  window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect=function(clip,time){
    if(!clip||clip.ethvelleEffectClip)return null;
    if(clip.visualEffects&&clip.visualEffects.type==='blur')return clip.visualEffects;
    const t=Number(time)||0,id=String(clip.id||'');
    const layer=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectType==='blur'&&String(o.ethvelleEffectTargetId)===id&&t>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(o.trimEnd||0)&&t>=Number(clip.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(clip.trimEnd||0));
    return layer?layer.visualEffects:null;
  };
  if(typeof priorSetVisualEffect==='function'){
    window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=function(type){
      const selected=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
      if(type==='none'&&selected&&['video','image'].includes(selected.type)){
        (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(layer=>{if(layer&&layer.ethvelleEffectClip&&String(layer.ethvelleEffectTargetId)===String(selected.id)){layer.visualEffects=Object.assign({},layer.visualEffects||{},{type:'none'});layer.ethvelleEffectType='none';}});
        return priorSetVisualEffect.apply(this,arguments);
      }
      if(type==='none'||!sceneEffectTypes.includes(String(type))||!selected)return priorSetVisualEffect.apply(this,arguments);
      if(selected.ethvelleEffectClip){selected.visualEffects=Object.assign({},selected.visualEffects||{},{type:String(type)});selected.ethvelleEffectType=String(type);try{renderFrame();persistProjectState();renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');}catch(_e){}return selected;}
      if(['video','image'].includes(selected.type))return addTrackedEffectClip(selected,String(type));
      return priorSetVisualEffect.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  // Add a readable label inside effect clips so they remain identifiable among image layers.
  const priorClipFill=window.clipFillMarkup;
  if(typeof priorClipFill==='function'&&!priorClipFill.__evv243EffectLabel){
    const wrapped=function(ov,width){
      if(ov&&ov.ethvelleEffectClip&&ov.type==='image'){
        const src=(ov.thumbs&&ov.thumbs.length)?ov.thumbs[0]:ov.src;
        return (src?`<div class="clip-thumb-strip" style="background-image:url(&quot;${escapeHtml(String(src))}&quot;);background-size:100% 100%;background-repeat:no-repeat"></div>`:'')+`<div class="overlay-clip-label">${escapeHtml(ov.ethvelleEffectLabel||ov.name||'Effect')}</div>`;
      }
      return priorClipFill.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv243EffectLabel=true;window.clipFillMarkup=wrapped;
  }

})();
</script>
