<script id="ethvelle-v244-effect-clip-mosaic-replace-and-preview-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const list=()=>Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]);
  const selectedId=()=>String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip||''));
  const getSelected=()=>list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===selectedId())||null;
  const labels={rain:'Rain',storm:'Storm',clouds:'Clouds',snow:'Snow',lightning:'Lightning',flash:'Flash Lighting',fog:'Fog',sunrise:'Cloud Sunrise',blur:'Blur',mosaic:'Mosaic Blur',...(window.__evSceneFxPack?window.__evSceneFxPack.labels:{})};
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  /* stageW/stageH and overlayMediaEls are top-level lexical bindings in Ethvelle's
     classic scripts. Read them directly (window.stageW/window.overlayMediaEls are not
     guaranteed to exist in Android WebView). */
  function stageWidthValue(fallback){try{if(typeof stageW!=='undefined'&&Number(stageW)>0)return Number(stageW);}catch(_e){}return Number(fallback)||1;}
  function stageHeightValue(fallback){try{if(typeof stageH!=='undefined'&&Number(stageH)>0)return Number(stageH);}catch(_e){}return Number(fallback)||1;}
  function liveMediaFor(id){try{return typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[String(id)]||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}}

  /* The v234 pill updater can keep recreating the node after a selection or scroll.
     Remove it at insertion time too; CSS above is the final visual safety net. */
  function removeDurationPills(root){
    const scope=root&&root.querySelectorAll?root:document;
    scope.querySelectorAll('.ev-clip-dur-pill').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  }
  removeDurationPills(document);
  const timerRoot=$('timelineInner');
  if(timerRoot&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const timerObserver=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      for(const rec of records){
        if(rec.addedNodes)rec.addedNodes.forEach(n=>{if(n.nodeType===1){if(n.matches&&n.matches('.ev-clip-dur-pill'))n.remove();removeDurationPills(n);}});
      }
    });
    timerObserver.observe(timerRoot,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }

  function markEffectClipNodes(){
    const arr=list();
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const item=arr.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.overlayId));
      const yes=!!(item&&(item.ethvelleEffectClip||item.ethvelleAssetEffect));
      el.classList.toggle('ev-v244-effect-clip',yes);
      if(yes){el.dataset.evEffectType=String(item.ethvelleEffectType||'effect');el.title=(item.ethvelleEffectLabel||item.name||'Effect')+(item.ethvelleAssetEffect?'':' · 5 seconds');}
      else{delete el.dataset.evEffectType;}
    });
  }
  const oldRenderLayers=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof oldRenderLayers==='function'&&!oldRenderLayers.__evv244MarkEffects){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderLayers.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(markEffectClipNodes);return r;};
    wrapped.__evv244MarkEffects=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',markEffectClipNodes,{once:true});else requestAnimationFrame(markEffectClipNodes);

  /* Replacing an effect clip means choosing another effect for that SAME five-second clip,
     not opening the device media picker and not creating a duplicate layer. */
  const oldReplace=window.replaceSelectedClip;
  if(typeof oldReplace==='function'&&!oldReplace.__evv244EffectReplace){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const clip=getSelected();
      if(clip&&clip.ethvelleEffectClip){
        if(typeof window.openPanel==='function')window.openPanel('visualEffects');
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.renderVisualPanel==='function')window.renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');}catch(_e){}});
        return clip;
      }
      return oldReplace.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv244EffectReplace=true;window.replaceSelectedClip=wrapped;
  }

  function setEffectMetadata(clip,type){
    if(!clip||!clip.ethvelleEffectClip)return;
    const label=labels[type]||'Effect';
    clip.ethvelleEffectType=String(type);
    clip.ethvelleEffectLabel=label;
    clip.name=label+' Effect';
    clip.color='#5578F5';
    clip.visualEffects=Object.assign({},clip.visualEffects||{},{type:String(type)});
    clip.thumbs=[effectThumb(type)];
  }
  function effectThumb(type){
    const glyph={rain:'≋',storm:'ϟ',clouds:'☁',snow:'✳',lightning:'ϟ',flash:'✦',fog:'〰',sunrise:'◉',blur:'◌',mosaic:'▦',...(window.__evSceneFxPack?window.__evSceneFxPack.glyphs:{})}[type]||'FX';
    const svg='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="96" height="48" viewBox="0 0 96 48"><defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#243B80"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#503B8D"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="96" height="48" rx="7" fill="url(#g)"/><text x="48" y="32" text-anchor="middle" font-size="25" fill="#FFFFFF" font-family="Arial">'+glyph+'</text></svg>';
    return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
  }

  /* Add a real Mosaic Blur option. For a source clip, reuse the existing v243 blur-clip
     creation path, then change the newly-created layer's effect type to mosaic. For an
     effect clip, only its type changes: its id, duration, row and target remain unchanged. */
  const previousEffectSetter=window.setEthvelleVisualEffect;
  if(typeof previousEffectSetter==='function'&&!previousEffectSetter.__evv244Mosaic){
    const wrapped=function(type){
      type=String(type||'');
      const before=getSelected();
      if(type==='mosaic'){
        if(!before)return previousEffectSetter.apply(this,arguments);
        let result=null;
        if(before.ethvelleEffectClip){
          result=before;
        }else if(before.type==='video'||before.type==='image'){
          result=previousEffectSetter.call(this,'blur');
          if(!result||!result.ethvelleEffectClip){
            const now=getSelected();
            result=now&&now.ethvelleEffectClip?now:null;
          }
        }else return previousEffectSetter.apply(this,arguments);
        if(result){
          setEffectMetadata(result,'mosaic');
          try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
          try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayListPanel==='function')window.renderOverlayListPanel();}catch(_e){}
          try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
          try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
          requestFxPreview();
          requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.renderVisualPanel==='function')window.renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');}catch(_e){}});
          return result;
        }
      }
      const result=previousEffectSetter.apply(this,arguments);
      const after=getSelected();
      if(after&&after.ethvelleEffectClip&&labels[type]&&type!=='none'){
        setEffectMetadata(after,type);
        try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
        requestFxPreview();
        return after;
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv244Mosaic=true;window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=wrapped;
  }

  /* Keep mosaic out of the regular CSS blur filter. Its pixels are drawn from the selected
     source media frame by the stage/export canvas below. Standard Blur remains unchanged. */
  const oldBlurLookup=window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect;
  if(typeof oldBlurLookup==='function'&&!oldBlurLookup.__evv244NoMosaicFilter){
    const wrapped=function(clip,time){
      const fx=oldBlurLookup.apply(this,arguments);
      return fx&&String(fx.type||'')==='mosaic'?null:fx;
    };
    wrapped.__evv244NoMosaicFilter=true;window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect=wrapped;
  }

  /* Restore the effect controls in this panel only. Controls write to the selected effect
     clip's saved settings and repaint the current paused frame immediately. */
  const oldRenderVisualPanel=window.renderVisualPanel;
  if(typeof oldRenderVisualPanel==='function'&&!oldRenderVisualPanel.__evv244FxControls){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=oldRenderVisualPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='visualEffects')requestAnimationFrame(injectFxControls);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv244FxControls=true;window.renderVisualPanel=wrapped;
  }
  function injectFxControls(){
    const host=$('evvVisualPanel');if(!host)return;
    const clip=getSelected();if(!clip||!['video','image'].includes(clip.type)&&!clip.ethvelleEffectClip)return;
    const fx=Object.assign({type:'none',intensity:135,density:145,speed:115,scale:100,quality:100,x:0,y:0},clip.visualEffects||{});
    const grid=host.querySelector('.evv-choice-grid');
    if(grid&&clip.ethvelleEffectKind!=='character'&&!grid.querySelector('[data-ev-v244-mosaic]')){
      const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='evv-choice'+(fx.type==='mosaic'?' active':'');btn.textContent='Mosaic Blur';btn.dataset.evV244Mosaic='1';
      btn.addEventListener('click',()=>window.setEthvelleVisualEffect('mosaic'));
      grid.appendChild(btn);
    }
    let panel=host.querySelector('.evv-fx-adjustments');
    if(!panel){panel=document.createElement('div');panel.className='evv-fx-adjustments';host.appendChild(panel);}
    const fields=[
      ['intensity','Intensity',0,250,1,'%'],['density','Density',10,300,1,'%'],
      ['speed','Animation Speed',0,300,1,'%'],['scale','Effect Size',25,250,1,'%'],['quality','Quality',25,150,1,'%'],
      ['x','Horizontal Position',-100,100,1,'%'],['y','Vertical Position',-100,100,1,'%']
    ];
    panel.innerHTML=fields.filter(f=>!(clip.ethvelleEffectKind==='character'&&f[0]==='density')&&!(clip.ethvelleEffectKind!=='character'&&f[0]==='quality')).map(([prop,label,min,max,step,unit])=>{
      const value=clamp(fx[prop],min,max),id='evv244_fx_'+prop;
      return '<div class="evv-slider-card"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="'+id+'">'+label+'</label><output id="'+id+'_out">'+Math.round(value)+unit+'</output></div><input id="'+id+'" type="range" min="'+min+'" max="'+max+'" step="'+step+'" value="'+value+'" oninput="window.setEthvelleEffectSetting(\''+prop+'\',this.value,false);document.getElementById(\''+id+'_out\').textContent=Math.round(Number(this.value))+\''+unit+'\'" onchange="window.setEthvelleEffectSetting(\''+prop+'\',this.value,true)"></div>';
    }).join('');
    panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(input=>{
      const paint=()=>{const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,v=Number(input.value)||0;input.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',clamp((v-min)/(max-min||1)*100,0,100)+'%');};
      input.addEventListener('input',paint);paint();
    });
  }

  /* Draw a real pixel-block mosaic of the target's video/image pixels. It is clipped by the
     target's crop/mask and follows the same keyframed/tracked transform as the source clip. */
  const scratchById=Object.create(null);
  function drawMosaicEffect(ctx,effectClip,t,ow,oh){
    const targetId=String(effectClip.ethvelleEffectTargetId||'');
    const target=list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===targetId);
    if(!target||!['video','image'].includes(target.type))return;
    if(t<Number(effectClip.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(effectClip.trimEnd||0)||t<Number(target.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(target.trimEnd||0))return;
    const media=liveMediaFor(targetId);
    if(!media)return;
    const sw=media.tagName==='VIDEO'?Number(media.videoWidth):Number(media.naturalWidth||media.width);
    const sh=media.tagName==='VIDEO'?Number(media.videoHeight):Number(media.naturalHeight||media.height);
    if(!(sw>0&&sh>0))return;
    if(media.tagName==='VIDEO'&&(media.readyState<2||media.seeking))return;
    if(media.tagName==='IMG'&&!media.complete)return;
    const fx=Object.assign({intensity:135,density:145,scale:100},effectClip.visualEffects||{});
    const strength=clamp(fx.intensity,0,250)/135;if(strength<=0)return;
    const scale=clamp(fx.scale,25,250)/100;
    const density=clamp(fx.density,10,300)/100;
    const block=Math.max(2,Math.min(72,Math.round((10/density)*(100/scale))));
    const lowW=Math.max(1,Math.min(640,Math.round(sw/block))),lowH=Math.max(1,Math.min(640,Math.round(sh/block)));
    let scratch=scratchById[targetId];
    if(!scratch){scratch=document.createElement('canvas');scratchById[targetId]=scratch;}
    if(scratch.width!==lowW||scratch.height!==lowH){scratch.width=lowW;scratch.height=lowH;}
    const sctx=scratch.getContext('2d');if(!sctx)return;
    try{sctx.clearRect(0,0,lowW,lowH);sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;sctx.drawImage(media,0,0,lowW,lowH);}catch(_e){return;}

    const stageWidth=stageWidthValue(ow),stageHeight=stageHeightValue(oh);
    const swScale=ow/Math.max(1,stageWidth),shScale=oh/Math.max(1,stageHeight);
    const fit=Math.min(stageWidth/sw,stageHeight/sh),cw=sw*fit,ch=sh*fit,cx=(stageWidth-cw)/2,cy=(stageHeight-ch)/2;
    let crop={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(effectClip.crop||target.crop):(effectClip.crop||target.crop||crop);}catch(_e){}
    const left=clamp(crop.left,0,.92),right=clamp(crop.right,0,.92),top=clamp(crop.top,0,.92),bottom=clamp(crop.bottom,0,.92);
    const vx=cx+left*cw,vy=cy+top*ch,vw=Math.max(1,cw*(1-left-right)),vh=Math.max(1,ch*(1-top-bottom));
    let keyed=null,tr=null,motion={x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
    try{keyed=window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(effectClip,'transform',t):null;}catch(_e){}
    try{tr=effectClip.clipTracking&&effectClip.clipTracking.enabled&&window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(effectClip,t,keyed):null;}catch(_e){}
    tr=tr||keyed||effectClip.transform||target.transform||{};
    try{if(window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState)motion=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(effectClip,t)||motion;}catch(_e){}
    const ox=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0),oy=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0);
    const sc=Math.max(.0001,(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1));
    const rot=(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0);
    const owScale=stageWidthValue(ow),ohScale=stageHeightValue(oh);
    ctx.save();ctx.scale(swScale,shScale);
    ctx.translate(owScale/2+ox,ohScale/2+oy);if(rot)ctx.rotate(rot*Math.PI/180);
    ctx.scale(sc*(effectClip.mirrorX?-1:1),sc*(effectClip.mirrorY?-1:1));ctx.translate(-owScale/2,-ohScale/2);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(vx,vy,vw,vh);ctx.clip();
    if(effectClip.visualMask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip)try{window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(ctx,effectClip.visualMask,owScale,ohScale);}catch(_e){}
    const prevSmooth=ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled;ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,strength);
    ctx.drawImage(scratch,0,0,lowW,lowH,cx,cy,cw,ch);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=prevSmooth;ctx.restore();
  }
  const oldDrawFx=window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext;
  if(typeof oldDrawFx==='function'&&!oldDrawFx.__evv244Mosaic){
    const wrapped=function(ctx,t,outW,outH){
      const r=oldDrawFx.apply(this,arguments);
      const time=Number(t)||0,ow=outW||stageWidthValue(0),oh=outH||stageHeightValue(0);
      if(ctx&&Array.isArray(list()))list().forEach(o=>{
        if(!o||!o.ethvelleEffectClip||String(o.ethvelleEffectType||'')!=='mosaic')return;
        drawMosaicEffect(ctx,o,time,ow,oh);
      });
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__evv244Mosaic=true;window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext=wrapped;
  }

  /* renderFrame() also runs for a paused scrub/selection. The old effect preview was only
     refreshed by drawStick(), which is not guaranteed to run for a still media-only frame. */
  let fxRaf=0;
  function paintEffectPreview(){
    fxRaf=0;
    if(window.__evFxGate&&!window.__evFxGate(paintEffectPreview))return;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext)return;
    let canvas=$('evcaV70FxCanvas');
    if(!canvas){canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.id='evcaV70FxCanvas';stage.appendChild(canvas);}
    const sw0=Math.max(1,stageWidthValue(stage.clientWidth||1)),sh0=Math.max(1,stageHeightValue(stage.clientHeight||1));
    const HD=window.ethvelleFxPreviewSize?window.ethvelleFxPreviewSize(sw0,sh0):[sw0,sh0],sw=HD[0],sh=HD[1];
    if(canvas.width!==sw||canvas.height!==sh){canvas.width=sw;canvas.height=sh;}
    canvas.style.position='absolute';canvas.style.inset='0';canvas.style.width='100%';canvas.style.height='100%';canvas.style.pointerEvents='none';canvas.style.zIndex='8';
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;
    const now=Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,sw,sh);
    try{window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext(ctx,now,sw,sh);canvas.dataset.evFxPainted='1';canvas.dataset.evFxTime=String(now);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle effect still-preview:',err);}
  }
  function requestFxPreview(){if(!fxRaf)fxRaf=requestAnimationFrame(paintEffectPreview);}
  const oldRenderFrame=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof oldRenderFrame==='function'&&!oldRenderFrame.__evv244FxStill){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderFrame.apply(this,arguments);requestFxPreview();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv244FxStill=true;window.renderFrame=wrapped;
  }
  const oldRenderMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderMedia.__evv244FxStill){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderMedia.apply(this,arguments);requestFxPreview();markEffectClipNodes();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv244FxStill=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
  const oldEffectSetting=window.setEthvelleEffectSetting;
  if(typeof oldEffectSetting==='function'&&!oldEffectSetting.__evv244Preview){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldEffectSetting.apply(this,arguments);requestFxPreview();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv244Preview=true;window.setEthvelleEffectSetting=wrapped;
  }

  window.__ethvelleV244Debug={removeDurationPills,markEffectClipNodes,paintEffectPreview,drawMosaicEffect};

<script id="ethvelle-v245-flat-effect-clip-live-preview-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const list=()=>Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
  const safeLabel=v=>String(v||'Effect').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const labels={rain:'Rain',storm:'Storm',clouds:'Clouds',snow:'Snow',lightning:'Lightning',flash:'Flash Lighting',fog:'Fog',sunrise:'Cloud Sunrise',blur:'Blur',mosaic:'Mosaic Blur',...(window.__evSceneFxPack?window.__evSceneFxPack.labels:{})};

  /* The clipFillMarkup override from v243 generated a thumbnail strip for effects. Replace
     that artwork with one plain, non-repeating label block so it cannot look like stacked
     round/circular mini-clips. */
  const previousFill=window.clipFillMarkup;
  if(typeof previousFill==='function'&&!previousFill.__evv245FlatEffects){
    const wrapped=function(clip,width){
      if(clip&&clip.ethvelleEffectClip){
        const title=labels[String(clip.ethvelleEffectType||'')]||clip.ethvelleEffectLabel||clip.name||'Effect';
        return '<div class="anim-clip-body ev-v246-effect-body"><span class="anim-clip-label ev-v246-effect-label">'+safeLabel(title)+'</span></div>';
      }
      return previousFill.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv245FlatEffects=true;
    window.clipFillMarkup=wrapped;
  }

  function markFlatEffectNodes(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const clip=list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.overlayId));
      const yes=!!(clip&&(clip.ethvelleEffectClip||clip.ethvelleAssetEffect));
      el.classList.toggle('ev-v245-effect-clip',yes);
      el.classList.toggle('ev-v246-effect-clip',yes);
      if(yes){
        el.classList.add('ev-v244-effect-clip');
        const oldThumb=el.querySelector('.clip-thumb-strip');if(oldThumb)oldThumb.remove();
        el.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip-label,.ev-v245-effect-fill').forEach(n=>n.remove());
        let body=el.querySelector('.ev-v246-effect-body');
        const label=labels[String(clip.ethvelleEffectType||'')]||clip.ethvelleEffectLabel||clip.name||'Effect';
        if(!body){
          body=document.createElement('div');body.className='anim-clip-body ev-v246-effect-body';
          const text=document.createElement('span');text.className='anim-clip-label ev-v246-effect-label';
          body.appendChild(text);el.insertBefore(body,el.firstChild);
        }
        const text=body.querySelector('.ev-v246-effect-label');if(text&&text.textContent!==label)text.textContent=label;
      }
    });
  }

  /* Refresh the effect canvas on each animation frame during canvas transforms. In v244 the
     live video element moved immediately, but the separately-painted FX canvas was refreshed
     only after renderFrame/renderOverlayMedia; that left the effect behind/blank until release.
     This scheduler is RAF-coalesced so a pointer stream still causes at most one effect paint
     per display frame. */
  let previewRaf=0;
  function scheduleEffectPreview(){
    if(previewRaf)return;
    previewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      previewRaf=0;
      try{
        const dbg=window.__ethvelleV244Debug;
        if(dbg&&typeof dbg.paintEffectPreview==='function')dbg.paintEffectPreview();
        else if(typeof window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext==='function'){
          const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return;
          let canvas=$('evcaV70FxCanvas');
          if(!canvas){canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.id='evcaV70FxCanvas';stage.appendChild(canvas);}
          const w0=Math.max(1,Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:stage.clientWidth)||stage.clientWidth||1);
          const h0=Math.max(1,Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:stage.clientHeight)||stage.clientHeight||1);
          const HD=window.ethvelleFxPreviewSize?window.ethvelleFxPreviewSize(w0,h0):[w0,h0],w=HD[0],h=HD[1];
          if(canvas.width!==w||canvas.height!==h){canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;}
          canvas.style.cssText+=';position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;pointer-events:none;z-index:8';
          const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(ctx){ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext(ctx,Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0)||0,w,h);}
        }
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle effect drag preview:',err);}
    });
  }
  function wrapRefresh(name,marker){
    const base=window[name];
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base[marker])return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=base.apply(this,arguments);scheduleEffectPreview();return result;};
    wrapped[marker]=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  wrapRefresh('renderOverlayLayers','__evv245Fx');
  wrapRefresh('renderOverlayMedia','__evv245Fx');
  wrapRefresh('renderFrame','__evv245Fx');
  wrapRefresh('applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast','__evv245Fx');
  wrapRefresh('ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource','__evv245Fx');

  /* Also cover direct pointer paths used by the selection box/transform handles. The target
     may be a child overlay or a sibling selection layer, so test containment in canvasStage. */
  let stagePointerId=null;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const stage=$('canvasStage');
    if(stage&&stage.contains(e.target))stagePointerId=e.pointerId;
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(stagePointerId!==null&&e.pointerId===stagePointerId)scheduleEffectPreview();
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  function endStagePointer(e){if(stagePointerId===e.pointerId){stagePointerId=null;scheduleEffectPreview();}}
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',endStagePointer,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',endStagePointer,true);
  window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{stagePointerId=null;scheduleEffectPreview();});

  /* Keep the clip preview synchronized during timeline moves too; time-range checks inside
     the renderer still determine whether the effect is active at the current playhead. */
  const timeline=$('timelineScroll')||$('timelineInner');
  if(timeline){
    timeline.addEventListener('pointermove',scheduleEffectPreview,{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',scheduleEffectPreview,{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointercancel',scheduleEffectPreview,{passive:true});
  }

  const oldRender=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'&&!oldRender.__evv245MarkFlat){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(markFlatEffectNodes);return r;};
    wrapped.__evv245MarkFlat=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',markFlatEffectNodes,{once:true});
  else requestAnimationFrame(markFlatEffectNodes);
  scheduleEffectPreview();
  window.__ethvelleV245Debug={markFlatEffectNodes,scheduleEffectPreview};
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v249-effect-frames-full-bleed-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  function syncFullBleedFrames(){
    const root=document.getElementById('timelineInner');if(!root)return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip.ev-v246-effect-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const body=el.querySelector('.ev-v246-effect-body');if(!body)return;
      const strip=body.querySelector('.ev-v248-effect-frames');if(!strip)return;
      const width=Math.max(30,el.getBoundingClientRect().width||0);
      const count=Math.max(2,Math.min(24,Math.floor(width/27)));
      const id=String(el.dataset.overlayId||'');
      let type='blur',asset=null;
      try{const c=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(x=>x&&String(x.id)===id);if(c){type=String(c.ethvelleEffectType||'blur');if(c.ethvelleAssetEffect){asset=c;type='asset:'+c.ethvelleAssetEffect.id;}}}catch(_e){}
      const signature=type+'|'+count;
      el.style.setProperty('--ev-v249-frame-count',String(count));
      strip.style.setProperty('--ev-v249-frame-count',String(count));
      if(strip.dataset.signature!==signature){
        const svgs={
          rain:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 14.5a4 4 0 0 1 .5-8A6 6 0 0 1 17 8a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 1 6.8M8 17l-1 3m6-3-1 3m6-3-1 3"/></svg>',
          storm:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 13.5a4 4 0 0 1 .5-8A6 6 0 0 1 17 7a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 1 6.5M13 12l-4 6h3l-1 4 5-7h-3l1-3"/></svg>',
          clouds:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 15a4 4 0 0 1 .5-8A6 6 0 0 1 16 8a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 1 7H4Z"/></svg>',
          snow:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2v20M3.3 7l17.4 10M3.3 17 20.7 7M9 4l3 2 3-2M9 20l3-2 3 2"/></svg>',
          lightning:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M13 2 5 13h6l-1 9 9-13h-6l1-7Z"/></svg>',
          flash:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2v4m0 12v4M2 12h4m12 0h4M4.9 4.9l2.8 2.8m8.6 8.6 2.8 2.8m0-14.2-2.8 2.8m-8.6 8.6-2.8 2.8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/></svg>',
          fog:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 8h18M1 12h22M4 16h16M7 20h10"/></svg>',
          sunrise:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 18h18M5 15a7 7 0 0 1 14 0M12 2v3m-8 1 2 2m14-2-2 2"/></svg>',
          blur:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="6"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/></svg>',
          mosaic:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 3h8v8H3zM13 3h8v5h-8zM13 10h8v11h-8zM3 13h8v8H3z"/></svg>'
        };
        const icon=asset&&window.ethvelleAssetEffectFrame?window.ethvelleAssetEffectFrame(asset):(svgs[type]||(window.__evSceneFxPack&&window.__evSceneFxPack.icon(type))||svgs.blur);
        strip.innerHTML=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>'<span class="ev-v248-effect-frame">'+(window.ethvelleCharFxLib&&window.ethvelleCharFxLib.has(type)?window.ethvelleCharFxLib.frameInner(type,i,icon):icon)+'</span>').join('');
        strip.dataset.signature=signature;
      }
      body.querySelectorAll('.ev-v246-effect-label,.anim-clip-label').forEach(label=>label.remove());
    });
  }
  let raf=0;
  function schedule(){if(raf)return;raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{raf=0;syncFullBleedFrames();});}
  const base=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof base==='function'&&!base.__evv249FrameFill){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=base.apply(this,arguments);schedule();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv249FrameFill=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
  const timeline=document.getElementById('timelineScroll')||document.getElementById('timelineInner');
  if(timeline){timeline.addEventListener('scroll',schedule,{passive:true});timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',schedule,{passive:true});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',schedule,{once:true});else schedule();
  window.__ethvelleV249EffectFrames={sync:syncFullBleedFrames,schedule};
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v255-anim-clip-name-fit-css">
  /* v255  -  Animation clip names always stay visible, however small the clip gets.
     Stage 1: name fits            -> shown normally.
     Stage 2: slightly too small   -> name is cut with "..." (ellipsis).
     Stage 3: much smaller         -> first word on top, last word underneath, font shrinks to fit
                                      (e.g. "Idle sway" -> Idle / sway).
     Stage 4: tiny sliver          -> initials stacked, font keeps shrinking so something is
                                      always visible. A clip is never blank.
     Sizes are written by JS as --ev255-max (usable width) and --ev255-fs (font size). Until JS has
     measured, the label simply isn't width-limited, so it can never collapse to nothing. */
  #timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label{
    display:block!important;
    width:max-content!important;
    max-width:var(--ev255-max,none)!important;
    font-size:var(--ev255-fs,10.5px)!important;
    line-height:1.05!important;
    text-align:center!important;
    white-space:nowrap!important;
    overflow:hidden!important;
    text-overflow:ellipsis!important;
    visibility:visible!important;
    opacity:1!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label.ev255-stack{
    display:flex!important;flex-direction:column!important;
    align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;
    line-height:1!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label .ev255-line{
    display:block;max-width:100%;
    white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;
  }
  #timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label.ev255-tiny,
  #timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label.ev255-tiny .ev255-line{
    text-overflow:clip!important;
  }
</style>

<script id="ethvelle-v255-anim-clip-name-fit-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const BASE_FS=10.5;      /* normal one-line label size used everywhere else */
  const STACK_MAX_FS=9.5;  /* biggest size allowed for the two-line stacked label */
  const STACK_MIN_FS=4.5;  /* below this, words are replaced by initials */
  const MIN_FS=3.2;        /* absolute floor  -  keeps a sliver of the name visible */
  const SELECTOR='#timelineInner .anim-clip .anim-clip-label';
  const FALLBACK_NAME='Animation';

  let ctx=null,family='';
  const widths=new Map();
  function setFamily(label){
    try{const f=getComputedStyle(label).fontFamily;if(f&&f!==family){family=f;widths.clear();}}catch(_e){}
  }
  /* Width of `text` at BASE_FS; scaled linearly for other sizes. */
  function measure(text){
    let w=widths.get(text);
    if(w!==undefined)return w;
    if(!ctx){const c=document.createElement('canvas');ctx=c.getContext&&c.getContext('2d');}
    if(ctx){ctx.font='700 '+BASE_FS+'px '+(family||'sans-serif');w=ctx.measureText(text).width;}
    else w=text.length*BASE_FS*0.58;
    widths.set(text,w);
    return w;
  }
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));

  /* Pure decision logic  -  given the name and the clip's pixel width, choose how to draw it. */
  function plan(full,clipW){
    const gutter=clamp(clipW*0.2,1.5,26);        /* keeps clear of the trim grips on wide clips */
    const avail=Math.max(2,clipW-gutter*2);
    const words=full.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const wFull=measure(full)+1;

    if(wFull<=avail)return{mode:'single',fs:BASE_FS,lines:[full],avail};

    /* Stage 2: still room for a recognisable chunk -> cut with "..." */
    if(avail>=Math.max(wFull*0.62,28))return{mode:'ellipsis',fs:BASE_FS,lines:[full],avail};

    if(words.length>=2){
      /* Stage 3: first word above, last word below, font shrinks to fit */
      const a=words[0],b=words[words.length-1];
      const wMax=Math.max(measure(a),measure(b))+1;
      const fs=Math.min(STACK_MAX_FS,BASE_FS*avail/wMax);
      if(fs>=STACK_MIN_FS)return{mode:'stack',fs,lines:[a,b],avail};
      /* Stage 4: initials, still stacked */
      const ia=Array.from(a)[0]||'',ib=Array.from(b)[0]||'';
      const wI=Math.max(measure(ia),measure(ib))+1;
      return{mode:'stack-tiny',fs:clamp(BASE_FS*avail/wI,MIN_FS,9),lines:[ia,ib],avail};
    }
    /* One-word name: shrink the font to fit, then fall back to its first letter */
    const fs=BASE_FS*avail/wFull;
    if(fs>=STACK_MIN_FS)return{mode:'shrink',fs,lines:[full],avail};
    const i=Array.from(full)[0]||'';
    return{mode:'single-tiny',fs:clamp(BASE_FS*avail/(measure(i)+1),MIN_FS,9),lines:[i],avail};
  }

  function apply(label){
    const clip=label.closest('.anim-clip');if(!clip)return;
    const w=clip.offsetWidth||parseFloat(clip.style.width)||0;
    if(w<=0)return;
    setFamily(label);

    /* Work out the real (full) name, even if this label was already rewritten by us. */
    const mine=label.dataset.ev255Shown;
    let full;
    if(mine!==undefined&&label.textContent===mine)full=label.dataset.ev255Full||'';
    else full=(label.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    if(!full)full=FALLBACK_NAME;                    /* never blank */

    const p=plan(full,w);
    const sig=p.mode+'|'+p.fs.toFixed(1)+'|'+Math.round(p.avail)+'|'+p.lines.join('/')+'|'+full;
    if(label.dataset.ev255Sig===sig)return;

    label.textContent='';
    if(p.mode==='stack'||p.mode==='stack-tiny'){
      p.lines.forEach(t=>{const s=document.createElement('span');s.className='ev255-line';s.textContent=t;label.appendChild(s);});
    }else label.textContent=p.lines[0];

    label.classList.toggle('ev255-stack',p.mode==='stack'||p.mode==='stack-tiny');
    label.classList.toggle('ev255-tiny',p.mode==='stack-tiny'||p.mode==='single-tiny');
    label.style.setProperty('--ev255-max',p.avail.toFixed(1)+'px');
    label.style.setProperty('--ev255-fs',p.fs.toFixed(2)+'px');
    label.dataset.ev255Mode=p.mode;
    label.dataset.ev255Full=full;
    label.dataset.ev255Shown=label.textContent;
    label.dataset.ev255Sig=sig;
    label.setAttribute('aria-label',full);
  }

  let raf=0,busy=false,observer=null;
  function run(){
    raf=0;busy=true;
    try{document.querySelectorAll(SELECTOR).forEach(apply);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle clip name fit:',err);}
    busy=false;
    if(observer)observer.takeRecords();            /* ignore the edits we just made ourselves */
  }
  function schedule(){if(!raf&&!busy)raf=requestAnimationFrame(run);}

  function start(){
    const root=document.getElementById('timelineInner');
    if(!root){requestAnimationFrame(start);return;}
    /* childList: clips re-rendered. style attr: a clip being trimmed/zoomed changes its width live. */
    observer=new MutationObserver(schedule);
    observer.observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style','class']});
    window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule,{passive:true});
    const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll')||root;
    ['pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel','wheel','touchend'].forEach(ev=>scroller.addEventListener(ev,schedule,{passive:true}));
    if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.ready)document.fonts.ready.then(()=>{widths.clear();document.querySelectorAll(SELECTOR).forEach(l=>{delete l.dataset.ev255Sig;});schedule();});
    schedule();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
  window.__ethvelleV255ClipNames={plan,run,schedule};
})();
</script>

  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories.ev-sheet-type-switch{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}   /* v344: 4 tabs (Humanoid, Beast Men, Animals, Birds) */
  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories .ev-sheet-type-btn{
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:7px;min-width:0;min-height:44px;padding:0 8px;
    border:1px solid #3C3F4D;border-radius:12px;background:#24262F;color:#D5D9E6;
    font:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.01em;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;
    box-shadow:none;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent;transition:background .16s,border-color .16s,color .16s,transform .12s;
  }
  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories .ev-sheet-type-btn svg{width:18px;height:18px;flex:0 0 18px;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.9;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round;}
  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories .ev-sheet-type-btn span{min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}
  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories .ev-sheet-type-btn:active{transform:scale(.97);}
  #screen-editor .evca-animation-categories .ev-sheet-type-btn.active{
    border-color:#527BFA;background:linear-gradient(165deg,#416FF4,#244BC5);color:#fff;box-shadow:none;
  }

  /* ===== Slightly thicker green borders: uploaded sheet block + copied tick button ===== */
  #autoRigUploadBackdrop .two-sheet-upload-area.ready{border-width:2px;border-style:solid;border-color:rgba(52,211,153,.65);}
  #autoRigUploadBackdrop .ev-sheet-copy-sq.copied{border-width:1.5px;border-color:rgba(52,211,153,.65);}

  /* ===== Animal / bird animation lists: no gray note or "matching model" label ===== */
  #screen-editor .evca-anim-type-note,#screen-editor .evca-anim-match-label,#screen-editor .evca-mouth-item-help{display:none!important;}

  /* ===== Model editor: "Model name" title above the field, evenly padded ===== */
  #sheetEd .ed-model-name-panel{
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch;gap:6px;margin:0;padding:4px 12px 12px;
  }
  #sheetEd .ed-model-name-panel label{
    display:block;margin:0;padding:0 2px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.01em;line-height:1.2;color:#D5D9E6;
  }
  #sheetEd .ed-model-name-panel input{
    display:block;width:100%;min-width:0;height:40px;padding:0 12px;border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;
  }

  /* ===== Animation search: canvas keeps its size while the keyboard is open ===== */
  body.ev-kb-lock #screen-editor .canvas-wrap{
    flex:0 0 auto!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;
    height:var(--ev-kb-wrap-h)!important;min-height:var(--ev-kb-wrap-h)!important;max-height:var(--ev-kb-wrap-h)!important;
  }
</style>


<!-- Ethvelle v262: instant directional same-row clip seek, committed from the real selection path. -->
<!-- Ethvelle v263: same-lane directional clip seek; synchronous with actual clip selection. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v264-directional-clip-seek">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV264DirectionalClipSeek)return;
  window.__ethvelleV264DirectionalClipSeek=true;
  const CLIPS='#timelineInner .anim-clip,#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id],#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id]';
  const ROWS='.overlay-track-row,#backgroundRow,.char-row';
  // v283: the clip trim handles (edges) are NOT skipped any more - a quick tap on an edge seeks exactly
  // like a tap on the clip body. A long-press / drag on the handle (trim) still cancels the seek
  // because it moves > 6px, lasts > 300ms or sets the clip's 'dragging' class.
  const SKIP='.kf-marker,.sel-edge-left,.sel-edge-right,.sel-edge-top,.sel-edge-bottom';
  let intent=null,timer=0,dragObserver=null,lastTap=null,lastSeek=null;
  const now=()=>Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
  // v282 FIX ("selecting a clip sometimes doesn't move the scrubber"): a tap used to be timed with
  // performance.now() inside the handlers. Selecting a clip on a heavy project can block the main
  // thread for 200+ ms (the selection runs in the same pointerdown), so a quick tap reached
  // pointerup "late", looked like a long press (>220 ms) and the seek was silently skipped. The
  // contact time now comes from the INPUT timestamps (when the finger touched / left the glass),
  // which do not change when the page is busy.
  const TAP_MAX_MS=300;
  function evTime(e){
    const ts=Number(e&&e.timeStamp),n=performance.now();
    return (Number.isFinite(ts)&&ts>0&&Math.abs(n-ts)<10000)?ts:n;
  }
  function rowOf(el){return el&&el.closest?el.closest(ROWS):null;}
  function laneKey(el){
    const row=rowOf(el);if(!row)return null;
    return {row,top:parseFloat(el.style&&el.style.top)||0};
  }
  function refOf(el){
    if(!el)return '';
    if(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null)return String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef);
    if(el.id==='animClipEl')return 'main';
    if(el.dataset.segIdx!=null)return 'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx;
    if(el.dataset.overlayId!=null)return String(el.dataset.overlayId);
    if(el.dataset.bgId!=null)return String(el.dataset.bgId);
    return '';
  }
  function elementFor(ref){
    if(ref==null)return null;
    const key=String(ref);
    if(key==='main')return document.getElementById('animClipEl');
    if(/^seg\d+$/.test(key))return document.getElementById(key+'El');
    const safe=key.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"');
    return document.querySelector('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id="'+safe+'"],#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id="'+safe+'"],#timelineInner .anim-clip[data-readonly-clip-ref="'+safe+'"]');
  }
  function modelFor(ref,el){
    try{
      if(el&&el.dataset.readonlyCharIdx!=null){
        const idx=Number(el.dataset.readonlyCharIdx),c=Array.isArray(characters)?characters[idx]:null;
        const all=c&&typeof getAllStickmanClips==='function'?getAllStickmanClips(c.clip,c.segments||[]):[];
        const item=all.find(x=>String(x.ref)===String(ref));return item&&item.clip||null;
      }
      if(String(ref)==='main')return typeof activeClip!=='undefined'?activeClip:null;
      if(/^seg\d+$/.test(String(ref)))return (typeof splitStickmanSegments!=='undefined'?splitStickmanSegments:[])[Number(String(ref).slice(3))]||null;
      const ov=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(x=>x&&String(x.id)===String(ref));if(ov)return ov;
      return (Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>x&&String(x.id)===String(ref))||null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function rangeFor(ref,el){
    const m=modelFor(ref,el);let a=Number(m&&m.trimStart),b=Number(m&&m.trimEnd);
    if(!(Number.isFinite(a)&&Number.isFinite(b)&&b>a)){
      const pps=typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?Number(pxPerSecond()):90;
      const left=parseFloat(el&&el.style&&el.style.left),width=parseFloat(el&&el.style&&el.style.width);
      if(!(Number.isFinite(left)&&Number.isFinite(width)&&pps>0))return null;
      a=left/pps;b=(left+width)/pps;
    }
    return b>a?{start:a,end:b}:null;
  }
  function sameLane(a,b){
    if(!a||!b||!a.isConnected||!b.isConnected)return false;
    const ak=laneKey(a),bk=laneKey(b);
    return !!(ak&&bk&&ak.row===bk.row&&Math.abs(ak.top-bk.top)<=.5);
  }
  function allLaneClips(target){
    const key=laneKey(target);if(!key)return [];
    return Array.from(key.row.querySelectorAll(CLIPS)).filter(el=>{
      const other=laneKey(el);
      return !!(other&&other.row===key.row&&Math.abs(other.top-key.top)<=.5);
    });
  }
  function isFirstInLane(target){
    const key=laneKey(target),tr=target.getBoundingClientRect();
    return !allLaneClips(target).some(el=>{
      const other=laneKey(el);
      return el!==target&&!!(key&&other&&other.row===key.row&&Math.abs(other.top-key.top)<=.5)&&el.getBoundingClientRect().left<tr.left-1;
    });
  }
  function clear(){
    if(timer){clearTimeout(timer);timer=0;}
    if(dragObserver){dragObserver.disconnect();dragObserver=null;}
    intent=null;
  }
  function watchDragging(d){
    if(!d||!d.targetEl||!window.MutationObserver)return;
    dragObserver=new MutationObserver(function(){
      if(intent===d&&d.targetEl.classList.contains('dragging'))clear();
    });
    dragObserver.observe(d.targetEl,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  function commitSeek(d,direction){
    if(!d||d.done||!direction)return false;
    const r=d.targetRange;if(!r||!(r.end>r.start))return false;
    const fps=Math.max(1,Number(typeof FPS!=='undefined'?FPS:24)||24);
    const target=direction==='right'?r.start:Math.max(r.start,r.end-1/fps);
    d.done=true;
    lastSeek={ref:d.targetRef,from:now(),at:Date.now()};
    try{
      if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
      if(typeof setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')setCurrentAbsoluteTime(target);
      else if(typeof seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds==='function')seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(target);
      if(typeof movePlayhead==='function')movePlayhead();
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      return true;
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v264 directional clip seek:',err);return false;}
  }
  function directionFor(d){
    if(!d||!d.targetRange)return '';
    // v275/v276 RULE (playhead based, works for left/right neighbours AND up/down rows):
    //  - scrubber already inside / touching the selected clip  -> leave it where it is
    //  - selected clip lies BEFORE the scrubber (left, or above/below on the left) -> show that clip's END
    //  - selected clip lies AFTER  the scrubber (right, or above/below on the right) -> show that clip's START
    // v419: tapping the FIRST clip of any row only selects it. The scrubber stays exactly where it is.
    if(d.firstInLane)return '';
    const r=d.targetRange,t=now();
    const fps=Math.max(1,Number(typeof FPS!=='undefined'?FPS:24)||24),eps=0.4/fps;
    if(t<r.start-eps)return 'right';
    if(t>r.end+eps)return 'left';
    // v276: scrubber sitting on the shared edge of two touching clips. The LEFT clip must show
    // its END, the RIGHT clip its START (previously it counted as "touching" and never moved).
    const nearStart=Math.abs(t-r.start)<=eps,nearEnd=Math.abs(t-r.end)<=eps;
    if(nearEnd&&!nearStart)return 'left';
    if(nearStart)return 'right';
    // Playhead touches this clip. Re-tapping the already-selected FIRST clip still returns to its start.
    if(d.targetRef===d.previousRef&&d.firstInLane)return 'right';
    return '';
  }
  function finishIfReady(d){
    if(d&&intent===d&&d.doubleTap){clear();return;}
    if(!d||intent!==d||!d.pointerUp||!d.selectionSeen||d.moved)return;
    const dir=directionFor(d);
    if(dir)commitSeek(d,dir);
    clear();
  }
  function onDown(e){
    clear();
    if(e.button>0||!e.target||!e.target.closest||!e.target.closest('#timelineScroll')||e.target.closest(SKIP))return;
    const target=e.target.closest(CLIPS);if(!target||target.classList.contains('dragging'))return;
    const targetRef=refOf(target);if(!targetRef)return;
    const previousRef=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?String(selectedClip):'';
    const targetRow=rowOf(target),previousElement=elementFor(previousRef);
    const previousRow=rowOf(previousElement);
    let sameCharacterRow=false;
    if(/^char\d+$/.test(previousRef)&&targetRow&&targetRow.classList.contains('char-row')){
      sameCharacterRow=String(targetRow.dataset.charIdx)===previousRef.slice(4);
    }
    const same=sameLane(previousElement,target);
    const targetRect=target.getBoundingClientRect(),previousRect=previousElement&&previousElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const laneClips=allLaneClips(target);
    /* v418: a second quick tap on the same clip is a double-tap (edge-trim gesture). It must never move the
       scrubber, and a seek the first tap just made is undone so the scrubber stays exactly where it was. */
    const downMs=Date.now();
    const isDouble=!!(lastTap&&lastTap.ref===targetRef&&downMs-lastTap.upAt<560);
    if(isDouble){
      if(lastSeek&&lastSeek.ref===targetRef&&downMs-lastSeek.at<1500&&Number.isFinite(lastSeek.from)){
        try{
          if(typeof setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')setCurrentAbsoluteTime(lastSeek.from);
          else if(typeof seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds==='function')seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(lastSeek.from);
          if(typeof movePlayhead==='function')movePlayhead();
          if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
        }catch(_e){}
      }
      lastTap=null;lastSeek=null;
    }
    intent={doubleTap:isDouble,
      targetRef,previousRef,targetEl:target,singleClipLane:laneClips.length===1,pointerId:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,downAt:evTime(e),maxMove:0,moved:false,pointerUp:false,selectionSeen:false,done:false,
      targetRange:rangeFor(targetRef,target),previousRange:previousElement?rangeFor(previousRef,previousElement):null,
      targetLeft:targetRect.left,previousLeft:previousRect?previousRect.left:NaN,
      sameLane:same,firstInLane:isFirstInLane(target),previousIsSameCharacterRow:sameCharacterRow
    };
    watchDragging(intent);
  }
  function onMove(e){
    const d=intent;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    if(d.targetEl&&d.targetEl.classList.contains('dragging')){clear();return;}
    const moved=Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.x,e.clientY-d.y);
    if(moved>d.maxMove)d.maxMove=moved;
    if(d.maxMove>6){d.moved=true;clear();}
  }
  function onUp(e){
    const d=intent;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    if(d.targetEl&&d.targetEl.classList.contains('dragging')){clear();return;}
    const finalMove=Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.x,e.clientY-d.y);
    if(finalMove>d.maxMove)d.maxMove=finalMove;
    if(!(evTime(e)-d.downAt<TAP_MAX_MS&&d.maxMove<=6)){d.moved=true;lastTap=null;clear();return;}
    d.pointerUp=true;
    lastTap=d.doubleTap?null:{ref:d.targetRef,upAt:Date.now()};
    d.selectionSeen=String((typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:'')===d.targetRef;
    finishIfReady(d);
    if(intent===d){
      if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);
      timer=setTimeout(function(){if(intent===d)clear();},400);
    }
  }
  function onCancel(e){if(intent&&intent.pointerId===e.pointerId)clear();}
  // Events the v283 early forwarder already handled (edge/handle taps) are skipped here.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){if(!e.__evV283Forwarded)onDown(e);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){if(!e.__evV283Forwarded)onMove(e);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',function(e){if(!e.__evV283Forwarded)onUp(e);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(e){if(!e.__evV283Forwarded)onCancel(e);},true);
  window.__ethvelleV283HandleSeek={pointerdown:onDown,pointermove:onMove,pointerup:onUp,pointercancel:onCancel};
  const base=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof base==='function'&&!base.__ethvelleV264DirectionalSeek){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const d=intent;
      const result=base.apply(this,arguments);
      if(d&&intent===d&&!d.moved&&!(d.targetEl&&d.targetEl.classList.contains('dragging'))&&String(which)===d.targetRef){
        d.selectionSeen=String((typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:'')===d.targetRef;
        finishIfReady(d);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV264DirectionalSeek=true;
    window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
    try{selectClipForChrome=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v265: preserve v140/v165 routing and reinforce only the native-video audio route. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v265-video-audio-route-recovery">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV265VideoAudioRouteRecovery)return;
  window.__ethvelleV265VideoAudioRouteRecovery=true;
  // Keep the established v165 scheduler as the authority: it decides whether sound should
  // come from the video's own decoder or from a separate scheduled audio buffer.
  const originalRamp=window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible;
  if(typeof originalRamp!=='function')return;
  function byId(id){
    return (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(function(o){return o&&o.type==='video'&&String(o.id)===String(id);})||null;
  }
  function liveFor(ov,el){
    const id=String((ov&&ov.id)||(el&&el.dataset&&el.dataset.evV72OverlayId)||'');
    return id?byId(id):null;
  }
  function timelineTime(){
    try{return Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;}
  }
  function isPreferred(ov,t){
    // v279: every active forward-playing video layer is audible (overlaps are heard doubled).
    return !!(ov&&ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0));
  }
  function nativeVideoIsReady(el,ov,t){
    const nativeIds=window.__ethvelleNativeLongVideoAudioIds;
    return !!(ov&&nativeIds&&typeof nativeIds.has==='function'&&nativeIds.has(String(ov.id))&&
      isPlaying&&!ov.reversePlayback&&el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.isConnected&&el.readyState>=2&&
      !el.paused&&!el.seeking&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0)&&isPreferred(ov,t));
  }
  function applyNativeVideoGain(el,ov,t){
    if(!nativeVideoIsReady(el,ov,t))return false;
    let volume=ov.volume==null?1:Number(ov.volume);
    try{
      if(typeof ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'){
        const animated=ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'volume',t);
        if(animated!=null)volume=Number(animated);
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    if(!Number.isFinite(volume))volume=1;
    volume=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,volume));
    try{
      const nodes=el.__evV140AudioNodes||null;
      if(nodes&&nodes.gain&&nodes.ctx){
        if(nodes.ctx.state==='suspended')nodes.ctx.resume().catch(function(){});
        const param=nodes.gain.gain,now=nodes.ctx.currentTime;
        param.cancelScheduledValues(now);
        param.setTargetAtTime(volume,now,.015);
        el.volume=1;
      }else{
        // Native fallback for a decoder whose Web Audio gain graph could not be created.
        el.volume=volume;
      }
      el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');
      el.dataset.evV265NativeAudioActive='1';
      return true;
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('Ethvelle native video audio recovery:',err);
      return false;
    }
  }
  function ramp(el,ov){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return originalRamp.apply(this,arguments);
    const live=liveFor(ov,el)||ov;
    let result=false;
    try{result=originalRamp.call(this,el,live);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle video audio route:',err);}
    // The original ramp owns overlap and scheduled-buffer routing. This small final gain
    // check only applies when the canonical scheduler explicitly selected native video audio.
    if(!live)return result;
    const forced=applyNativeVideoGain(el,live,timelineTime());
    return forced||result;
  }
  ramp.__ethvelleV265AudioRoute=true;
  window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible=ramp;
  try{evRampOverlayVideoAudible=ramp;}catch(_e){}

  function bind(el){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||el.dataset.evV265AudioBound==='1')return;
    el.dataset.evV265AudioBound='1';
    function refresh(event){
      const ov=liveFor(null,el);
      if(!ov)return;
      if(event&&event.type==='playing'&&!el.paused)el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen='1';
      if(isPlaying&&!el.paused&&!el.seeking)ramp(el,ov);
    }
    ['playing','timeupdate','seeked','canplay','loadeddata'].forEach(function(name){el.addEventListener(name,refresh,{passive:true});});
  }
  const previousSetup=window.setupOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof previousSetup==='function'&&!previousSetup.__ethvelleV265AudioRoute){
    const setup=function(el,ov){const result=previousSetup.apply(this,arguments);bind(result||el);return result;};
    setup.__ethvelleV265AudioRoute=true;
    window.setupOverlayVideoEl=setup;
    try{setupOverlayVideoEl=setup;}catch(_e){}
  }
  Object.keys(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls:{}).forEach(function(id){bind(overlayMediaEls[id]);});

  // Resume the same AudioContext synchronously in the trusted Play gesture; don't replace
  // the existing V140 gesture-prime or V165 sample scheduler.
  const previousStart=window.startPlayback;
  if(typeof previousStart==='function'&&!previousStart.__ethvelleV265AudioRoute){
    const start=function(){
      try{
        const ctx=typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'?ensureAudioOverlayContext():null;
        if(ctx&&ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume().catch(function(){});
      }catch(_e){}
      return previousStart.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    start.__ethvelleV265AudioRoute=true;
    window.startPlayback=start;
    try{startPlayback=start;}catch(_e){}
  }
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v264: full rendered animal/bird artwork bounds for canvas selection. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v264-species-selection-bounds">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds)return;
  window.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds=true;
  function isSpeciesSheet(c){
    const type=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'');
    return !!(c&&c.sheet&&c.sheet.parts&&c.sheet.draw&&Array.isArray(c.sheet.order)&&(type==='animal'||type==='bird'));
  }
  function speciesArtBounds(c){
    if(!isSpeciesSheet(c)||typeof sheetRigForCharacter!=='function'||typeof sheetSolveRig!=='function'||typeof SHEET_BONES==='undefined')return null;
    try{
      const sh=c.sheet,rig=sheetRigForCharacter(c),restBones={};
      Object.keys(rig.bones||{}).forEach(key=>{
        const b=rig.bones[key];
        restBones[key]={...b,off:b.off?{...b.off}:undefined,angle:Number.isFinite(rig.baseAngles&&rig.baseAngles[key])?rig.baseAngles[key]:Number(b.angle)||0};
      });
      const S=sheetSolveRig(restBones,rig.baseAngles||{});
      let torsoLength=0;
      ['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck'].forEach(key=>{if(restBones[key])torsoLength+=Number(restBones[key].length)||0;});
      const k=torsoLength/Math.max(1e-6,Number(sh.refLen)||1);
      if(!(k>0))return null;
      let minX=Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
      const add=(x,y)=>{if(Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)){minX=Math.min(minX,x);minY=Math.min(minY,y);maxX=Math.max(maxX,x);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y);}};
      const order=Array.isArray(sh.order)?sh.order:[];
      order.forEach(key=>{
        if((typeof SHEET_ALT_OF!=='undefined'&&SHEET_ALT_OF[key])||(typeof SHEET_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(key))||(String(c.modelType||'')==='animal'&&typeof SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(key))||(String(c.modelType||'')==='humanoid'&&typeof SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS!=='undefined'&&SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.includes(key))||(String(c.modelType||'')==='bird'&&typeof SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(key)))return;
        if((key==='eye_L'||key==='eye_R')&&c.expr&&(c.expr.blink||c.expr.ambientBlink||c.expr.eyeState==='closed'))return;
        let dynamic=null;
        if(key==='head'&&typeof sheetRuntimeHeadVariant==='function')dynamic=sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh,c.expr);
        if((key==='handL'||key==='handR')&&typeof sheetRuntimeHandVariant==='function')dynamic=sheetRuntimeHandVariant(sh,c.expr,key==='handL'?'L':'R');
        const useKey=dynamic?key:(typeof sheetActiveKey==='function'?sheetActiveKey(key,c.expr,sh.parts):key);
        const d=(sh.draw||{})[useKey]||(sh.draw||{})[key];
        const part=dynamic?sh.parts[key]:sh.parts[useKey];
        let sb=SHEET_BONES[useKey];
        if(useKey==='legL_lower'&&sh.parts.shoeL)sb=['shinL','shinL'];
        if(useKey==='legR_lower'&&sh.parts.shoeR)sb=['shinR','shinR'];
        if(!d||!part||!sb||!S[sb[0]]||!S[sb[1]])return;
        const a=S[sb[0]],b=S[sb[1]],dx=b.e.x-a.s.x,dy=b.e.y-a.s.y;
        const angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Number(d.ra||0)+Number(d.rot||0),cs=Math.cos(angle),sn=Math.sin(angle);
        const mirror=dynamic&&key==='handL'&&dynamic.side!=='L'?-1:1;
        const sx=k*(Number.isFinite(Number(d.sx))?Number(d.sx):1)*mirror;
        const sy=k*(Number.isFinite(Number(d.sy))?Number(d.sy):1);
        const w=Math.max(0,Number(part.w)||0),h=Math.max(0,Number(part.h)||0);
        const px=Number(d.pvx)||0,py=Number(d.pvy)||0;
        if(!(w>0&&h>0))return;
        [[-px,-py],[-px+w,-py],[-px+w,-py+h],[-px,-py+h]].forEach(([lx,ly])=>{
          const x=lx*sx,y=ly*sy;
          add(a.s.x+x*cs-y*sn,a.s.y+x*sn+y*cs);
        });
      });
      if(!Number.isFinite(minX))return null;
      // Keep the established finger-sized drag ring while extending the underlying frame
      // to every sheet part (especially the separately articulated head and tail).
      const pad=(Number(typeof SB_JOINT_HIT_R!=='undefined'?SB_JOINT_HIT_R:26)||26)+16;
      return {minX:minX-pad,minY:minY-pad,maxX:maxX+pad,maxY:maxY+pad};
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v264 species artwork bounds:',err);return null;}
  }
  const oldRest=window.characterRestBounds;
  if(typeof oldRest==='function'&&!oldRest.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const c=typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;
      if(isSpeciesSheet(c)){const b=speciesArtBounds(c);if(b)return b;}
      return oldRest.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds=true;
    window.characterRestBounds=wrapped;
    try{characterRestBounds=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const oldPick=window.charPickGeom;
  if(typeof oldPick==='function'&&!oldPick.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds){
    const wrappedPick=function(c){
      if(isSpeciesSheet(c)){
        const bb=speciesArtBounds(c);
        if(bb&&c.bones&&c.bones.hip&&c.modelTransform){
          const trim=(Number(typeof SB_JOINT_HIT_R!=='undefined'?SB_JOINT_HIT_R:26)||26)+16-14;
          const minX=bb.minX+trim,minY=bb.minY+trim,maxX=bb.maxX-trim,maxY=bb.maxY-trim;
          return selBoxGeom({obj:c.modelTransform,cB:{x:(minX+maxX)/2,y:(minY+maxY)/2},P0:{x:c.bones.hip.x,y:c.bones.hip.y},w0:Math.max(maxX-minX,20),h0:Math.max(maxY-minY,20)});
        }
      }
      return oldPick.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedPick.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBounds=true;
    window.charPickGeom=wrappedPick;
    try{charPickGeom=wrappedPick;}catch(_e){}
  }
  window.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBoundsApi={measure:speciesArtBounds};
  try{if(typeof invalidateCharacterRestBoundsCache==='function')invalidateCharacterRestBoundsCache();}catch(_e){}
  try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')requestAnimationFrame(()=>updateSelBox());}catch(_e){}
})();
</script>
