<script id="ethvelle-v271-logo-edge-smoother">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Fixes the jagged / "cracked" pixel edges on the logo:
     - the big hosted PNG is re-scaled once in the browser with step-down, high-quality smoothing
       (browsers show stair-stepped edges when a huge image is shrunk to a small box in one go);
     - if the PNG has a near-black opaque background, its faint noise and hard rectangle edge/corners
       are levelled to pure black and feathered, so nothing shows at the image border.
     The logo stays externally hosted. If the browser blocks the pixel read (CORS), nothing changes. */
  var SRC='https://i.ibb.co.com/pBy7qxjG/file-0000000097ac8211b2cf9825ab2e845f.png';
  var TARGETS=[
    ['#splashLogoImg',140,140],
    ['.home-brand-logo-only',180,48],
    ['.admin-login-logo',118,94],
    ['.ev-login-consent-mark img',82,62],
    ['img.brand-logo',26,26]
  ];
  var TUTORIAL=['#tutorialGrid .tutorial-meta-icon img',34,34];
  var base=null,cache={};

  function downscale(img,tw,th){
    var src=img,w=img.naturalWidth,h=img.naturalHeight;
    while(w/2>=tw&&h/2>=th){
      w=Math.max(tw,Math.round(w/2));h=Math.max(th,Math.round(h/2));
      var c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;
      var x=c.getContext('2d');x.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;x.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
      x.drawImage(src,0,0,w,h);src=c;
    }
    var out=document.createElement('canvas');out.width=tw;out.height=th;
    var ox=out.getContext('2d');ox.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ox.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
    ox.drawImage(src,0,0,tw,th);
    return out;
  }

  /* Only touches images that have an opaque, near-black border. Transparent PNGs are left alone. */
  function cleanEdges(cv){
    try{
      var w=cv.width,h=cv.height;if(w<16||h<16)return;
      var cx=cv.getContext('2d'),id=cx.getImageData(0,0,w,h),p=id.data;
      var ring=Math.max(2,Math.round(Math.min(w,h)*0.03));
      var hist=[new Uint32Array(256),new Uint32Array(256),new Uint32Array(256)],n=0,clear=false;
      function scan(x0,y0,x1,y1){
        for(var yy=y0;yy<y1&&!clear;yy++)for(var xx=x0;xx<x1;xx++){
          var o=(yy*w+xx)*4;
          if(p[o+3]<245){clear=true;return;}
          hist[0][p[o]]++;hist[1][p[o+1]]++;hist[2][p[o+2]]++;n++;
        }
      }
      scan(0,0,w,ring);scan(0,h-ring,w,h);scan(0,ring,ring,h-ring);scan(w-ring,ring,w,h-ring);
      if(clear||!n)return;
      var fl=[0,0,0],c,v,acc;
      for(c=0;c<3;c++){
        acc=0;
        for(v=0;v<256;v++){acc+=hist[c][v];if(acc>=n*0.95){fl[c]=v;break;}}
        if(fl[c]>48)return;               // border isn't near-black: leave the image untouched
      }
      var inv=[255/(255-fl[0]),255/(255-fl[1]),255/(255-fl[2])],x,y,o,e,t,val;
      for(y=0;y<h;y++){
        var ey=Math.min(y,h-1-y);
        for(x=0;x<w;x++){
          e=Math.min(ey,x,w-1-x);
          t=e>=ring?1:e/ring;t=t*t*(3-2*t);
          o=(y*w+x)*4;
          for(c=0;c<3;c++){val=(p[o+c]-fl[c])*inv[c];p[o+c]=val<0?0:val*t;}
        }
      }
      cx.putImageData(id,0,0);
    }catch(_e){}
  }

  function toURL(cv){
    return new Promise(function(res){
      try{
        if(cv.toBlob)cv.toBlob(function(b){res(b?URL.createObjectURL(b):cv.toDataURL('image/png'));},'image/png');
        else res(cv.toDataURL('image/png'));
      }catch(_e){res(null);}
    });
  }

  function variant(bw,bh){
    var dpr=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,3),k=bw+'x'+bh+'@'+dpr;
    if(cache[k])return cache[k];
    var nw=base.naturalWidth,nh=base.naturalHeight;
    var s=Math.min(bw*dpr*2/nw,bh*dpr*2/nh,1);
    var cv=downscale(base,Math.max(1,Math.round(nw*s)),Math.max(1,Math.round(nh*s)));
    cleanEdges(cv);
    return (cache[k]=toURL(cv));
  }

  function apply(el,bw,bh){
    if(!el||el.__evSharp||(el.getAttribute('src')||'')!==SRC)return;
    variant(bw,bh).then(function(url){
      if(!url||(el.getAttribute('src')||'')!==SRC)return;
      el.__evSharp=1;el.setAttribute('src',url);
      if(el.id==='splashLogoImg'){
        var sp=document.getElementById('splashLogo');
        if(sp)sp.style.setProperty('--splash-logo-src','url("'+url+'")');
      }
    });
  }

  function run(){
    TARGETS.forEach(function(t){
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(t[0]),function(el){apply(el,t[1],t[2]);});
    });
  }
  function runTutorial(){
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(TUTORIAL[0]),function(el){apply(el,TUTORIAL[1],TUTORIAL[2]);});
  }

  var im=new Image();
  im.crossOrigin='anonymous';im.decoding='async';
  im.onload=function(){
    if(!im.naturalWidth)return;
    base=im;run();runTutorial();
    var grid=document.getElementById('tutorialGrid');
    if(grid&&window.MutationObserver)new MutationObserver(runTutorial).observe(grid,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  };
  im.onerror=function(){};   // blocked or offline: keep the original logo exactly as it was
  im.src=SRC;
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v275: audio self-heal (stuck decode / suspended context / stuck element flags). -->
<script id="ethvelle-v275-audio-self-heal">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV275AudioHeal)return;
  window.__ethvelleV275AudioHeal=true;
  const seenLoading=new Map(), retries=new Map(), flagSince=new WeakMap();
  function ctx(){try{return typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'?ensureAudioOverlayContext():null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function resumeCtx(){
    const c=ctx();
    if(c&&c.state!=='running'&&c.state!=='closed'){try{c.resume().catch(()=>{});}catch(_e){}}
  }
  // 1) The audio engine caches a decode result per source FOREVER. One slow/failed decode (or a
  //    promise that never settles) therefore left that clip silent until reload. Re-decode it here.
  function redecode(key,entry){
    const c=ctx();
    if(!c||typeof fetch!=='function'||typeof c.decodeAudioData!=='function')return;
    entry.__evHealing=true;
    fetch(key,{mode:'cors',credentials:'same-origin'}).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status);return r.arrayBuffer();})
      .then(bytes=>c.decodeAudioData(bytes))
      .then(buf=>{
        if(!buf||!buf.length)throw new Error('empty');
        entry.buffer=buf;entry.error=null;entry.status='ready';
      })
      .catch(()=>{entry.status='failed';})
      .finally(()=>{entry.__evHealing=false;});
  }
  function healCache(){
    const cache=window.__ethvelleDecodedAudioCache;
    if(!(cache instanceof Map))return;
    const now=performance.now();
    cache.forEach((entry,key)=>{
      if(!entry||entry.__evHealing||entry.status==='ready')return;
      if(entry.status==='loading'){
        if(!seenLoading.has(key))seenLoading.set(key,now);
        if(now-seenLoading.get(key)>10000){seenLoading.delete(key);redecode(key,entry);}
        return;
      }
      seenLoading.delete(key);
      if(entry.status==='failed'&&!/too large/i.test(String(entry.error||''))){
        const n=retries.get(key)||0;
        if(n<3){retries.set(key,n+1);redecode(key,entry);}
      }
    });
  }
  // 2) Element-fallback flags that never clear block audio from ever starting again.
  function healElements(){
    if(typeof audioOverlayEls==='undefined'||!audioOverlayEls)return;
    const now=performance.now();
    Object.keys(audioOverlayEls).forEach(id=>{
      const el=audioOverlayEls[id];if(!el||!el.dataset)return;
      ['evAudioPlayPending','evAudioSyncing'].forEach(flag=>{
        if(el.dataset[flag]!=='1'){return;}
        let rec=flagSince.get(el);if(!rec){rec={};flagSince.set(el,rec);}
        if(!rec[flag])rec[flag]=now;
        if(now-rec[flag]>2500){delete el.dataset[flag];rec[flag]=0;}
      });
    });
  }
  // 3) Browsers suspend/interrupt the AudioContext (tab switch, call, power-save). Wake it on any use.
  ['pointerdown','touchend','keydown'].forEach(t=>document.addEventListener(t,resumeCtx,{capture:true,passive:true}));
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden)resumeCtx();});
  window.addEventListener('focus',resumeCtx);
  setInterval(function(){
    try{const pl=(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying);if(!pl)healCache();healElements();if(pl)resumeCtx();}catch(_e){}
  },1500);
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v276: Enhance recovery (muted / hidden / frozen enhanced video, lost selection). -->
<script id="ethvelle-v276-enhance-recovery">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV276EnhanceRecovery)return;
  window.__ethvelleV276EnhanceRecovery=true;
  const playing=()=>{try{return typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&!!isPlaying;}catch(_e){return false;}};
  const nowT=()=>{try{return Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;}};
  const ovById=id=>{try{return (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const elOf=id=>{try{return (typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[String(id)]:null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  function wake(){
    try{const c=typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'?ensureAudioOverlayContext():null;if(c&&c.state!=='running'&&c.state!=='closed')c.resume().catch(()=>{});}catch(_e){}
  }
  // After Enhance / Apply: the clip must stay selected (the panel says "select the video clip"
  // when selection is lost) and its video element must not stay muted/hidden from the priming pass.
  function afterEnhance(id){
    try{
      wake();
      const ov=id&&ovById(id);if(!ov)return;
      const enhancePanelOpen=!!document.getElementById('evltEnhancePanel');
      if(!playing()&&enhancePanelOpen){
        const cur=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?String(selectedClip):'';
        if(cur!==String(id)&&typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function'){
          window.selectClipForChrome(id);
          if(typeof window.openPanel==='function'){try{window.openPanel('evltEnhance');}catch(_e){}}
        }
      }
      const el=ov.type==='video'?elOf(id):null;
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.isConnected){
        if(el.paused){ // stale "was playing" markers would keep the engine from ever un-muting it
          ['evPlayingEventSeen','evGainRampActive','evGainRampDone','evStablePlayingSince'].forEach(k=>{delete el.dataset[k];});
        }
        if(el.style.visibility==='hidden'&&!el.dataset.evIncomingHidden&&!el.__evEnhPrimeInProgress&&el.dataset.evPoolFree!=='1'){
          const t=nowT();
          if(t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0))el.style.visibility='';
        }
      }
      try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v276 afterEnhance:',err);}
  }
  function wrapAsync(name){
    const orig=window[name];
    if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__ev276)return;
    const w=async function(){
      const lockedId=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?String(selectedClip):null;
      try{return await orig.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{[350,1400,3200,6500].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>afterEnhance(lockedId),ms));}
    };
    w.__ev276=true;window[name]=w;
  }
  wrapAsync('ethvelleStartVideoEnhance');
  wrapAsync('ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance');

  // v277 ROOT-CAUSE FIX for stutter + missing audio:
  // The native-video audio routine pauses ("evStopOverlayVideo") any video that is not yet
  // marked "playing event seen". It was called from canplay/loadeddata/timeupdate right after
  // play() but BEFORE the browser's 'playing' event, so a video that had just started got
  // paused again, restarted, paused ... (visible stutter) and ended up muted. Skip the routine
  // in that short window; the normal 'playing' event runs it a moment later.
  (function(){
    const prev=window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__ev277Guard)return;
    const guarded=function(el,ov){
      try{
        if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&playing()&&!el.paused&&el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen!=='1')return false;
      }catch(_e){}
      return prev.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    guarded.__ev277Guard=true;
    window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible=guarded;
    try{evRampOverlayVideoAudible=guarded;}catch(_e){}
  })();

  // Conservative self-heal: if a video is genuinely advancing but lost its "playing" marker (so the
  // engine would keep it muted), restore the marker. Touches nothing else (no play/pause/visibility).
  const samples=new WeakMap();
  setInterval(function(){
    try{
      if(!playing())return;
      if(typeof evVideoBufferStall!=='undefined'&&evVideoBufferStall)return;
      const t=nowT();
      (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
        if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||ov.reversePlayback)return;
        if(!(t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)+.3&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0)-.1))return;
        const el=elOf(ov.id);
        if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||!el.isConnected)return;
        const s=samples.get(el)||{ct:null,ok:0};
        const ct=Number(el.currentTime)||0;
        const advancing=s.ct!=null&&(ct-s.ct)>.15&&!el.paused&&!el.seeking&&el.readyState>=3;
        s.ok=advancing?s.ok+1:0;
        if(s.ok>=2&&el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen!=='1')el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen='1';
        s.ct=ct;samples.set(el,s);
      });
    }catch(_e){}
  },400);
})();
</script>
<!-- Ethvelle v281: with a clip selected, Play runs ONLY that clip once (then stops at its end);
     the clip keeps its selection border (timeline + canvas box) the whole time. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v281-selected-clip-play-once">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV281SelectedPlayOnce)return;
  window.__ethvelleV281SelectedPlayOnce=true;

  const EPS=1e-6;
  let armed=null;        // {id,start,end} while a selected-clip-only run is pending/active
  let keepSelection=0;   // >0 while startPlayback runs: its deselectAllClips() call is skipped

  function num(v,d){const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:d;}
  function fps(){return Math.max(1,num(typeof FPS!=='undefined'?FPS:30,30));}

  // Timeline range [start,end) of whatever is selected right now, or null when nothing
  // plain is selected (a selected keyframe keeps the old behaviour: Play clears it).
  function selectedRange(){
    try{
      if(selectedClip==null||selectedKfIdx!==null)return null;
      if(window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe)return null;
      const key=String(selectedClip);
      let models=[];
      if(key==='main'){
        if(activeClip)models=[activeClip];
      }else if(/^seg\d+$/.test(key)){
        const seg=Array.isArray(splitStickmanSegments)?splitStickmanSegments[Number(key.slice(3))]:null;
        if(seg)models=[seg];
      }else if(/^char\d+$/.test(key)){
        const ch=Array.isArray(characters)?characters[Number(key.slice(4))]:null;
        if(ch){
          if(ch.clip)models.push(ch.clip);
          if(Array.isArray(ch.segments))models=models.concat(ch.segments);
        }
      }else{
        const m=(Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds.find(b=>b&&String(b.id)===key):null)||
                (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===key):null);
        if(m)models=[m];
      }
      let start=Infinity,end=-Infinity;
      models.forEach(m=>{
        if(!m)return;
        const s=Number(m.trimStart),e=Number(m.trimEnd);
        if(!Number.isFinite(s)||!Number.isFinite(e))return;
        start=Math.min(start,s);end=Math.max(end,e);
      });
      if(!(end>start))return null;
      return {id:key,start:Math.max(0,start),end:end};
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }

  // v283 FIX ("playing a selected clip does not stop at the exact end edge"): stopPlaybackAtProjectEnd()
  // deliberately parks ONE FRAME (~33ms) before the end, which is right for the project's last frame
  // but left the scrubber visibly short of the clip's end edge. After the normal stop (media paused,
  // audio stopped, icon reset) the playhead is moved onto the end edge itself, inset by 0.5 ms so the
  // clip (active on [trimStart,trimEnd)) still draws its final frame instead of going blank.
  const END_INSET=0.0005;
  function settleOnClipEnd(end){
    try{
      const exact=Math.max(0,num(end,0)-END_INSET);
      setCurrentAbsoluteTime(exact);
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      if(typeof movePlayhead==='function')movePlayhead();
    }catch(_e){}
  }

  // User pressed Play: remember the selected clip's range and, unless the playhead is already
  // inside it (and not at its very end), put the playhead at the clip's start.
  function armForPlay(){
    const range=selectedRange();
    if(!range){armed=null;return;}
    armed=range;
    const now=num(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec,0);
    const tail=2/fps();
    const from=(now>=range.start-EPS&&now<range.end-tail)?now:range.start;
    if(Math.abs(from-now)>EPS){
      try{
        seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(from);
        if(typeof movePlayhead==='function')movePlayhead();
      }catch(_e){}
    }
  }

  function wrapGlobal(name,make){
    const prev=window[name];
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__ethvelleV281)return;
    const wrapped=make(prev);
    wrapped.__ethvelleV281=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
    try{
      if(name==='togglePlayback')togglePlayback=wrapped;
      else if(name==='startPlayback')startPlayback=wrapped;
      else if(name==='playbackTick')playbackTick=wrapped;
      else if(name==='deselectAllClips')deselectAllClips=wrapped;
    }catch(_e){}
  }

  // 1) Play button / any transport toggle: arm the selected-clip run (pause just disarms it).
  wrapGlobal('togglePlayback',function(prev){
    return function(){
      if(!isPlaying)armForPlay();else armed=null;
      return prev.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  });

  // 2) Starting playback normally clears the selection; skip that while a selected-clip run is
  //    armed so the clip keeps its border. The range is re-read here so a trim made before a
  //    resume is honoured, and a changed selection drops the run.
  wrapGlobal('startPlayback',function(prev){
    return function(){
      if(armed){
        const r=selectedRange();
        if(!r||r.id!==armed.id)armed=null;
        else{armed.start=r.start;armed.end=r.end;}
      }
      if(!armed||isPlaying)return prev.apply(this,arguments);
      keepSelection++;
      try{return prev.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{keepSelection--;}
    };
  });
  wrapGlobal('deselectAllClips',function(prev){
    return function(){
      if(keepSelection>0)return;
      return prev.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  });

  // 3) Stop exactly at the end of the selected clip (once, no loop, no spill into other clips).
  wrapGlobal('playbackTick',function(prev){
    return function(){
      if(armed&&isPlaying){
        const r=selectedRange();
        if(!r||r.id!==armed.id){armed=null;}
        else{
          armed.start=r.start;armed.end=r.end;
          const t=num(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec,0);
          if(t>=armed.end-EPS){
            const end=armed.end;
            armed=null;
            try{stopPlaybackAtProjectEnd(end);}
            catch(_e){try{pausePlayback();}catch(_e2){}}
            settleOnClipEnd(end);
            return;
          }
        }
      }
      return prev.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  });
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v284: clip bottom toolbar - fixed button order per clip type, and no stale-toolbar flash on selection. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v284-toolbar-order-css">
  /* Button order (flex `order`, so it holds no matter which script inserted or moved a button):
       media / audio / background : Delete, Done, Split, Forward, Backward, Duplicate, then the rest
       model (character) row       : Delete model, Delete, Done, Split, Forward, Backward, Duplicate, then the rest
       text                        : Text edit, Delete, Done, Split, Forward, Backward, Duplicate, then the rest */
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipTextEditTool{order:-70!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipDeleteTool{order:-60!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipDeleteCharacterTool{order:-65!important;}

  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipSplitTool{order:-40!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipForwardTool{order:-30!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip #clipBackwardTool{order:-20!important;}

  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip[data-ev-kind="background"] #clipBackwardTool{display:none!important;}
  /* While a different kind of clip is being selected the buttons stay hidden until every script
     that adjusts the toolbar has finished, so the previous clip's buttons are never painted. */
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip.ev-v284-switching > .tool-item{visibility:hidden!important;transition:none!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v284-toolbar-order-and-switch">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV284Toolbar)return;
  window.__ethvelleV284Toolbar=true;
  const row=()=>document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');
  const SWITCH='ev-v284-switching';
  function overlayFor(key){
    try{return (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===key)||null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function kindFor(which){
    if(which==null||which==='')return '';
    const key=String(which);
    if(key==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(key)||/^char\d+$/.test(key))return 'model';
    const ov=overlayFor(key);
    if(ov)return ov.type==='text'?'text':(ov.type==='audio'?'audio':'media');
    try{if((Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).some(b=>b&&String(b.id)===key))return 'background';}catch(_e){}
    return 'media';
  }
  // Two clips with the same signature get the same buttons, so switching between them needs no mask.
  function signatureFor(which){
    const kind=kindFor(which);
    const ov=overlayFor(String(which));
    return kind+':'+(ov?String(ov.type||''):'');
  }
  function applyKind(which){
    const r=row();const kind=kindFor(which);
    if(r&&kind&&r.dataset.evKind!==kind)r.dataset.evKind=kind;
  }
  let lastSig='',token=0,observer=null,failsafe=0;
  function finish(my){
    if(my!==token)return;
    if(observer){observer.disconnect();observer=null;}
    if(failsafe){clearTimeout(failsafe);failsafe=0;}
    const r=row();if(r)r.classList.remove(SWITCH);
  }
  // Keep the buttons hidden until a whole animation frame passes with no further toolbar change.
  // Scripts that adjust the toolbar in requestAnimationFrame run before this callback in the same
  // frame, so when nothing else changes the buttons are shown again before that frame is painted.
  function beginMask(r){
    const my=++token;
    if(observer){observer.disconnect();observer=null;}
    if(failsafe){clearTimeout(failsafe);failsafe=0;}
    r.classList.add(SWITCH);
    let dirty=false;const t0=performance.now();
    if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
      observer=new MutationObserver(function(){dirty=true;});
      observer.observe(r,{attributes:true,childList:true,subtree:true,attributeFilter:['style','class']});
    }
    const step=function(){
      if(my!==token)return;
      if(dirty&&performance.now()-t0<240){dirty=false;requestAnimationFrame(step);return;}
      finish(my);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
    failsafe=setTimeout(function(){finish(my);},320);
  }
  const prevSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof prevSelect==='function'&&!prevSelect.__evV284){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const r=row();
      let changed=false;
      try{changed=String(which)!==String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){}
      if(r&&which!=null){
        applyKind(which);
        const sig=signatureFor(which);
        if(changed){
          // The first tools must be the visible ones, so never inherit the old scroll offset.
          r.scrollLeft=0;
          if(sig!==lastSig)beginMask(r);
        }
        lastSig=sig;
      }
      return prevSelect.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV284=true;
    window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
    try{selectClipForChrome=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  // Selection can also change without selectClipForChrome (it assigns selectedClip itself and
  // then re-syncs the toolbar), so keep the kind in step after every toolbar sync as well.
  const prevSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof prevSync==='function'&&!prevSync.__evV284){
    const wrappedSync=function(){
      const result=prevSync.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)applyKind(selectedClip);}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSync.__evV284=true;
    window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrappedSync;
    try{syncBottomToolsMode=wrappedSync;}catch(_e){}
  }
  try{if(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null){applyKind(selectedClip);lastSig=signatureFor(selectedClip);}}catch(_e){}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v290-ui-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Credit history search */
  window.filterCreditHistory=function(q){
    window.__evCreditQuery=String(q||'');
    const list=document.querySelector('.myplan-credit-history');if(!list)return;
    const query=window.__evCreditQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    let shown=0;
    list.querySelectorAll('.myplan-credit-row').forEach(function(row){
      const hit=!query||row.textContent.toLowerCase().indexOf(query)!==-1;
      row.style.display=hit?'':'none';if(hit)shown++;
    });
    let empty=list.querySelector('.ev-credit-nomatch');
    if(query&&!shown&&list.querySelector('.myplan-credit-row')){
      if(!empty){empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='myplan-credit-empty ev-credit-nomatch';list.appendChild(empty);}
      empty.textContent='No credit activity matches “'+window.__evCreditQuery.trim()+'”.';
    }else if(empty){empty.remove();}
  };
  /* Credit history: once the list hits its top/bottom, keep sliding the page. */
  function scrollParent(el){
    for(let p=el.parentElement;p;p=p.parentElement){
      const oy=getComputedStyle(p).overflowY;
      if((oy==='auto'||oy==='scroll')&&p.scrollHeight>p.clientHeight+1)return p;
    }
    return null;
  }
  let lastY=null,lastList=null;
  document.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
    const l=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.myplan-credit-history');
    lastList=l||null;lastY=l?e.touches[0].clientY:null;
  },{passive:true,capture:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
    if(!lastList||lastY==null)return;
    const y=e.touches[0].clientY,dy=y-lastY;lastY=y;
    if(lastList.scrollHeight<=lastList.clientHeight+1)return;
    const atTop=lastList.scrollTop<=0,atBottom=lastList.scrollTop+lastList.clientHeight>=lastList.scrollHeight-1;
    if((dy>0&&atTop)||(dy<0&&atBottom)){
      const p=scrollParent(lastList);
      if(p){p.scrollTop-=dy;if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();}
    }
  },{passive:false,capture:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchend',function(){lastList=null;lastY=null;},{passive:true,capture:true});

  /* Policy / Terms emails always show the email from Help & support. */
  function syncLegalEmails(){
    try{
      const email=(typeof getContactInfo==='function'&&getContactInfo().email)||'';
      if(!email)return;
      document.querySelectorAll('a.ev-support-email-link').forEach(function(a){
        if(a.textContent!==email)a.textContent=email;
        const h='mailto:'+email;if(a.getAttribute('href')!==h)a.setAttribute('href',h);
      });
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  window.syncLegalEmails=syncLegalEmails;
  const prevGo=window.goTo;
  if(typeof prevGo==='function'&&!prevGo.__evV290){
    const w=function(){const r=prevGo.apply(this,arguments);syncLegalEmails();return r;};
    w.__evV290=true;window.goTo=w;try{goTo=w;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const prevCards=window.renderHelpContactCards;
  if(typeof prevCards==='function'&&!prevCards.__evV290){
    const w=function(){const r=prevCards.apply(this,arguments);syncLegalEmails();return r;};
    w.__evV290=true;window.renderHelpContactCards=w;try{renderHelpContactCards=w;}catch(_e){}
  }
  [0,800,2500,6000].forEach(function(t){setTimeout(syncLegalEmails,t);});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v291-billing-plan-tracking">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV291Billing)return;window.__ethvelleV291Billing=true;
  function planOf(el){const card=el.closest('.myplan-available-plan');return card?{planId:card.dataset.planId||'',planName:card.dataset.planName||'',planPrice:card.dataset.planPrice||''}:null;}
  function isPro(d){
    if(String(d.planId).toLowerCase()==='pro'||String(d.planName).trim().toLowerCase()==='pro')return true;
    try{return !getPlans().some(p=>String(p.id).toLowerCase()==='pro'||String(p.name||'').trim().toLowerCase()==='pro');}catch(_e){return true;}
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const t=e.target;if(!t||!t.closest||!t.closest('#myPlanScroll'))return;
    const d=planOf(t);if(!d)return;
    try{
      if(t.closest('button.plan-cta')){if(isPro(d))trackEvent('billing_upgrade_to_pro_click',Object.assign({screen:'myplan'},d));}
      else trackEvent('billing_plan_click',Object.assign({screen:'myplan'},d));
    }catch(_e){}
  },true);
})();

<script id="ethvelle-v293-credits-plans-events">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const ms=v=>{try{return typeof creditTimestampMs==='function'?creditTimestampMs(v):(v&&v.toMillis?v.toMillis():(v?new Date(v).getTime():0));}catch(_e){return 0;}};
  window.openCreditsHistoryScreen=function(){goTo('credits-history');window.renderCreditsHistoryScreen();};
  function left(t){
    let d=t-Date.now();if(d<=0)return 'Expired';
    const D=Math.floor(d/864e5);d%=864e5;const H=Math.floor(d/36e5);d%=36e5;const M=Math.floor(d/6e4);const S=Math.floor((d%6e4)/1e3);
    return (D?D+'d ':'')+String(H).padStart(2,'0')+'h '+String(M).padStart(2,'0')+'m '+String(S).padStart(2,'0')+'s';
  }
  window.evActivePlansHtml=function(owned){
    const box=h=>'<div class="ev-plan-card"><div class="ev-plan-meta" style="margin:0">'+h+'</div></div>';
    if(!currentUser)return box('Sign in to see your active plans.');
    const paid=(owned||[]).filter(p=>Number(p.price)>0&&!['free','p_free'].includes(String(p.id)));
    if(!paid.length)return box('No active paid plan right now.');
    let pool=Math.max(0,Number(myCreditsBalance)||0);
    return paid.map(p=>{
      const st=(myPlanOrderStates&&myPlanOrderStates[String(p.id)])||{};
      const grant=Number(typeof getPlanCreditGrant==='function'?getPlanCreditGrant(p):0)||Number(st.creditsGrantAmount)||0;
      const start=ms(st.approvedAt)||ms(st.creditsGrantedAt)||ms(st.createdAt);
      const exp=ms(st.expiresAt)||(start?start+(Number(p.durationDays)||30)*864e5:0);
      const rem=grant>0?Math.min(pool,grant):pool;pool-=rem;
      const pct=grant>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(rem/grant*100))):0;
      const timer=exp?'<span class="ev-plan-timer" data-ev-exp="'+exp+'">'+left(exp)+'</span>':'';
      return '<div class="ev-plan-card"><div class="ev-plan-top"><span class="ev-plan-name">'+escapeHtml(p.name||'Plan')+'</span>'+timer+'</div>'
        +'<div class="ev-plan-meta"><span>Credits remaining</span><b>'+rem.toLocaleString()+(grant>0?' / '+grant.toLocaleString():'')+'</b></div>'
        +(grant>0?'<div class="ev-plan-bar"><i style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></div>':'')+'</div>';
    }).join('');
  };
  setInterval(function(){document.querySelectorAll('[data-ev-exp]').forEach(function(el){const t=Number(el.dataset.evExp);el.textContent=left(t);el.classList.toggle('expired',t<=Date.now());});},1000);
  // Events for the admin's Auto email & alerts engine (queued in Firestore `autoMessages`).
  window.evQueueAutoEvent=function(event,vars,user){
    try{
      const u=user||currentUser;if(!u||!(firebaseReady&&fbDb))return;
      const EVD={
        account_created:{subject:'Welcome to Ethvelle!',body:'Welcome to Ethvelle! Your account is ready. Start creating your first video today.'},
        order_placed:{subject:'Order received',body:'We received your plan order. We will verify the payment and activate your plan soon.'},
        plan_approved:{subject:'Your plan is active',body:'Your plan is now active. Enjoy all the features that come with it.'},
        plan_rejected:{subject:'Order could not be verified',body:'We could not verify your plan order. Please contact support or try again.'},
        credits_added:{subject:'Credits added',body:'Credits were added to your account. You can check your balance in your profile.'},
        credits_spent:{subject:'Credits used',body:'Credits were used from your balance.'}
      };
      const def=EVD[event];if(!def)return;
      const fill=function(t,v){return String(t||'').replace(/\{\{(\w+)\}\}/g,function(_,k){return v[k]==null?'':String(v[k]);});};
      const FV=firebase.firestore.FieldValue;
      // 1) read the settings saved in the admin panel (Auto email & alerts) -- the admin file does not need to be open
      fbDb.collection('siteContent').doc('autoMessaging').get().then(function(d){return d.exists?(d.data()||{}):{};}).catch(function(){return {};}).then(async function(cfg){
        if(cfg.enabled===false)return;
        const c=Object.assign({notif:true,email:true,subject:def.subject,body:def.body},(cfg.events||{})[event]||{});
        const v=Object.assign({name:u.displayName||(u.email||'').split('@')[0]||'there',date:new Date().toLocaleDateString()},vars||{});
        const subject=fill(c.subject,v),body=fill(c.body,v);
        const mode=cfg.emailMode||'off';
        const wantNotif=!!c.notif,wantEmail=!!(c.email&&u.email&&mode!=='off');
        let notifDone=!wantNotif,emailDone=!wantEmail;
        // 2) in-app notification straight to this user's inbox
        if(wantNotif){
          // Popup place comes from admin > Auto email & alerts (default: all screens). 'none' = inbox only.
          let showOn=c.showOn;showOn=Array.isArray(showOn)?showOn.join(','):String(showOn==null||showOn===''?'all':showOn);
          const nb={title:subject,body:body,target:String(u.uid),targetLabel:u.displayName||u.email||'One user',showOn:showOn,createdAt:FV.serverTimestamp()};
          try{
            try{await fbDb.collection('notifications').add(Object.assign({auto:true,event:event},nb));}
            catch(_e1){await fbDb.collection('notifications').add(nb);} // same fields as a normal admin notification
            notifDone=true;
          }catch(_e){console.warn('Ethvelle: auto notification could not be saved; queued for the admin panel',_e);}
        }
        // 3) email (Firebase Trigger Email extension or EmailJS), same as the admin engine
        if(wantEmail){
          try{
            if(mode==='extension'){
              const esc=function(x){return String(x).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(ch){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[ch];});};
              await fbDb.collection('mail').add({to:u.email,message:{subject:subject,text:body,html:'<div style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:15px;line-height:1.5">'+esc(body).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')+'<br><br>\u2014 Ethvelle</div>'}});emailDone=true;
            }else{
              const j=cfg.emailjs||{};
              if(j.serviceId&&j.templateId&&j.publicKey){
                const r=await fetch('https://api.emailjs.com/api/v1.0/email/send',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({service_id:j.serviceId,template_id:j.templateId,user_id:j.publicKey,template_params:{to_email:u.email,subject:subject,message:body}})});
                emailDone=!!r.ok;
              }
            }
          }catch(_e){}
        }
        // 4) log it; anything that could not be sent from here stays queued so the admin engine can finish it
        fbDb.collection('autoMessages').add({event:event,uid:u.uid,email:u.email||null,name:u.displayName||null,vars:vars||{},status:(notifDone&&emailDone)?'sent':'queued',notifDone:notifDone,emailDone:emailDone,createdAt:FV.serverTimestamp()}).catch(function(){});
      }).catch(function(){});
    }catch(_e){}
  };
  const prev=window.changeMyCreditBalance;
  if(typeof prev==='function'){
    const w=async function(delta,entry){
      const r=await prev.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(Number(delta)<0&&r&&r.id)window.evQueueAutoEvent('credits_spent',{credits:Math.abs(Math.trunc(Number(delta))),balance:r.balance,reason:(entry&&entry.description)||'Video export'});}catch(_e){}
      return r;
    };
    window.changeMyCreditBalance=w;try{changeMyCreditBalance=w;}catch(_e){}
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v295-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* ---- pencil on the text box corner ---- */
  const PENCIL='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L8 18l-4 1 1-4Z"/></svg>';
  const SCALE='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 4h6v6M10 20H4v-6M20 4l-7 7M4 20l7-7"/></svg>';
  function isTextSelected(){
    try{const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);return Array.isArray(overlays)&&overlays.some(o=>String(o.id)===id&&o.type==='text');}catch(_e){return false;}
  }
  function syncIcon(){
    const b=document.getElementById('sbScale');if(!b)return;
    const want='scale'; /* v307: same two-arrow resize/rotate icon for text and normal layers */
    const markup=want==='pencil'?PENCIL:SCALE;
    if(b.dataset.evIcon===want&&b.innerHTML===markup){
      b.title=want==='pencil'?'Tap to edit text, drag to resize / rotate':'Drag to resize / rotate';
      b.setAttribute('aria-label',b.title);return;
    }
    b.dataset.evIcon=want;b.innerHTML=markup;
    b.title=want==='pencil'?'Tap to edit text, drag to resize / rotate':'Drag to resize / rotate';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label',b.title);
  }
  window.__ethvelleSyncSelectionHandleIcon=syncIcon;
  syncIcon();
  setInterval(syncIcon,200); // retained only as a recovery fallback; selection wrappers update it synchronously
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',syncIcon,{capture:true,passive:true});
  let down=null;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    const b=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#sbScale');
    down=null; /* v307: tapping the resize handle no longer opens the text editor */
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',function(e){
    const d=down;down=null;if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.x,e.clientY-d.y)>8||performance.now()-d.t>650)return;
    if(!isTextSelected())return;
    try{if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextComposer==='function')window.openEthvelleTextComposer(selectedClip);}catch(_e){}
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(){down=null;},{capture:true,passive:true});

  /* ---- stroke layer: rebuild it in the same task as every text-layer render, before the browser can paint ---- */
  ['renderOverlayLayers','renderFrame'].forEach(function(name){
    const orig=window[name];
    if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__evV295)return;
    const w=function(){
      const r=orig.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(typeof window.ethvelleSyncStrokeLayers==='function')window.ethvelleSyncStrokeLayers();}catch(_e){}
      return r;
    };
    w.__evV295=true;
    window[name]=w;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v297-selection-shadow-composer-preview-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const PENCIL='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L8 18l-4 1 1-4Z"/></svg>';
  const SCALE='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 4h6v6M10 20H4v-6M20 4l-7 7M4 20l7-7"/></svg>';
  function selectedText(){
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
      return !!id&&Array.isArray(overlays)&&overlays.some(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&o.type==='text');
    }catch(_e){return false;}
  }
  function syncHandle(){
    const b=$('sbScale');if(!b)return;
    const want='scale'; /* v307 */
    const markup=want==='pencil'?PENCIL:SCALE;
    /* A few older selection paths recreate the scale SVG without clearing data-ev-icon.
       Compare the actual markup too, so a stale dataset can never leave the arrow visible. */
    if(b.dataset.evIcon!==want||b.innerHTML!==markup){b.dataset.evIcon=want;b.innerHTML=markup;}
    b.title=want==='pencil'?'Tap to edit text, drag to resize / rotate':'Drag to resize / rotate';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label',b.title);
  }
  function syncSelectedControls(){
    syncHandle();
    /* Selection can be assigned AFTER the original selection-box paint. Repair the text
       corners here, in the same call stack, rather than waiting for the v16 rAF fallback. */
    if(selectedText()&&!window.__evTextDragActive&&typeof window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners==='function'){
      try{window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners();}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function wrapSync(name){
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evV297Handle)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=original.apply(this,arguments);syncSelectedControls();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV297Handle=true;window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  }
  ['updateSelBox','selectClipForChrome','onClipSelected','selBoxApply','deselectAllClips'].forEach(wrapSync);
  syncSelectedControls();
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',syncSelectedControls,{capture:true,passive:true});

  let inputObserved=null,resizeObserver=null,previewBusy=false;
  function ensurePreview(input){
    let host=$('evtcStagePreview');
    if(!host){
      host=document.createElement('div');host.id='evtcStagePreview';host.className='ethvelle-overlay-text-content';
      host.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      if(input&&input.parentNode)input.parentNode.insertBefore(host,input);
      else{const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(root)root.appendChild(host);}
    }
    return host;
  }
  function clearInputMirror(input){
    if(!input)return;
    input.classList.remove('ev-v297-mirrored-text');
    input.style.removeProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color');
    input.style.removeProperty('-webkit-text-stroke');
    input.style.removeProperty('text-shadow');
    input.style.removeProperty('filter');
    input.style.removeProperty('text-decoration');
    input.style.removeProperty('z-index');
  }
  function syncComposerPreview(input,model){
    if(previewBusy)return;
    const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
    if(!input||!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const text=String(input.value||'');
    const host=ensurePreview(input);if(!host)return;
    if(!text){host.style.display='none';host.textContent='';clearInputMirror(input);return;}
    previewBusy=true;
    try{
      const rect=input.getBoundingClientRect(),cs=getComputedStyle(input),t=model||((typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'&&window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget())||{});
      host.style.display='block';
      host.style.left=rect.left+'px';host.style.top=rect.top+'px';host.style.width=rect.width+'px';host.style.height=rect.height+'px';
      host.style.position='fixed';host.style.zIndex='100003';host.style.boxSizing='border-box';host.style.margin='0';
      host.style.border='0';host.style.outline='0';host.style.padding=cs.padding;host.style.overflow='visible';
      host.style.whiteSpace=cs.whiteSpace||'pre-wrap';host.style.overflowWrap=cs.overflowWrap||'anywhere';
      host.style.textAlign=cs.textAlign||t.textAlign||'center';host.style.fontFamily=cs.fontFamily;
      host.style.fontSize=cs.fontSize;host.style.fontWeight=cs.fontWeight;host.style.fontStyle=cs.fontStyle;
      host.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba?window.ethvelleHexToRgba(t.textColor||'#FFFFFF',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textOpacity==null?100:t.textOpacity)))/100):cs.color;
      host.style.textTransform=t.textTransform||'none';host.style.pointerEvents='none';host.style.userSelect='none';
      const visual=Object.assign({},t,{textContent:text,textBackgroundEnabled:false});
      const oldGlyph=host.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      if(!oldGlyph||oldGlyph.textContent!==text)host.textContent=text;
      if(typeof window.ethvelleApplyTextEffectsToPreview==='function')window.ethvelleApplyTextEffectsToPreview(host,visual);
      // applyEffectsToNode owns glyph effects; restore the textarea's exact text-box geometry afterwards.
      host.style.position='fixed';host.style.left=rect.left+'px';host.style.top=rect.top+'px';
      host.style.width=rect.width+'px';host.style.height=rect.height+'px';host.style.boxSizing='border-box';
      host.style.padding=cs.padding;host.style.margin='0';host.style.textAlign=cs.textAlign||'center';
      host.style.fontFamily=cs.fontFamily;host.style.fontSize=cs.fontSize;host.style.fontWeight=cs.fontWeight;host.style.fontStyle=cs.fontStyle;
      host.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba?window.ethvelleHexToRgba(t.textColor||'#FFFFFF',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textOpacity==null?100:t.textOpacity)))/100):cs.color;
      host.style.textTransform=t.textTransform||'none';host.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textOverallOpacity==null?100:t.textOverallOpacity)))/100);
      host.style.zIndex='100003';host.style.display='block';
      // The backplate is already a separate composer layer; only the independent Highlight effect belongs here.
      if(t.textBackgroundEnabled)host.classList.remove('ev-text-highlight-plate');
      /* Bend/curve turns the glyph into one transformed span per character. In Chromium,
         background-clip:text and -webkit-text-stroke on the outer span do not paint those
         nested characters. Mirror the fill on each character, then rebuild the stroke clone
         from that final character markup and apply its stroke to each copied character. */
      const previewGlyph=host.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      if(previewGlyph&&previewGlyph.children&&previewGlyph.children.length){
        const grad=String(t.textGradient||''),texture=String(t.textTexture||'');
        const fill=grad||texture;
        const glyphRect=previewGlyph.getBoundingClientRect();
        const fullSize=Math.max(1,glyphRect.width)+'px '+Math.max(1,glyphRect.height)+'px';
        Array.from(previewGlyph.children).forEach(function(ch){
          if(fill){
            ch.style.backgroundImage=fill;
            ch.style.backgroundClip='text';ch.style.webkitBackgroundClip='text';
            ch.style.webkitTextFillColor='transparent';ch.style.color='transparent';
            ch.style.backgroundRepeat='no-repeat';
            ch.style.backgroundSize=grad?fullSize:String(t.textTextureSize||'100% 100%');
            if(grad){const cr=ch.getBoundingClientRect();ch.style.backgroundPosition=(-Math.round(cr.left-glyphRect.left))+'px 0px';}
          }else{
            ch.style.removeProperty('background-image');ch.style.removeProperty('background-clip');
            ch.style.removeProperty('-webkit-background-clip');ch.style.removeProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color');
            ch.style.removeProperty('background-size');ch.style.removeProperty('background-position');
            ch.style.removeProperty('background-repeat');ch.style.removeProperty('color');
          }
        });
      }
      /* Per-character fill styling changes the glyph's innerHTML signature. Rebuild the twin
         after those writes so MutationObserver cannot later replace our character-level stroke. */
      if(typeof window.ethvelleSyncStrokeLayers==='function')window.ethvelleSyncStrokeLayers();
      const previewStroke=host.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-stroke-clone');
      if(previewGlyph&&previewGlyph.children&&previewGlyph.children.length&&previewStroke){
        const stroke=getComputedStyle(previewStroke).webkitTextStroke;
        if(stroke&&stroke!=='0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'){
          previewStroke.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(function(ch){
            ch.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-stroke',stroke,'important');
            ch.style.setProperty('paint-order','normal','important');
          });
        }
      }
      input.classList.add('ev-v297-mirrored-text');
      input.style.setProperty('color','transparent','important');
      input.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color','transparent','important');
      input.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-stroke','0 transparent','important');
      input.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none','important');
      input.style.setProperty('filter','none','important');
      input.style.setProperty('background','transparent','important');
      input.style.setProperty('text-decoration','none','important');
      input.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');
      /* Keep the editable textarea above the visual mirror so the native caret and selection
         remain visible; the textarea glyphs are transparent, so the styled mirror still paints. */
      input.style.setProperty('z-index','100004','important');
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v297 composer preview:',err);}
    finally{previewBusy=false;}
  }
  window.__ethvelleV297SyncComposerEffectPreview=syncComposerPreview;
  function resync(){const input=$('evtcStageText');if(input){const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;syncComposerPreview(input,t);}}
  function observeInput(){
    const input=$('evtcStageText');if(!input||inputObserved===input)return;
    inputObserved=input;
    input.addEventListener('input',resync,{passive:true});
    if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
      try{resizeObserver=new ResizeObserver(resync);resizeObserver.observe(input);}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function wrapAfter(name,after){
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evV297After)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=original.apply(this,arguments);try{after.apply(this,arguments);}catch(_e){}return result;};
    wrapped.__evV297After=true;window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  }
  const repaint=()=>{
    observeInput();
    const input=$('evtcStageText');if(!input)return;
    const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
    syncComposerPreview(input,t);
  };
  wrapAfter('openEthvelleTextComposer',function(){
    observeInput();
    if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartRepaintText==='function')window.ethvellePicsartRepaintText();
    repaint();
  });
  wrapAfter('updateEthvelleComposerText',repaint);
  wrapAfter('setEthvelleComposerColor',repaint);
  wrapAfter('setEthvelleComposerOpacity',repaint);
  wrapAfter('ethvelleComposerToggleBold',repaint);
  wrapAfter('ethvellePicsartRepaintText',repaint);
  window.addEventListener('resize',repaint,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport){window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',repaint,{passive:true});window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',repaint,{passive:true});}
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',observeInput,{once:true});
  observeInput();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v299-character-clip-fx">
/* v299: per-clip Adjust (colour) + Clip animations (in / out / overall) for character animation clips.
   The settings live on the clip object itself (clip.adjustments / clip.clipAnimations), exactly like
   image and video clips, and are applied around each character when it is painted  -  on the live
   stage, in frame capture and in exported video (export sets window.__evCharFxTime per frame). */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  function clipsOf(c){
    try{
      if(typeof activeCharacter==='function' && c===activeCharacter()) return getAllStickmanClips(activeClip, splitStickmanSegments);
      return getAllStickmanClips(c.clip, c.segments||[]);
    }catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  function clipAt(c,t){
    const list=clipsOf(c);
    for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){const cl=list[i].clip;if(cl&&t>=cl.trimStart&&t<cl.trimEnd)return cl;}
    return null;
  }
  function nowT(){
    const o=window.__evCharFxTime;
    if(typeof o==='number'&&isFinite(o))return o;
    return (typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'&&isFinite(currentAbsoluteTimeSec))?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0;
  }
  function fxFor(char){
    if(!char||!char.bones)return null;
    const cl=clipAt(char,nowT());
    if(!cl)return null;
    let filter='none';
    if(cl.adjustments&&typeof window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter==='function'){try{filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(cl.adjustments,0)||'none';}catch(_e){}}
    let a=null;
    if(cl.clipAnimations&&typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'){
      try{a=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(cl,nowT());}catch(_e){}
      if(a&&a.opacity===1&&a.scale===1&&!a.x&&!a.y&&!a.rotation)a=null;
    }
    if(filter==='none'&&!a)return null;
    return {filter,anim:a};
  }
  // Splitting a clip: both halves keep the colour adjustments; the In animation stays on the first
  // half and the Out animation moves to the second half.
  window.__evSplitCarryFx=function(first,second){
    try{
      if(first.adjustments)second.adjustments=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(first.adjustments));
      if(first.adjustmentsPreset)second.adjustmentsPreset=first.adjustmentsPreset;
      if(first.clipAnimations){
        const ca=first.clipAnimations;
        second.clipAnimations={...ca,in:'none'};
        first.clipAnimations={...ca,out:'none'};
      }
    }catch(_e){}
  };
  const orig=window.drawCharacterToContext;
  if(typeof orig==='function'&&!orig.__ev299Fx){
    const wrapped=function(targetCtx,char){
      let fx=null;
      try{fx=fxFor(char);}catch(_e){fx=null;}
      if(!fx||!targetCtx)return orig.apply(this,arguments);
      targetCtx.save();
      try{
        if(fx.filter!=='none')targetCtx.filter=fx.filter;
        const a=fx.anim;
        if(a){
          const hip=char.bones&&char.bones.hip,mt=char.modelTransform||{};
          const px=(hip?hip.x:0)+(Number(mt.offsetX)||0),py=(hip?hip.y:0)+(Number(mt.offsetY)||0);
          targetCtx.globalAlpha*=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,a.opacity));
          targetCtx.translate(px+a.x,py+a.y);
          targetCtx.rotate((a.rotation||0)*Math.PI/180);
          targetCtx.scale(a.scale,a.scale);
          targetCtx.translate(-px,-py);
        }
        return orig.apply(this,arguments);
      }finally{targetCtx.restore();}
    };
    wrapped.__ev299Fx=true;
    window.drawCharacterToContext=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v300-monthly-credits-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV300MonthlyCredits)return;
  window.__ethvelleV300MonthlyCredits=true;
  const DAY=86400000,WINDOW_MS=30*DAY,MAX_TIMER=2147483000;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let expiryTimer=0;

  function creditTime(value){
    try{
      if(typeof creditTimestampMs==='function')return Number(creditTimestampMs(value))||0;
      if(value&&typeof value.toMillis==='function')return Number(value.toMillis())||0;
      if(typeof value==='number')return value;
      const n=new Date(value||0).getTime();return Number.isFinite(n)?n:0;
    }catch(_e){return 0;}
  }
  function monthlyTransactions(){
    const now=Date.now(),cutoff=now-WINDOW_MS;
    return (Array.isArray(myCreditTransactions)?myCreditTransactions:[]).filter(t=>{
      const stamp=creditTime(t&&t.createdAt);
      return stamp>=cutoff&&stamp<=now+5*60*1000;
    });
  }
  function decorateHistoryRows(rows){
    rows.forEach((row,index)=>{
      const side=row.querySelector('.myplan-credit-row-side');
      if(!side)return;
      let actions=side.querySelector('.myplan-credit-actions');
      if(!actions){
        actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='myplan-credit-actions';
        const del=side.querySelector('.myplan-credit-delete');
        if(del)actions.appendChild(del);
        side.appendChild(actions);
      }
      row.dataset.evCreditRow=String(index);
    });
  }

  function scheduleHistoryExpiry(){
    if(expiryTimer)clearTimeout(expiryTimer);
    expiryTimer=0;
    const now=Date.now();
    let next=Infinity;
    (Array.isArray(myCreditTransactions)?myCreditTransactions:[]).forEach(t=>{
      const stamp=creditTime(t&&t.createdAt);if(!stamp)return;
      const expires=stamp+WINDOW_MS+20;
      if(expires>now&&expires<next)next=expires;
    });
    if(!Number.isFinite(next))return;
    const delay=Math.max(1000,Math.min(MAX_TIMER,next-now));
    expiryTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      const screen=$('screen-credits-history');
      if(screen&&screen.classList.contains('active'))window.renderCreditsHistoryScreen();
      else scheduleHistoryExpiry();
    },delay);
  }

  const baseHistoryRenderer=window.renderCreditsHistoryScreen;
  if(typeof baseHistoryRenderer==='function'&&!baseHistoryRenderer.__evv300){
    const wrappedHistoryRenderer=function(){
      const allTransactions=myCreditTransactions;
      const inWindow=monthlyTransactions();
      myCreditTransactions=inWindow;
      let result;
      try{result=baseHistoryRenderer.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{myCreditTransactions=allTransactions;}
      const scroll=$('creditsHistoryScroll');
      const heading=document.querySelector('#screen-credits-history .sub-topbar h1');
      if(heading)heading.textContent='Credits history of this month';
      if(scroll){
        const section=scroll.querySelector('.myplan-credit-history-card');
        if(section){
          section.setAttribute('aria-label','Credits history of this month');
          const list=section.querySelector('.myplan-credit-history');
          if(list&&!inWindow.length&&currentUser){
            const empty=list.querySelector('.myplan-credit-empty');
            if(empty)empty.textContent='No credit activity in the last 30 days.';
          }
          const rows=list?Array.from(list.querySelectorAll('.myplan-credit-row')):[];
          const visibleWindow=inWindow.filter(t=>!myHiddenCreditTransactionIds.includes(String(t&&t.id)));
          rows.forEach((row,index)=>{row.dataset.evCreditId=String(visibleWindow[index]&&visibleWindow[index].id||'');});
          decorateHistoryRows(rows);
        }
      }
      scheduleHistoryExpiry();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedHistoryRenderer.__evv300=true;
    window.renderCreditsHistoryScreen=wrappedHistoryRenderer;
    try{renderCreditsHistoryScreen=wrappedHistoryRenderer;}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* Plan progress indicators use the same billing state/credit pool as the original renderer. */
  function remainingTime(end){
    let d=end-Date.now();if(d<=0)return 'Expired';
    const days=Math.floor(d/DAY);d%=DAY;const hours=Math.floor(d/3600000);d%=3600000;
    const minutes=Math.floor(d/60000);const seconds=Math.floor((d%60000)/1000);
    return (days?days+'d ':'')+String(hours).padStart(2,'0')+'h '+String(minutes).padStart(2,'0')+'m '+String(seconds).padStart(2,'0')+'s';
  }
  const clamp=(n,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,n));
  window.evActivePlansHtml=function(owned){
    const box=message=>'<div class="ev-plan-card"><div class="ev-plan-empty-meta">'+message+'</div></div>';
    if(!currentUser)return box('Sign in to see your active plans.');
    const paid=(owned||[]).filter(p=>Number(p.price)>0&&!['free','p_free'].includes(String(p.id)));
    if(!paid.length)return box('No active paid plan right now.');
    let pool=Math.max(0,Number(myCreditsBalance)||0);
    const now=Date.now();
    return paid.map(p=>{
      const state=(myPlanOrderStates&&myPlanOrderStates[String(p.id)])||{};
      const grant=Number(typeof getPlanCreditGrant==='function'?getPlanCreditGrant(p):0)||Number(state.creditsGrantAmount)||0;
      const startFromState=creditTime(state.approvedAt)||creditTime(state.creditsGrantedAt)||creditTime(state.createdAt);
      const duration=Math.max(1,Number(p.durationDays)||30)*DAY;
      const exp=creditTime(state.expiresAt)||(startFromState?startFromState+duration:0);
      const start=startFromState||(exp?exp-duration:0);
      const rem=grant>0?Math.min(pool,grant):pool;pool-=rem;
      const creditPct=grant>0?clamp(Math.round(rem/grant*100),0,100):(rem>0?100:0);
      const timelinePct=exp&&start&&exp>start?clamp(Math.round((exp-now)/(exp-start)*100),0,100):(exp?(exp>now?100:0):100);
      const timelineCountdown=exp?'<span class="ev-plan-timer ev-plan-timeline-countdown" data-ev-exp="'+exp+'">'+remainingTime(exp)+'</span>':'<span class="ev-plan-timeline-countdown">No expiry</span>';
      const creditValue=rem.toLocaleString()+(grant>0?' / '+grant.toLocaleString():'');
      return '<div class="ev-plan-card">'
        +'<div class="ev-plan-top"><span class="ev-plan-name">'+escapeHtml(p.name||'Plan')+'</span></div>'
        +'<div class="ev-plan-timeline">'
          +'<div class="ev-plan-meter-head"><span>Timeline</span>'+timelineCountdown+'</div>'
          +'<div class="ev-plan-timebar"><i class="ev-plan-timeline-fill" data-ev-start="'+start+'" data-ev-end="'+exp+'" style="width:'+timelinePct+'%"></i></div>'
        +'</div>'
        +'<div class="ev-plan-credit-block">'
          +'<div class="ev-plan-credit-meta"><span>Remaining credits</span><b>'+creditValue+'</b></div>'
          +'<div class="ev-plan-credit-bar"><i style="width:'+creditPct+'%"></i></div>'
        +'</div>'
      +'</div>';
    }).join('');
  };
  if(!window.__evv300PlanProgressTimer){
    window.__evv300PlanProgressTimer=setInterval(()=>{
      const now=Date.now();
      document.querySelectorAll('.ev-plan-timeline-fill[data-ev-end]').forEach(fill=>{
        const start=Number(fill.dataset.evStart)||0,end=Number(fill.dataset.evEnd)||0;
        if(!end)return;
        const pct=start&&end>start?clamp((end-now)/(end-start)*100,0,100):(end>now?100:0);
        fill.style.width=pct.toFixed(2)+'%';
        const value=fill.closest('.ev-plan-timeline')?.querySelector('.ev-plan-timeline-value');
        if(value)value.textContent=Math.round(pct)+'% left';
      });
    },60000);
  }

  /* Move Adjust beside Animations only for character animation clips; restore its normal spot otherwise. */
  function isCharacterAnimationClip(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':(selectedClip||''));
    return id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id);
  }
  let toolbarRaf=0;
  function scheduleCharacterAdjustPlacement(){
    if(toolbarRaf)return;
    const run=()=>{toolbarRaf=0;placeCharacterAdjustTool();};
    toolbarRaf=window.requestAnimationFrame?requestAnimationFrame(run):setTimeout(run,0);
  }
  function placeCharacterAdjustTool(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip'),anim=$('clipAnimTool'),adjust=$('clipAdjustTool');
    if(!row||!adjust||!adjust.isConnected)return;
    if(isCharacterAnimationClip()&&anim&&anim.parentElement===row){
      if(anim.nextElementSibling!==adjust)row.insertBefore(adjust,anim.nextElementSibling);
    }else{
      const anchor=$('clipEnhanceTool')||$('clipForwardTool')||$('clipBackwardTool')||$('clipDuplicateTool');
      if(anchor&&anchor!==adjust&&adjust.nextElementSibling!==anchor)row.insertBefore(adjust,anchor);
    }
  }
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__evv300AdjustOrder){
    const syncWrapped=function(){const out=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);scheduleCharacterAdjustPlacement();return out;};
    syncWrapped.__evv300AdjustOrder=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=syncWrapped;
    try{syncBottomToolsMode=syncWrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const toolbar=$('bottomToolsClip');
  if(toolbar&&window.MutationObserver){
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>scheduleCharacterAdjustPlacement());
    observer.observe(toolbar,{childList:true,subtree:false,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style']});
  }
  scheduleCharacterAdjustPlacement();
  setTimeout(scheduleCharacterAdjustPlacement,350);
})();
</script>
