<!-- v304: (1) blue text caret that always sits beside the rendered text, (2) "Retype" action in the text-editing bottom toolbar -->
<style id="ethvelle-v304-caret-retype-css">
  /* The writing caret ("scrubber") is blue. While the styled preview is showing, the caret is drawn
     by #evtcBlueCaret from the preview's real glyph positions and the native one is hidden. */
  #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcStageText{caret-color:#2F7BFF!important;}
  #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcStageText.ev-v304-own-caret{caret-color:transparent!important;}
  #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcBlueCaret{
    position:fixed;z-index:100006;display:none;box-sizing:border-box;width:2px;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;
    border-radius:1px;background:#2F7BFF;pointer-events:none;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none;
    animation:evtcBlueCaretBlink 1.1s steps(1,end) infinite;
  }
  @keyframes evtcBlueCaretBlink{0%,55%{opacity:1}56%,100%{opacity:0}}

  /* Retype: first entry of the text-editing bottom toolbar (hidden inside the composer itself). */
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab{position:relative;margin-right:7px;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab{color:#A4A7B7;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab svg{stroke:currentColor;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab::after{
    content:"";position:absolute;right:-5px;top:13px;bottom:13px;width:1px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.14);pointer-events:none;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab:active{background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);color:#DDE6FF;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-in-composer .evpa-tab.evpa-retype-tab{display:none!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v304-caret-retype-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let caretEl=null,rafId=0,lastKey='',moInput=null,moHost=null,moHostEl=null,globalsWired=false;

  const composerOn=()=>{const r=$('ethvelleTextComposer');return !!(r&&r.classList.contains('active'));};

  function ensureCaretEl(input){
    if(!caretEl){caretEl=document.createElement('div');caretEl.id='evtcBlueCaret';caretEl.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
    if(caretEl.parentNode!==input.parentNode)input.parentNode.appendChild(caretEl);
    return caretEl;
  }
  function hideCaret(input){
    if(caretEl&&caretEl.style.display!=='none')caretEl.style.display='none';
    if(input&&input.classList.contains('ev-v304-own-caret'))input.classList.remove('ev-v304-own-caret');
  }

  /* 1) The textarea only supplies typing, selection and the on-screen keyboard; the styled preview paints the
        text. Give the textarea the preview's own letter-spacing / line-height / text-transform so both lay the
        text out identically (it used to keep its CSS defaults, so any Spacing / line-height change, or even
        just a big font, pushed the caret away from the text). */
  function syncTypography(input,glyph,host){
    const ls=glyph.style.letterSpacing;
    const want={
      'letter-spacing':(ls&&ls!=='normal')?ls:'0px',
      'line-height':glyph.style.lineHeight||'1.16',
      'text-transform':(host&&host.style.textTransform)||'none'
    };
    for(const k in want){
      if(input.style.getPropertyValue(k)!==want[k]||input.style.getPropertyPriority(k)!=='important')input.style.setProperty(k,want[k],'important');
    }
  }

  /* 2) Caret position measured from the preview's real glyphs, so it stays beside the visible text whatever
        font, size, stroke, spacing, bend or perspective is applied. Returns null when it cannot be measured
        (then the native caret, also blue, is used). */
  /* v305: the outline (stroke), its 3D extrusion and the font size make the visible text wider than its glyph box,
        so a caret placed exactly on the glyph edge touched / hid under the outline. Measure how far the outline
        reaches on screen and keep the caret a small, size-based distance outside of it. */
  function edgePad(host,glyph){
    const gs=getComputedStyle(glyph),fs=parseFloat(gs.fontSize)||40;
    let k=1;                                                 // on-screen scale of the preview (zoom / transform)
    try{if(glyph.offsetWidth>0){const bw=glyph.getBoundingClientRect().width/glyph.offsetWidth;if(isFinite(bw)&&bw>0.05)k=bw;}}catch(_e){}
    let out=0;
    const clone=host.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-stroke-clone');
    if(clone){
      const cs=getComputedStyle(clone);
      const w=(parseFloat(cs.webkitTextStrokeWidth)||0)/2;   // the stroke is painted at 2x width, half of it outside the glyph
      const vis=parseFloat(cs.opacity);
      if(w>0.01&&!(vis===0)){
        out=w;
        if(typeof window.ethvelleStrokeExtrude==='function'&&cs.filter&&cs.filter!=='none'){
          try{out+=window.ethvelleStrokeExtrude(w,1,cs.webkitTextStrokeColor).depth*0.32;}catch(_e){}
        }
      }
    }
    const gap=Math.max(3,Math.min(12,fs*0.07));            // breathing room that grows with the font size
    return (out+gap)*k;
  }

  function caretFromGlyph(input,glyph){
    const val=input.value,idx=input.selectionStart;
    if(!val||idx==null)return null;
    const segs=[];let total=0;
    const walker=document.createTreeWalker(glyph,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    for(let n=walker.nextNode();n;n=walker.nextNode()){if(n.data.length){segs.push({n:n,s:total});total+=n.data.length;}}
    if(total!==val.length)return null;                      // preview has not caught up with the text yet
    const rectOf=(a,b)=>{
      for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++){
        const sg=segs[i];
        if(a>=sg.s&&a<sg.s+sg.n.data.length){
          const rg=document.createRange();
          rg.setStart(sg.n,a-sg.s);rg.setEnd(sg.n,Math.min(b,sg.s+sg.n.data.length)-sg.s);
          const rs=rg.getClientRects();
          return rs.length?rs[rs.length-1]:null;
        }
      }
      return null;
    };
    const pack=(x,top,h,e)=>(isFinite(x)&&isFinite(top)&&isFinite(h)&&h>0)?{x:x,top:top,h:h,e:e||0}:null;   // e: +1 = after the last glyph of a line, -1 = before the first glyph, 0 = between glyphs
    const gs=getComputedStyle(glyph),rtl=gs.direction==='rtl';
    const fs=parseFloat(gs.fontSize)||40;
    const lhPx=parseFloat(gs.lineHeight)||fs*1.16;
    let r;
    if(idx>0){
      if(val.charAt(idx-1)==='\n'){                         // caret sits at the start of a new line
        if(idx<val.length&&val.charAt(idx)!=='\n'){
          r=rectOf(idx,idx+1);
          return r?pack(rtl?r.right:r.left,r.top,r.height,-1):null;
        }
        let k=idx-1,nl=0;while(k>=0&&val.charAt(k)==='\n'){nl++;k--;}
        if(k<0)return null;
        r=rectOf(k,k+1);if(!r)return null;                  // empty line: same column as the line above, one line lower
        const gr=glyph.getBoundingClientRect(),al=getComputedStyle(input).textAlign;
        const l=gr.left+(parseFloat(gs.paddingLeft)||0),rr=gr.right-(parseFloat(gs.paddingRight)||0);
        const x=al==='center'?(l+rr)/2:(al==='right'?rr:l);
        return pack(x,r.top+nl*lhPx,r.height);
      }
      let a=idx-1;
      const lo=val.charCodeAt(idx-1);
      if(lo>=0xDC00&&lo<=0xDFFF&&idx>=2){const hi=val.charCodeAt(idx-2);if(hi>=0xD800&&hi<=0xDBFF)a=idx-2;}
      r=rectOf(a,idx);
      return r?pack(rtl?r.left:r.right,r.top,r.height,(idx>=val.length||val.charAt(idx)==='\n')?1:0):null;
    }
    r=rectOf(0,1);                                          // caret before the first character
    return r?pack(rtl?r.right:r.left,r.top,r.height,-1):null;
  }

  function update(){
    rafId=0;
    const input=$('evtcStageText');
    if(!input)return;
    if(!composerOn()){hideCaret(input);return;}
    const host=$('evtcStagePreview');
    const glyph=(host&&host.style.display!=='none')?host.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer'):null;
    let pos=null,pad=0,rtlDir=1;
    if(glyph){
      syncTypography(input,glyph,host);
      if(document.activeElement===input&&input.selectionStart===input.selectionEnd){
        try{pos=caretFromGlyph(input,glyph);if(pos&&pos.e){pad=edgePad(host,glyph);rtlDir=getComputedStyle(glyph).direction==='rtl'?-1:1;}}catch(_e){pos=null;}
      }
    }
    if(!pos){hideCaret(input);return;}
    const c=ensureCaretEl(input),h=Math.max(8,pos.h);
    pos.x+=pos.e*rtlDir*pad;
    c.style.left=(pos.x-1)+'px';c.style.top=pos.top+'px';c.style.height=h+'px';
    if(c.style.display!=='block')c.style.display='block';
    const key=Math.round(pos.x*10)+':'+Math.round(pos.top*10)+':'+Math.round(h*10);
    if(key!==lastKey){lastKey=key;c.style.animation='none';void c.offsetWidth;c.style.animation='';}   // solid right after it moves
    if(!input.classList.contains('ev-v304-own-caret'))input.classList.add('ev-v304-own-caret');
  }
  function schedule(){if(!rafId)rafId=requestAnimationFrame(update);}

  function wire(){
    const input=$('evtcStageText');
    if(input&&!input.__ev304){
      input.__ev304=true;
      ['input','keydown','keyup','click','focus','blur','select','pointerup','touchend','compositionupdate','compositionend'].forEach(ev=>input.addEventListener(ev,schedule,{passive:true}));
      if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){moInput=new MutationObserver(schedule);moInput.observe(input,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style','class']});}
    }
    const host=$('evtcStagePreview');
    if(host&&host!==moHostEl&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
      moHostEl=host;if(moHost)moHost.disconnect();
      moHost=new MutationObserver(schedule);moHost.observe(host,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,characterData:true});
    }
    if(!globalsWired){
      globalsWired=true;
      document.addEventListener('selectionchange',schedule,{passive:true});
      window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
      if(window.visualViewport){window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',schedule,{passive:true});}
      if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.addEventListener)document.fonts.addEventListener('loadingdone',schedule);
      /* Safety net: re-measure a few times a second while the writing screen is open. */
      setInterval(function(){if(composerOn()){wire();schedule();}},160);
    }
  }

  /* ---- Retype: reopen the writing screen for the selected text layer from the text-editing bottom toolbar ---- */
  const RETYPE_ICON='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L8 18l-4 1 1-4Z"/></svg>';
  function selectedTextId(){
    let id=null;
    try{id=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:null;}catch(_e){}
    if(id==null){try{const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;if(t&&t.id!=null)id=t.id;}catch(_e){}}
    return id;
  }
  function onRetype(e){
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    const id=selectedTextId();
    if(id==null||typeof window.openEthvelleTextComposer!=='function'){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Select a text layer first');return;}
    window.openEthvelleTextComposer(id);
    wire();
    /* Ready to type straight away, caret after the last character. */
    setTimeout(function(){
      const input=$('evtcStageText');
      if(!input||!composerOn())return;
      try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});const n=input.value.length;input.setSelectionRange(n,n);}catch(_e){}
      schedule();
    },120);
  }
  function ensureRetypeTab(){
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');if(!tabs)return;
    let b=tabs.querySelector('.evpa-retype-tab');
    if(!b){
      b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='evpa-tab evpa-retype-tab';
      b.setAttribute('aria-label','Retype text');b.title='Retype text';
      b.innerHTML=RETYPE_ICON+'<span>Retype</span>';
      b.addEventListener('click',onRetype);
    }
    if(tabs.firstElementChild!==b)tabs.insertBefore(b,tabs.firstChild);
  }

  function wrapAfter(name,fn){
    const orig=window[name];
    if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__ev304)return;
    const w=function(){const r=orig.apply(this,arguments);try{fn();}catch(_e){}return r;};
    w.__ev304=true;window[name]=w;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  }
  wrapAfter('openEthvelleTextComposer',function(){wire();schedule();});
  wrapAfter('closeEthvelleTextComposer',function(){schedule();});
  wrapAfter('openEthvelleTextStylePanel',ensureRetypeTab);
  wrapAfter('ethvellePicsartSetTool',ensureRetypeTab);

  ensureRetypeTab();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){ensureRetypeTab();wire();},{once:true});
  window.addEventListener('load',function(){ensureRetypeTab();},{once:true});
  setTimeout(ensureRetypeTab,1500);
  wire();
})();
</script>


<style id="ethvelle-v330-face-hands-css">
  #screen-editor .evpa-choice-preview canvas.evpa-compose{max-width:100%;max-height:46px;object-fit:contain;display:block}
  #screen-editor .evpa-wings-section .evpa-choice-add,#screen-editor .evpa-wings-section .evpa-section-add{display:none!important}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v330-face-hands-current-structure">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* v330: Face & hands panel previews for the CURRENT humanoid (14+19 part) and bird (7+7 part) sheets.
     Expression / mouth tiles are composited from the model's own head, eye, brow, beak and inner-mouth
     parts, using the same nesting transforms the stage renderer uses, so each tile shows what you get. */
  const back=()=>document.getElementById('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
  const curChar=()=>{try{return typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  function parseSpec(str){const o={};String(str||'').split('|').forEach(pair=>{const i=pair.indexOf(':');if(i>0)o[pair.slice(0,i)]=pair.slice(i+1);});return o;}
  function composeFace(c,spec){
    const sh=c&&c.sheet;if(!sh||!sh.parts||!sh.draw||typeof sheetImgFor!=='function'||typeof sheetNestInfo!=='function')return {canvas:null,pending:[]};
    const P=sh.parts,D=sh.draw,type=String(c.modelType||sh.modelType||'humanoid'),bird=type==='bird';
    const emo=spec.emo&&spec.emo!=='none'?spec.emo:null;
    const headKey=(!bird&&emo&&P['head_'+emo])?'head_'+emo:'head';
    const head=P[headKey],hd=D[headKey]||D.head,baseHd=D.head||hd;
    if(!head||!head.src||!hd||!baseHd)return {canvas:null,pending:[]};
    const pending=[];
    const imgOf=src=>{const im=sheetImgFor(src);if(!(im.complete&&im.naturalWidth)){pending.push(im);return null;}return im;};
    const headImg=imgOf(head.src);if(!headImg)return {canvas:null,pending};
    const num=(v,d)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
    const hsx=num(hd.sx,1),hsy=num(hd.sy,1),hpx=num(hd.pvx,0),hpy=num(hd.pvy,0);
    const xa=-hpx*hsx,xb=(headImg.naturalWidth-hpx)*hsx,ya=-hpy*hsy,yb=(headImg.naturalHeight-hpy)*hsy;
    const x0=Math.min(xa,xb),x1=Math.max(xa,xb),y0=Math.min(ya,yb),y1=Math.max(ya,yb);
    const CW=128,CH=96,pad=6,sc=Math.min((CW-pad*2)/Math.max(1,x1-x0),(CH-pad*2)/Math.max(1,y1-y0));
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=CW;cv.height=CH;cv.className='evpa-compose';
    const g=cv.getContext('2d');if(!g)return {canvas:null,pending:[]};
    g.save();g.translate(CW/2-((x0+x1)/2)*sc,CH/2-((y0+y1)/2)*sc);g.scale(sc,sc);
    const bsx=num(baseHd.sx,1),bsy=num(baseHd.sy,1);
    /* inner mouth sits behind an expression head (humanoid kit) */
    if(!bird&&P.mouth_inner&&headKey!=='head'&&typeof sheetAnimalMouthInnerLayer==='function'){
      try{const L=sheetAnimalMouthInnerLayer(sh);if(L&&L.canvas){g.save();g.scale(bsx,bsy);g.drawImage(L.canvas,-L.pivotX,-L.pivotY,P.head.w,P.head.h);g.restore();}}catch(_e){}
    }
    g.save();g.scale(hsx,hsy);g.drawImage(headImg,-hpx,-hpy);g.restore();
    const nested=(key,moodRot)=>{
      const part=P[key];if(!part||!part.src)return;
      let nest=sheetNestInfo(sh,key);if(!nest)return;
      if(key==='beak_open'){const cn=sheetNestInfo(sh,'beak_closed');if(cn)nest={...nest,nestDx:cn.nestDx,nestDy:cn.nestDy,nestRot:cn.nestRot};}
      const im=imgOf(part.src);if(!im)return;
      const pvx=num(nest.nestPivotX,num((D[key]||{}).pvx,num(part.dpx,0))),pvy=num(nest.nestPivotY,num((D[key]||{}).pvy,num(part.dpy,0)));
      g.save();g.translate(num(nest.nestDx,0)*bsx,num(nest.nestDy,0)*bsy);g.rotate(num(nest.nestRot,0)+(moodRot||0));
      g.scale(num(nest.nestSx,1),num(nest.nestSy,1));g.drawImage(im,-pvx,-pvy,part.w,part.h);g.restore();
    };
    if(bird){
      const e=emo||'';const r=e==='angry'?.30:e==='sad'?-.24:e==='laugh'?.13:e==='shock'?-.10:0;
      nested(spec.eyes==='closed'&&P.bird_eye_closed?'bird_eye_closed':'bird_eye');
      nested('bird_brow',r);
      nested(spec.mouth==='open'&&P.beak_open?'beak_open':'beak_closed');
    }else{
      const e=emo||'';
      const rot=left=>e==='angry'?(left?1:-1)*.18:e==='sad'?(left?-1:1)*.11:e==='laugh'?(left?1:-1)*.07:e==='shock'?(left?-1:1)*.04:0;
      if(spec.eyes==='closed'&&P.eye_closed_L&&P.eye_closed_R){nested('eye_closed_L');nested('eye_closed_R');}
      else{nested('eye_L');nested('eye_R');}
      nested('brow_L',rot(true));nested('brow_R',rot(false));
    }
    g.restore();
    if(pending.length)return {canvas:null,pending};
    return {canvas:cv,pending:[]};
  }
  let repaintQueued=false;
  function paintPreviews(){
    const root=back();if(!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const c=curChar();if(!c||!c.sheet)return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.evpa-choice[data-compose]').forEach(btn=>{
      const holder=btn.querySelector('.evpa-choice-preview');if(!holder||holder.querySelector('canvas.evpa-compose'))return;
      let res;try{res=composeFace(c,parseSpec(btn.dataset.compose));}catch(err){console.warn('Face preview skipped',err);return;}
      if(res.canvas){holder.innerHTML='';holder.appendChild(res.canvas);}
      else if(res.pending.length&&!repaintQueued){
        repaintQueued=true;let left=res.pending.length;
        const done=()=>{if(--left<=0){repaintQueued=false;requestAnimationFrame(paintPreviews);}};
        res.pending.forEach(im=>{im.addEventListener('load',done,{once:true});im.addEventListener('error',done,{once:true});});
      }
    });
  }
  const prior=window.openClipCharacterActions;
  if(typeof prior==='function'&&!prior.__ethvelleV330){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);
      paintPreviews();requestAnimationFrame(()=>{paintPreviews();requestAnimationFrame(paintPreviews);});
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV330=true;window.openClipCharacterActions=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v334-character-effect-library">
/* Ethvelle character effects library (v334).
   Every effect is drawn procedurally from time, in "head units": 1 unit = the character's head
   radius, origin = head centre, up = -y. The same function feeds the live canvas, the exported
   video and the little frame-by-frame thumbnails shown inside the timeline effect clip. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.ethvelleCharFxLib)return;
  const TAU=Math.PI*2;
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const fr=x=>x-Math.floor(x);
  const hs=i=>fr(Math.sin((i+1)*127.1+37.7)*43758.5453);
  const ease=t=>{t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t);};
  const lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
  const rgba=(r,g,b,a)=>'rgba('+r+','+g+','+b+','+clamp(a,0,1)+')';
  const F0={fw:1,ex:.4,cx:.52,ey:.08,ny:.34,my:.5,sideX:.92};

  const TYPES=[
    ['anger','Anger'],['fire','Fire'],['tears','Tears'],['chi','Chi Ball'],
    ['blood','Blood'],['bandage','Bandage'],['bruise','Black Eye'],['nosebleed','Nosebleed'],
    ['sweat','Sweat'],['dizzy','Dizzy Stars'],['hearts','Hearts'],['sleep','Sleeping'],
    ['electric','Electric Shock'],['sparkle','Sparkles']
  ];

  /* ---------- small shape helpers (all in head units) ---------- */
  function dropPath(ctx,x,y,s){
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-s*1.7);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-s*.18,y-s*.75,x-s,y-s*.15,x-s,y+s*.35);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-s,y+s*1.0,x+s,y+s*1.0,x+s,y+s*.35);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+s,y-s*.15,x+s*.18,y-s*.75,x,y-s*1.7);ctx.closePath();
  }
  function heartPath(ctx,x,y,s){
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y+s*.95);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-s*1.7,y-s*.2,x-s*.95,y-s*1.3,x,y-s*.48);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+s*.95,y-s*1.3,x+s*1.7,y-s*.2,x,y+s*.95);ctx.closePath();
  }
  function starPath(ctx,x,y,s,rot,points,inner){
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<points*2;i++){const a=rot-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/points,r=i%2?s*inner:s;ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*r,y+Math.sin(a)*r);}
    ctx.closePath();
  }
  function softDisc(ctx,x,y,r,rgb,a){
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);
    const strength=clamp(a*1.6,0,1);g.addColorStop(0,rgba(rgb[0],rgb[1],rgb[2],strength));g.addColorStop(1,rgba(rgb[0],rgb[1],rgb[2],0));
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  }
  function bloodDrip(ctx,H,x0,y0,len,t,seed,period,w){
    /* A streak that grows downward, swells into a bulb, lets the bulb fall, then starts again. */
    const p=fr(t/period+hs(seed)),grow=ease(p/.72),L=len*(.25+.75*grow);
    const fade=p>.9?1-(p-.9)/.1:1;
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=fade;ctx.lineCap='round';
    const wob=Math.sin(t*2.1+seed)*.012;
    ctx.strokeStyle='#7E0C14';ctx.lineWidth=w*1.45;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0,y0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x0+wob,y0+L*.5,x0+wob*2,y0+L);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#D2202B';ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0,y0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x0+wob,y0+L*.5,x0+wob*2,y0+L);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,185,185,.8)';ctx.lineWidth=w*.22;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0-w*.22,y0+L*.08);ctx.lineTo(x0-w*.22+wob,y0+L*.72);ctx.stroke();
    if(p<.8){
      const bulb=w*(.62+.5*ease((p-.3)/.5));
      ctx.fillStyle='#D2202B';ctx.strokeStyle='#7E0C14';ctx.lineWidth=w*.3;
      dropPath(ctx,x0+wob*2,y0+L-bulb*.25,bulb);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    }else{
      const q=(p-.8)/.2,fy=y0+L+q*q*1.1,s=w*.7*(1-.25*q);
      ctx.globalAlpha*=1-q*.85;ctx.fillStyle='#D2202B';ctx.strokeStyle='#7E0C14';ctx.lineWidth=w*.3;
      dropPath(ctx,x0+wob*2,fy,s);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function waterDrop(ctx,x,y,s,alpha){
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y-s*1.7,x,y+s);g.addColorStop(0,'#F4FBFF');g.addColorStop(.48,'#A6DDFF');g.addColorStop(1,'#368FE5');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.strokeStyle='#155B9D';ctx.lineWidth=s*.25;dropPath(ctx,x,y,s);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.9)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-s*.38,y+s*.1,s*.17,s*.34,.25,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ---------- the effects ---------- */
  const FX={};

  FX.anger=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    // flushed cheeks that throb with the pulse of the pop
    const throb=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*7);
    softDisc(ctx,-H.face.cx,.28,.28,[255,52,52],.5+.26*throb);softDisc(ctx,H.face.cx,.28,.28,[255,52,52],.5+.26*throb);
    // steam puffs rising from both temples
    for(let side=-1;side<=1;side+=2)for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
      const p=fr(t*.85+j/3+(side>0?.17:0)),x=side*(.98+.2*p+Math.sin(t*3+j*2+side)*.05),y=-.1-p*1.25,r=.17+p*.3,a=Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.85;
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(244,244,250,a*.9);ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(150,150,175,a*.7);ctx.lineWidth=.035;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.arc(x+side*r*.55,y-r*.35,r*.72,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    }
    // the anime "cross-pop" vein: snaps big, settles, then pulses
    const c=t%.95,pop=c<.12?ease(c/.12)*1.35:c<.3?lerp(1.35,1,ease((c-.12)/.18)):1+.06*Math.sin(t*10);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(.82,-.82);ctx.scale(pop*.5,pop*.5);ctx.rotate(-.12);
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(const pass of [[.27,'#6E0A12'],[.17,'#EE2433']]){
      ctx.strokeStyle=pass[1];ctx.lineWidth=pass[0];
      for(let sx=-1;sx<=1;sx+=2)for(let sy=-1;sy<=1;sy+=2){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sx*.08,sy*.62);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sx*.08,sy*.08,sx*.62,sy*.08);ctx.stroke();}
    }
    ctx.restore();
  };

  function flame(ctx,x,by,w,h,t,seed){
    const sway=Math.sin(t*7+seed*5)*w*.4+Math.sin(t*13+seed*3)*w*.18,hh=h*(.86+.14*Math.sin(t*9+seed*7));
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-w,by);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-w*1.3,by-hh*.32,x-w*.25+sway*.5,by-hh*.62,x+sway,by-hh);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+w*.3+sway*.5,by-hh*.58,x+w*1.3,by-hh*.32,x+w,by);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+w*.6,by+w*.55,x-w*.6,by+w*.55,x-w,by);ctx.closePath();
  }
  FX.fire=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const by=-.55;
    softDisc(ctx,0,-1.05,1.7,[255,120,30],.66);
    const spec=[[-.78,.34,.95],[.78,.34,.95],[-.42,.4,1.45],[.42,.4,1.4],[0,.5,1.95]];
    ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#FF7A1A';ctx.shadowBlur=.35*H.R;
    spec.forEach((s,i)=>{
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,by-s[2],0,by+.2);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFB347');g.addColorStop(.5,'#FF6A1E');g.addColorStop(1,'#D8281A');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;flame(ctx,s[0],by,s[1],s[2],t,i);ctx.fill();
    });
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    spec.forEach((s,i)=>{
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,by-s[2]*.7,0,by+.1);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFE27A');g.addColorStop(1,'#FF9A1F');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;flame(ctx,s[0],by,s[1]*.62,s[2]*.7,t+.13,i+3);ctx.fill();
    });
    spec.forEach((s,i)=>{ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,248,196,.95)';flame(ctx,s[0],by,s[1]*.3,s[2]*.38,t+.29,i+8);ctx.fill();});
    ctx.restore();
    const n=Math.round(9*clamp(k,.4,2));
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.75+hs(i)),x=(hs(i+9)-.5)*1.9+Math.sin(t*3+i)*.12,y=-.7-p*2.1,r=.055*(1-p*.7);
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(255,196+Math.round(40*hs(i+4)),70,1-p);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
  };

  FX.tears=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const ex=s*H.face.ex,ey=H.face.ey;
      // watery eye + flowing cheek stream
      const sh=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*5+s);
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(140,210,255,.72+.2*sh);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(ex,ey,.27,.17,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.85)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex-s*.08,ey-.05,.045,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(65,153,225,.88)';ctx.lineWidth=.1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ex,ey+.12);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(s*.5,.5,s*.46,.98);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([.16,.14]);ctx.lineDashOffset=-t*1.1;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.98)';ctx.lineWidth=.028;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ex-s*.02,ey+.12);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(s*.48,.5,s*.44,.98);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
        const p=fr(t*.8+i/3+(s>0?.19:0)),y=.2+p*p*2.5,x=s*(.44+.08*p),sz=(.06+.06*ease(p/.25))*(1-.2*p),a=1-clamp((p-.72)/.28,0,1);
        waterDrop(ctx,x,y,sz,a);
      }
    }
  };

  FX.chi=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    // drawn at the hand (translation done by the caller)
    const pulse=1+.09*Math.sin(t*8.5),rb=.62*pulse;
    softDisc(ctx,0,0,1.9*pulse,[70,150,255],.8);
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
      const a=i*TAU/12+t*1.4,fl=.55+.45*Math.sin(t*14+i*2.1),r0=rb*.95,r1=rb*(1.5+.6*fl);
      ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(170,220,255,.55*fl);ctx.lineWidth=.05;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r1,Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(-rb*.2,-rb*.2,0,0,0,rb);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFFFFF');g.addColorStop(.35,'#A9DBFF');g.addColorStop(1,'#2E6BFF');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,rb,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(205,236,255,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.06;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,rb*1.35,t*3.2,t*3.2+Math.PI*1.3);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth=.045;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,rb*1.7,-t*2.4,-t*2.4+Math.PI*1.05);ctx.stroke();
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.9+i/7),a=i*2.4+t*2.2,d=lerp(1.9,.7,p);
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(200,235,255,.9*Math.sin(p*Math.PI));ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(Math.cos(a)*d,Math.sin(a)*d,.06*(1.2-p*.5),0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
  };
  FX.chi.atHand=true;

  FX.blood=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    // gash on the forehead + the dark stain around it
    softDisc(ctx,-.34,-.6,.5,[140,10,20],.45);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(-.34,-.6);ctx.rotate(-.35);ctx.lineCap='round';
    ctx.strokeStyle='#5A0710';ctx.lineWidth=.12;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.28,0);ctx.lineTo(.28,.02);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#C4141C';ctx.lineWidth=.06;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.26,0);ctx.lineTo(.26,.02);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,-.52,-.56,.95,t,1,2.1,.075);
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,-.34,-.58,1.35,t,2,2.6,.085);
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,-.16,-.6,.7,t,3,1.8,.065);
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,.3,.62,.5,t,4,2.2,.05);
    // one-off spatter burst when the clip starts
    if(t>=0&&t<.55){
      for(let i=0;i<14;i++){
        const a=-Math.PI*(.15+.7*hs(i)),sp=1.2+1.8*hs(i+5),x=-.34+Math.cos(a)*sp*t,y=-.6+Math.sin(a)*sp*t+3.2*t*t;
        ctx.fillStyle=rgba(190,20,30,1-t/.55);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,.045+.045*hs(i+2),0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  };

  FX.bandage=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const wrap=ease(t/.55);
    ctx.save();
    // head wrap, revealed left to right when the clip starts
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(-1.1,-1,2.2*wrap,2);ctx.clip();
    const band=()=>{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.97,-.74);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-.58,.97,-.74);ctx.lineTo(.97,-.36);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-.2,-.97,-.36);ctx.closePath();};
    band();let g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,-.75,0,-.25);g.addColorStop(0,'#FBF5E8');g.addColorStop(1,'#E2D5BC');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();
    ctx.save();band();ctx.clip();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(140,115,80,.3)';ctx.lineWidth=.022;
    for(let x=-1.5;x<1.6;x+=.13){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,-.9);ctx.lineTo(x+.5,-.1);ctx.stroke();}
    for(let x=-1.5;x<1.6;x+=.13){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+.5,-.9);ctx.lineTo(x,-.1);ctx.stroke();}
    // blood slowly soaking through
    const soak=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*.9);
    softDisc(ctx,.34,-.52,.2+.1*soak,[185,20,30],.85);softDisc(ctx,.34,-.52,.09+.05*soak,[110,8,16],.9);
    ctx.restore();
    band();ctx.strokeStyle='#B9AB92';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    // fluttering tail of the wrap
    if(wrap>.95){
      const fl=Math.sin(t*3.4)*.07;
      ctx.fillStyle='#EFE4CE';ctx.strokeStyle='#B9AB92';ctx.lineWidth=.03;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(.9,-.6);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.15,-.62+fl,1.34,-.46+fl*1.4);ctx.lineTo(1.2,-.34+fl*1.4);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.02,-.46+fl,.9,-.42);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    }
    // adhesive cross plaster on the cheek, pops in with a little overshoot
    const q=clamp((t-.45)/.3,0,1),sc=q<1?ease(q)*1.18:1+.0*Math.sin(t);
    if(q>0){
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(.44,.3);ctx.scale(sc,sc);
      for(const rot of [Math.PI/4,-Math.PI/4]){
        ctx.save();ctx.rotate(rot);
        ctx.fillStyle='#EFC9A2';ctx.strokeStyle='#CFA47A';ctx.lineWidth=.03;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect?ctx.roundRect(-.34,-.1,.68,.2,.04):ctx.rect(-.34,-.1,.68,.2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(190,140,100,.55)';
        for(let x=-.28;x<=.29;x+=.1)for(let y=-.05;y<=.06;y+=.1){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,.012,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.fillStyle='#FCF8EE';ctx.strokeStyle='#D8CDB8';ctx.lineWidth=.025;ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(-.1,-.1,.2,.2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  };

  FX.bruise=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const pulse=.85+.15*Math.sin(t*2.4);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(-H.face.ex,H.face.ey-.02);ctx.scale(1,.8);
    softDisc(ctx,0,0,.62,[170,200,50],.46*pulse);
    softDisc(ctx,0,0,.5,[60,72,190],.72*pulse);
    softDisc(ctx,0,0,.38,[96,28,150],.92*pulse);
    softDisc(ctx,0,.02,.2,[45,12,70],.55*pulse);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.55)';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-.4,.0,.3,Math.PI*1.1,Math.PI*1.4);ctx.stroke();
    // impact ticks that flash at the start of each beat
    const beat=fr(t*.9);
    if(beat<.4){
      const a=1-beat/.4,d=.12+beat*.3;ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(255,205,60,a);ctx.lineWidth=.06;
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const an=-2.5+i*.45;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.78+Math.cos(an)*(.25+d),-.12+Math.sin(an)*(.25+d));ctx.lineTo(-.78+Math.cos(an)*(.45+d),-.12+Math.sin(an)*(.45+d));ctx.stroke();}
    }
  };

  FX.nosebleed=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    softDisc(ctx,0,H.face.ny+.02,.3,[185,12,24],.46);
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,-.07,H.face.ny,.62,t,5,1.9,.06);
    bloodDrip(ctx,H,.07,H.face.ny,.82,t,6,2.3,.065);
    // little dried streak on the lip
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,10,18,.55)';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.12,H.face.ny+.08);ctx.lineTo(.12,H.face.ny+.08);ctx.stroke();
  };

  FX.sweat=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    // sliding drop on the temple
    const p=fr(t*.55),y=-.5+p*1.1,s=.09+.07*ease(p/.25);
    waterDrop(ctx,H.face.sideX,y,s,1-clamp((p-.85)/.15,0,1));
    // drops flung off the head
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const side=i%2?1:-1,q=fr(t*1.05+i*.27),x=side*(.95+q*.95),y=-.55-.9*q+1.9*q*q;
